FILE: README.md
================================================
# BudgetBound â€” Budget-First Travel Planning Platform

BudgetBound is a full-stack travel planning platform built around a **budget-first discovery model**.

Instead of asking users where they want to go first, BudgetBound flips the travel discovery equation:

1. **What is your budget?** (e.g., â‚¹15,000, â‚¹50,000)
2. **Where are you starting from?** (e.g., Bangalore, Delhi, Mumbai â€” with an interactive map picker)
3. **How many days do you have?** (e.g., 3 days, 5 days)
4. **What type of trip do you want?** (Beach, Adventure, Nature, Religious, Romantic, City Exploration, etc.)

Based on these constraints and a comfort slider (0â€“100), the platform scores and ranks destinations, generates a day-by-day itinerary, predicts hidden expenses (taxes, local transit, emergency reserve), compares planned vs. actual spending, and exports offline PDF itineraries.

---

## Key Features

| Module | Route | Description |
|--------|-------|-------------|
| **Dashboard** | `/dashboard` | Trip summaries, budget vs. estimated vs. actual charts, expense category breakdown |
| **Trip Flow** | `/planner` | Budget-first planner â€” set constraints, get the best destination, full cost breakdown, and itinerary |
| **Destination Discovery** | `/reverse-search` | Same planner UI in *discover* mode â€” returns top 5 ranked destinations without committing to a plan |
| **Destination Explorer** | `/explorer`, `/explorer/:id` | Paginated catalog with search, category filters, and detailed destination pages |
| **Budget Optimizer** | `/optimizer/:tripId` | Rules-based cost optimization (hotel downgrade, transit swap, itinerary trim, surplus upgrade) |
| **Expense Tracker** | `/expenses/:tripId` | Log transactions by category, compare against estimates, view difference stats |
| **AI Companion** | `/ai-companion` | Groq-powered travel chat assistant (other AI tabs are planned) |

---

## Comprehensive Feature Breakdown

### 1. User Authentication & Security
* **Secure Signup & Login**: Credentials-based signup and login flows with email validation.
* **JWT-Based Session Tracking**: Stateless authentication using JSON Web Tokens (`HS256`). A React interceptor automatically attaches authorization headers to all outgoing requests.
* **Bcrypt Password Cryptography**: Passwords are securely hashed with bcrypt inside the database using standard salt rounds.
* **Client-Side Auth Guards**: React Router middleware (`ProtectedRoute` and `AnonymousRoute`) keeps unauthenticated users out of private views (planner, dashboard, optimizer, expenses) and automatically redirects authenticated users away from the login/signup screens.
* **Auto-Session Cleanup**: Axios request/response interceptors automatically log the user out and redirect them to the login page on a `401 Unauthorized` status response.

### 2. Budget-First Travel Planner & Discovery (`/planner`)
* **Interactive Map Location Picker**: Visual starting-city selector powered by **Leaflet & React-Leaflet**. Users can search for a starting city or place a pin on the interactive map, which geocodes/reverse-geocodes their selection using the Nominatim OpenStreetMap API.
* **Weighted Scoring Recommendation Engine**: Ranks destinations by checking how well they match constraints using a multi-criteria scoring algorithm:
  * **35% Budget Fit**: Prefers destinations utilizing budget efficiently. Excludes over-budget destinations entirely unless show over-budget is checked (Hard Budget Constraint).
  * **25% Category Match**: Blends main category matching with sub-category affinities.
  * **12% User Rating**: Normalizes destination rating to a 5-star standard.
  * **20% Comfort Match**: Aligns comfort level expectations with the destination profile (Comfort slider has a dynamic and strong influence).
  * **8% Duration Match**: Checks constraints against the destination's recommended duration.
* **Pacing & Comfort Slider**: Adjusts a slider between 0 and 100 to dynamically shift lodging recommendations (Budget, Mid-Range, Luxury), transport styles (Train vs. Flight), activities per day, and cost multipliers.
* **Detailed Cost Estimator**: Calculates costs across multiple categories: base transport, lodging (hotel), meals (food), activity fees, local transport, booking taxes (12%), and emergency reserve buffers (8%).
* **Itinerary Generator**: Constructs day-by-day plans with morning, afternoon, and evening slots. Integrates with the Groq API (e.g. `gemma2-9b-it`, `llama-3.1-8b-instant`) to generate customized itineraries, with an offline static template fallback system.
* **Trip Saving & Database Commit**: Saves the generated trip details, calculated budget allocations, and day-by-day itineraries to the database.

### 3. Dynamic Analytics Dashboard (`/dashboard`)
* **Summary Analytics Cards**: Real-time stats showing Total Trips Planned, Total Planned Budget, Total Spent (Actual Expenses), and Net Savings.
* **Trip Budget Comparison (Recharts Bar Chart)**: Dynamic visualization showing the financial health of the user's top 6 trips by comparing the original starting budget, the estimated planning cost, and the actual logged expenses side-by-side.
* **Expense Categories Breakdown (Recharts Pie Chart)**: Clean color-coded pie chart showing aggregate spending distribution across Transport, Hotel, Food, Activities, Shopping, and Other categories.
* **Recent Trips Feed**: Chronological feed of the 5 most recent planned trips with quick-view statistics.

### 4. Interactive Destination Catalog (`/explorer`)
* **Live Search & Filter**: Real-time keyword filtering of destinations by name, state, or keywords.
* **Advanced Filters**: Quick-select filters based on categories (Beach, Nature, Adventure, Religious, City Exploration, Romantic) and locations.
* **Detailed Explorer Card Profile**: Cards showing ratings, recommend duration, category badges, and budget level tags.
* **Rich Destination Detail Page (`/explorer/:id`)**: Displays high-res visual assets, seasonal planning recommendations, base travel stats, geographic context, and a checklist of popular tourist attractions.

### 5. Rules-Based Budget Optimizer (`/optimizer/:tripId`)
* **Dynamic Target Estimation**: Calculates current trip estimates and compares them with the initial budget to highlight deficits or surpluses.
* **Actionable Cost-Cut Recommendations**:
  * *Lodging Tier Downgrades*: Swaps luxury resorts to mid-range hotels, or mid-range hotels to cozy local homestays/hostels (saving 35-50% on lodging).
  * *Transit Method Swap*: Replaces flights with AC sleeper trains for a budget-friendly transit alternative.
  * *Itinerary Trimming*: Lowers pre-booked activities by 50% to save on tickets and entry fees.
* **Actionable Comfort Upgrades**:
  * *Surplus Upgrades*: Suggests premium room/hotel upgrades if the trip estimated cost is significantly under budget.
* **One-Click Apply**: Previews exact cost differences and applies adjustments directly, updating database allocations in real-time.

### 6. Expense Ledger & Tracker (`/expenses/:tripId`)
* **Transaction Logging CRUD**: Record expenses with exact dates, amounts, categories, and custom notes.
* **Variance Analysis Graphs**: Compares the user's actual spending against the pre-calculated budget allocations for each category. If no expenses exist, displays the "Planned Budget Allocation" chart. If expenses exist, shows a comparison of "Planned vs. Actual Spend".
* **Variance Indicator Badge**: Shows real-time difference figures (under-budget vs. over-budget) to highlight categories causing budget leaks. Shows warning cards and Forecasts.

### 7. Groq AI Travel Companion Chatbot (`/ai-companion`)
* **Interactive AI Interface**: Direct chat window to ask travel questions, local guidelines, packing tips, or regional cuisines.
* **Groq Llama/Gemma Model Integration**: Utilizes fast AI inference through the Groq SDK, configurable to models like `gemma2-9b-it`, `llama-3.1-8b-instant`, or `llama-3.3-70b-versatile`.
* **Smart fallback architecture**: Backend automatically falls back to static template rendering when `GROQ_API_KEY` is not present, keeping the application fully functional.

### 8. High-Fidelity PDF Export Generator
* **Aesthetic PDF Document Layout**: Clean document formats built using ReportLab, sporting modern fonts and aligned grid components.
* **Budget Breakdown Charts**: Aligns estimated costs, taxes, and reserves in structured tables.
* **Day-by-Day Travel Guide**: Embeds detailed activity timelines (Morning, Afternoon, Evening) for offline travel support.
* **Disk-Free PDF Streaming**: Streams raw PDF bytes from system memory straight to the user's browser, eliminating disk clutter.

### 9. Multi-Adapter Database Fallback
* **Automatic Database Engine Selector**: Connects to a robust PostgreSQL server by default, but automatically registers a local SQLite database adapter (`budgetbound.db`) if PostgreSQL is offline or unreachable.
* **60 Pre-seeded Indian Destinations**: Seeding system that populates the database with 60 detailed destinations categorized across 6 primary travel types.

---

## Technical Stack

### Frontend
- **Framework:** React 19 with TypeScript (Vite 8)
- **Styling:** Tailwind CSS v4 with class-based dark mode and glassmorphism
- **Routing:** React Router v7 with inline `ProtectedRoute` / `AnonymousRoute` guards in `App.tsx`
- **Charts:** Recharts (budget comparison bars, expense category pie charts)
- **Maps:** Leaflet + React-Leaflet (interactive starting-city picker via OpenStreetMap/Nominatim)
- **Icons:** Lucide React
- **HTTP Client:** Axios with JWT request interceptors and 401 session handling

### Backend
- **Framework:** FastAPI (Python 3.11) on Uvicorn
- **Database:** PostgreSQL with automatic **SQLite fallback** (`budgetbound.db`) when PostgreSQL is unreachable
- **ORM:** SQLAlchemy 2.x
- **Auth:** JWT bearer tokens (HMAC-SHA256) + bcrypt password hashing via `passlib`
- **PDF Reports:** ReportLab (multi-page travel plan PDFs)
- **AI:** Groq API (`gemini.py` service) for itinerary generation and chat; template fallback when no API key is set

---

## Core Algorithms

### 1. Weighted Destination Scoring

$$\text{Score} = 35\% \times \text{Budget Fit} + 25\% \times \text{Category Match} + 12\% \times \text{Rating} + 20\% \times \text{Comfort Match} + 8\% \times \text{Duration Suitability}$$

- **Budget Fit (35%):** Rewards destinations that use budget efficiently. Budget is enforced as a **hard constraint**â€”destinations over budget are filtered out.
- **Category Match (25%):** Blends main category (60%) with sub-category affinities from DB columns.
- **Rating (12%):** Normalized to 0â€“1 from a 5-star scale.
- **Comfort Match (20%):** Stronger alignment between comfort slider (0-100) and destination's comfort profile.
- **Duration Suitability (8%):** Suitability check compared with recommended trip days.

### 2. Regional Surcharges

Starting city and destination are mapped to region clusters (North, South, East, West). Multipliers are applied directly to base transport costs first, ensuring regional differences remain after minimum cost floors:

- **Same region:** 0.45Ã— transport multiplier (55% discount)
- **South â†” West (e.g. Chennai â†” Goa):** 1.05Ã— multiplier (medium-short haul)
- **North â†” West (e.g. Delhi â†” Goa), North â†” East (e.g. Delhi â†” Kolkata):** 1.25Ã— multiplier (medium-long haul)
- **North â†” South (e.g. Delhi â†” Chennai), East â†” West (e.g. Kolkata â†” Mumbai):** 1.45Ã— multiplier (long haul)

### 3. Rules-Based Budget Optimizer

- **Hotel Downgrade:** Luxury â†’ Mid-Range â†’ Budget (35â€“50% lodging savings)
- **Transit Swap:** Replace flights with trains for high-comfort trips
- **Itinerary Streamlining:** Halve daily activities to cut ticket costs
- **Surplus Upgrade:** Suggest luxury lodging when under budget

---

## Theme System

Class-based dark/light mode via `ThemeContext`:

```css
@variant dark (&:where(.dark, .dark *));
```

Selection persists in `localStorage` under the key `theme`. Recharts tooltips adapt background and text colors for both modes.

---

## AI Integration (Groq)

The backend uses the **Groq API** (not OpenAI) for AI features:

| Feature | Status | Notes |
|---------|--------|-------|
| **Itinerary generation** | Active | Used during trip planning; falls back to templates if `GROQ_API_KEY` is missing |
| **AI chat** (`/api/ai/chat`) | Active | Interactive travel companion in AI Companion page |
| **Trip regeneration** | Planned | Returns 501 |
| **Budget explanation** | Planned | Returns 501 |
| **Personalized planner** | Planned | Returns 501 |

Set `GROQ_API_KEY` in `backend/.env` to enable AI. Default model is `gemma2-9b-it`; override with `GROQ_MODEL`.

---

## Environment Variables

Create `backend/.env` with:

```env
# Database (PostgreSQL; falls back to SQLite if unreachable)
DATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/budgetbound

# Auth
JWT_SECRET_KEY=your_secret_key_here

# Groq AI (optional â€” templates used when absent)
GROQ_API_KEY=your_groq_api_key
GROQ_MODEL=gemma2-9b-it
```

The frontend API base URL is set in `frontend/src/services/api.ts` (`http://localhost:8000`).

---

## Installation & Setup

### Prerequisites

- Node.js 18+
- Python 3.11+
- PostgreSQL (optional â€” SQLite fallback works out of the box)

### Local Development

**1. Backend**

```bash
cd backend
python -m venv venv
# Windows:
venv\Scripts\activate
# macOS/Linux:
source venv/bin/activate

pip install -r requirements.txt
uvicorn main:app --reload
```

On startup, the server creates tables, attempts PostgreSQL, falls back to `budgetbound.db` if needed, and seeds 60 destinations.

- API: `http://localhost:8000`
- Swagger docs: `http://localhost:8000/docs`

**2. Frontend** (separate terminal)

```bash
cd frontend
npm install
npm run dev
```

App: `http://localhost:5173`

### Docker Compose

```bash
docker-compose up --build
```

| Service | URL |
|---------|-----|
| Frontend | `http://localhost:3000` |
| Backend API / Docs | `http://localhost:8000` / `http://localhost:8000/docs` |
| PostgreSQL | `localhost:5432` |

---

## API Overview

| Prefix | Endpoints |
|--------|-----------|
| `/api/auth` | `POST /register`, `POST /login`, `POST /login-json`, `GET /me` |
| `/api/destinations` | `GET /`, `GET /states`, `GET /categories`, `GET /{id}` |
| `/api/trips` | `POST /plan`, `POST /`, `GET /`, `GET /{id}`, `DELETE /{id}`, `GET /reverse/search`, `POST /{id}/optimize`, `POST /{id}/optimize/apply`, `GET /{id}/pdf` |
| `/api/expenses` | `POST /`, `GET /trip/{trip_id}`, `PUT /{id}`, `DELETE /{id}`, `GET /trip/{trip_id}/stats` |
| `/api/ai` | `POST /chat`, `POST /regenerate`, `POST /explain`, `POST /personalized-plan` |
| `/api/dashboard` | `GET /stats` |

---

## Database Seed (60 Destinations)

Six categories, 10 destinations each:

| Category | Examples |
|----------|----------|
| **Beach** | North Goa, South Goa, Gokarna, Varkala, Havelock Island, Kovalam, Pondicherry |
| **Nature** | Munnar, Coorg, Ooty, Wayanad, Kodaikanal, Araku Valley |
| **Adventure** | Manali, Leh Ladakh, Rishikesh, Auli, Spiti Valley, Bir Billing, Gulmarg |
| **Religious** | Kedarnath, Varanasi, Haridwar, Amritsar, Tirupati, Rameshwaram |
| **City Exploration** | Mumbai, Delhi, Bengaluru, Hyderabad, Kolkata, Jaipur, Chandigarh, Chennai |
| **Romantic** | Srinagar, Udaipur, Darjeeling, Nainital, Alleppey Houseboat, Shimla |

Each destination includes expanded metadata: `budget_tier`, `recommended_duration`, `comfort_level`, and per-category affinity scores used by the scoring engine.

version: '3.8'

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:15-alpine
    container_name: budgetbound_db
    restart: always
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      POSTGRES_DB: budgetbound
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - pgdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data

  backend:
    build: ./backend
    container_name: budgetbound_backend
    restart: always
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres:postgres@db:5432/budgetbound
      - JWT_SECRET_KEY=budgetbound_super_secret_signing_key_2026_dev
    depends_on:
      - db

  frontend:
    build: ./frontend
    container_name: budgetbound_frontend
    restart: always
    ports:
      - "3000:80"
    depends_on:
      - backend

volumes:
  pgdata:



================================================
FILE: verify_ai.py
================================================
import requests
import sys

BASE_URL = "http://localhost:8000"

def verify():
    print("1. Logging in...")
    email = "pdf_test_user@example.com"
    password = "password123"
    login_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/auth/login-json"
    try:
        r = requests.post(login_url, json={"email": email, "password": password})
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"Login failed: {r.status_code} - {r.text}")
            sys.exit(1)
        token = r.json().get("access_token")
        print("Login successful.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Login connection failed: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
    
    print("\n2. Testing AI Chat Assistant (/api/ai/chat)...")
    chat_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/ai/chat"
    chat_data = {"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "Suggest 3 budget activities in Bangalore under 500 INR each."}]}
    try:
        r = requests.post(chat_url, json=chat_data, headers=headers)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"Chat failed: {r.status_code} - {r.text}")
            sys.exit(1)
        resp = r.json()
        print("Chat response obtained:")
        print(resp.get("reply", "")[:200] + "...")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Chat request failed: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

    print("\n3. Testing AI Personalized Planner (/api/ai/personalized-plan)...")
    plan_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/ai/personalized-plan"
    plan_data = {
        "preferences": {
            "keywords": "Kashmir lakes",
            "budget": 20000.0,
            "days": 3,
            "special": "vegetarian food only"
        }
    }
    try:
        r = requests.post(plan_url, json=plan_data, headers=headers)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"Personalized plan failed: {r.status_code} - {r.text}")
            sys.exit(1)
        planned = r.json()
        print("Personalized planner response obtained successfully:")
        print(f"Plan Name: {planned.get('plan_name')}")
        print(f"Estimated Cost: {planned.get('estimated_cost')}")
        print(f"Days: {len(planned.get('days', []))}")
    except Exception as e:
        sys.exit(1)

    # Let's find a trip to test regenerator and explainer
    print("\n4. Getting user trips...")
    trips_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/trips/"
    try:
        r = requests.get(trips_url, headers=headers)
        trips = r.json()
        if not trips:
            print("No trips found to test regenerate and explain. Please run verify_pdf first.")
            sys.exit(1)
        trip_id = trips[0]["id"]
        print(f"Testing with Trip ID: {trip_id}")
    except Exception as e:
        sys.exit(1)
        
    print("\n5. Testing AI Explainability (/api/ai/explain)...")
    explain_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/ai/explain"
    try:
        r = requests.post(explain_url, json={"trip_id": trip_id}, headers=headers)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"Explain failed: {r.status_code} - {r.text}")
            sys.exit(1)
        explained = r.json()
        print("Explain response obtained:")
        print(explained.get("explanation", "")[:200] + "...")
    except Exception as e:
        sys.exit(1)
        
    print("\n6. Testing AI Trip Regenerator (/api/ai/regenerate)...")
    regen_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/ai/regenerate"
    regen_data = {
        "trip_id": trip_id,
        "instruction": "save money, use train transport and cheaper lodging"
    }
    try:
        r = requests.post(regen_url, json=regen_data, headers=headers)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"Regenerate failed: {r.status_code} - {r.text}")
            sys.exit(1)
        regenerated = r.json()
        print("Regenerator response obtained successfully:")
        print(f"Estimated Accommodation: {regenerated.get('costs', {}).get('hotel_cost')}")
        print(f"New Total Cost: {regenerated.get('costs', {}).get('total_estimated_cost')}")
    except Exception as e:
        sys.exit(1)

    print("\n7. Testing applying regeneration (PUT /api/trips/{trip_id})...")
    put_url = f"{BASE_URL}/api/trips/{trip_id}"
    try:
        r = requests.put(put_url, json=regenerated, headers=headers)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"Applying regeneration failed: {r.status_code} - {r.text}")
            sys.exit(1)
        print("PUT request successful. Trip updated in database.")
    except Exception as e:
        sys.exit(1)

    print("\nSUCCESS: All AI Copilot features successfully verified!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify()

from fastapi import FastAPI, Depends, HTTPException
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
import logging

from app import models, seed, auth
from app.database import engine, get_db
from app.routers import auth as auth_router, destinations, trips, expenses, ai_placeholders, notifications, documents, admin_analytics

import os
os.makedirs("uploads", exist_ok=True)

# Setup Logging
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger("budgetbound.api")

# Create tables in Database (if they don't exist)
try:
    models.Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    logger.info("Database tables initialized.")
    # Schema migration: Add new columns if missing
    from sqlalchemy import text
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        try:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE trips ADD COLUMN status VARCHAR DEFAULT 'Planned'"))
            logger.info("Database migration: Added status column.")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE trips ADD COLUMN start_date VARCHAR"))
            logger.info("Database migration: Added start_date column.")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE trips ADD COLUMN is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0"))
            logger.info("Database migration: Added is_public column.")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE trips ADD COLUMN share_token VARCHAR"))
            logger.info("Database migration: Added share_token column.")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            conn.execute(text("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS packing_items (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    trip_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    name VARCHAR NOT NULL,
                    checked BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
                    FOREIGN KEY(trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                )
            """))
            logger.info("Database migration: Created packing_items table.")
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.error(f"Database migration error creating packing_items: {ex}")
            pass
        # User preferences migrations
        for col, default_val in [
            ("photo_url", "VARCHAR"),
            ("default_budget", "FLOAT"),
            ("preferred_travel_style", "VARCHAR"),
            ("preferred_comfort_level", "INTEGER"),
            ("default_starting_city", "VARCHAR"),
            ("preferred_theme", "VARCHAR DEFAULT 'light'"),
            ("is_admin", "BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0")
        ]:
            try:
                conn.execute(text(f"ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN {col} {default_val}"))
                logger.info(f"Database migration: Added user column {col}.")
            except Exception:
                pass
        # Notifications table migration
        try:
            conn.execute(text("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notifications (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    type VARCHAR NOT NULL,
                    message VARCHAR NOT NULL,
                    is_read BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
                    FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                )
            """))
            logger.info("Database migration: Created notifications table.")
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.error(f"Database migration error creating notifications: {ex}")
            pass
        # Travel documents table migration
        try:
            conn.execute(text("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS travel_documents (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    trip_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    category VARCHAR NOT NULL,
                    filename VARCHAR NOT NULL,
                    filepath VARCHAR NOT NULL,
                    filesize INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    uploaded_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
                    FOREIGN KEY(trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                )
            """))
            logger.info("Database migration: Created travel_documents table.")
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.error(f"Database migration error creating travel_documents: {ex}")
            pass
except Exception as e:
    logger.error(f"Error initializing database tables: {e}")

@app.get("/api/dashboard/stats")
def get_dashboard_stats(
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    import datetime

    # Total Trips
    trips_list = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).all()
    
    # Auto-update status for "Planned" and "In Progress" based on dates
    for t in trips_list:
        if not t.status:
            t.status = "Planned"
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(t)

        if t.status in ["Planned", "In Progress"] and t.start_date:
            try:
                start_dt = datetime.datetime.strptime(t.start_date, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
                today = datetime.date.today()
                end_dt = start_dt + datetime.timedelta(days=t.days)
                
                new_status = t.status
                if today >= end_dt:
                    new_status = "Completed"
                elif today >= start_dt:
                    new_status = "In Progress"
                    
                if new_status != t.status:
                    t.status = new_status
                    db.commit()
                    db.refresh(t)
            except Exception:
                pass

    total_trips = len(trips_list)

    # Planned Budget
    planned_budget = sum([t.budget for t in trips_list])
    
    # Actual expenses for all trips
    trip_ids = [t.id for t in trips_list]
    all_expenses = db.query(models.Expense).filter(models.Expense.trip_id.in_(trip_ids)).all() if trip_ids else []
    actual_expenses = sum([e.amount for e in all_expenses])

    # Savings = planned budget - actual expenses
    savings = max(0.0, planned_budget - actual_expenses)

    # Status counts
    trips_planned = len([t for t in trips_list if t.status == "Planned"])
    trips_in_progress = len([t for t in trips_list if t.status == "In Progress"])
    trips_completed = len([t for t in trips_list if t.status == "Completed"])
    trips_cancelled = len([t for t in trips_list if t.status == "Cancelled"])
    trips_draft = len([t for t in trips_list if t.status == "Draft"])

    # Recent Trips (limit 5)
    recent_trips = []
    # Sort trips by created_at desc
    sorted_trips = sorted(trips_list, key=lambda x: x.created_at, reverse=True)[:5]
    for t in sorted_trips:
        recent_trips.append({
            "id": t.id,
            "destination": t.destination.name,
            "state": t.destination.state,
            "budget": t.budget,
            "estimated_cost": t.total_estimated_cost,
            "days": t.days,
            "category": t.travel_type,
            "date": t.created_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
            "status": t.status,
            "start_date": t.start_date
        })

    # Upcoming Trips (limit 5)
    upcoming_raw = [t for t in trips_list if t.status == "Planned"]
    upcoming_trips = []
    sorted_upcoming = sorted(upcoming_raw, key=lambda x: x.start_date or "9999-12-31")[:5]
    for t in sorted_upcoming:
        upcoming_trips.append({
            "id": t.id,
            "destination": t.destination.name,
            "state": t.destination.state,
            "budget": t.budget,
            "estimated_cost": t.total_estimated_cost,
            "days": t.days,
            "category": t.travel_type,
            "status": t.status,
            "start_date": t.start_date
        })

    # Chart 1: Budget Usage (Compare Budget vs Estimated vs Actual for each trip)
    budget_usage_chart = []
    for t in sorted(trips_list, key=lambda x: x.created_at)[:6]: # oldest to newest (limit 6)
        trip_expenses = [e.amount for e in all_expenses if e.trip_id == t.id]
        budget_usage_chart.append({
            "name": t.destination.name,
            "budget": t.budget,
            "estimated": t.total_estimated_cost,
            "actual": sum(trip_expenses)
        })

    # Chart 2: Expense Categories distribution across all trips
    expense_categories = {
        "Transport": 0.0,
        "Hotel": 0.0,
        "Food": 0.0,
        "Activities": 0.0,
        "Shopping": 0.0,
        "Other": 0.0
    }
    for e in all_expenses:
        cat = e.category
        if cat in expense_categories:
            expense_categories[cat] += e.amount
        else:
            expense_categories["Other"] += e.amount

    category_chart = [
        {"name": k, "value": round(v, 2)} for k, v in expense_categories.items() if v > 0
    ]
    # If empty, add placeholder values for chart aesthetic
    if not category_chart:
        category_chart = [
            {"name": "Transport", "value": 0.0},
            {"name": "Hotel", "value": 0.0},
            {"name": "Food", "value": 0.0},
            {"name": "Activities", "value": 0.0},
            {"name": "Shopping", "value": 0.0},
            {"name": "Other", "value": 0.0}
        ]

    return {
        "total_trips": total_trips,
        "planned_budget": round(planned_budget, 2),
        "actual_expenses": round(actual_expenses, 2),
        "savings": round(savings, 2),
        "trips_planned": trips_planned,
        "trips_in_progress": trips_in_progress,
        "trips_completed": trips_completed,
        "trips_cancelled": trips_cancelled,
        "trips_draft": trips_draft,
        "recent_trips": recent_trips,
        "upcoming_trips": upcoming_trips,
        "budget_usage_chart": budget_usage_chart,
        "category_chart": category_chart
    }

FILE: backend/app/auth.py
================================================
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.config import settings
from app.database import get_db
from app import models

# Password hashing
pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")

oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="api/auth/login")

def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)

def get_password_hash(password: str) -> str:
    return pwd_context.hash(password)

def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None) -> str:
    to_encode = data.copy()
    if expires_delta:
        expire = datetime.utcnow() + expires_delta
    else:
        expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithm=settings.JWT_ALGORITHM)
    return encoded_jwt

def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> models.User:
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Could not validate credentials",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[settings.JWT_ALGORITHM])
        email: str = payload.get("sub")
        if email is None:
            raise credentials_exception
    except JWTError:
        raise credentials_exception
        
    user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == email).first()
    if user is None:
        raise credentials_exception
    return user

import os
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings

class Settings(BaseSettings):
    JWT_SECRET_KEY: str = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "budgetbound_super_secret_signing_key_2026_dev")
    JWT_ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 1440  # 24 hours
    
    # Primary: PostgreSQL, Fallback: local SQLite file
    DATABASE_URL: str = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/budgetbound")
    SQLITE_FALLBACK_URL: str = "sqlite:///./budgetbound.db"

    class Config:
        env_file = ".env"
        extra = "ignore"

settings = Settings()

FILE: backend/app/database.py
================================================
import logging
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger("uvicorn.error")

# Try to connect to PostgreSQL. Fallback to SQLite if it fails.
engine = None
is_sqlite = False

try:
    # Attempt to initialize PostgreSQL engine
    if settings.DATABASE_URL.startswith("postgresql"):
        engine = create_engine(
            settings.DATABASE_URL,
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            connect_args={"connect_timeout": 5}  # Short timeout to detect failure quickly
        )
        # Test connection
        with engine.connect() as conn:
            logger.info("Successfully connected to PostgreSQL database.")
    else:
        raise ValueError("Not a PostgreSQL URL")
except (OperationalError, Exception) as e:
    logger.warning(
        f"PostgreSQL connection failed: {e}. Falling back to SQLite database at {settings.SQLITE_FALLBACK_URL}"
    )
    is_sqlite = True
    engine = create_engine(
        settings.SQLITE_FALLBACK_URL,
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}
    )

SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)
Base = declarative_base()

def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()

import datetime
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Float, DateTime, ForeignKey, JSON, Boolean
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.database import Base

class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String, nullable=False)
    email = Column(String, unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    hashed_password = Column(String, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.datetime.utcnow)

    # Profile & Preferences
    photo_url = Column(String, nullable=True)
    default_budget = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    preferred_travel_style = Column(String, nullable=True)
    preferred_comfort_level = Column(Integer, nullable=True)
    default_starting_city = Column(String, nullable=True)
    preferred_theme = Column(String, default="light", nullable=False)
    is_admin = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # Relationships
    trips = relationship("Trip", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    notifications = relationship("Notification", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    multistop_trips = relationship("MultiStopTrip", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class Destination(Base):
    __tablename__ = "destinations"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String, nullable=False)
    state = Column(String, nullable=False)
    avg_travel_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)  # travel cost per person (base flight/train)
    avg_hotel_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)   # mid-tier hotel cost per night
    avg_food_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)    # food cost per day per person
    avg_activity_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False) # cost per activity
    rating = Column(Float, default=4.0)
    category = Column(String, nullable=False)        # Nature, Adventure, Family, etc.
    best_season = Column(String, nullable=False)
    description = Column(String, nullable=False)
    attractions = Column(JSON, nullable=False)        # list of strings (sample attractions)

    # Expanded metadata
    budget_tier = Column(String, nullable=True)        # Budget, Mid-range, Premium
    recommended_duration = Column(Integer, nullable=True)
    comfort_level = Column(Integer, nullable=True)     # 0 to 100
    adventure_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    nature_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    family_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    romantic_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    religious_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    beach_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    city_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    solo_score = Column(Float, default=0.0)

    # Relationships
    trips = relationship("Trip", back_populates="destination")

class Trip(Base):
    __tablename__ = "trips"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    destination_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("destinations.id"), nullable=False)
    budget = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    days = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    travelers_count = Column(Integer, default=1)
    travel_type = Column(String, nullable=False)     # category
    comfort_score = Column(Integer, default=50)      # 0 to 100
    total_estimated_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    hotel_tier = Column(String, nullable=False)      # Budget, Mid, Luxury
    transport_mode = Column(String, nullable=False)  # Train, Flight
    status = Column(String, default="Planned", nullable=False)
    start_date = Column(String, nullable=True)       # YYYY-MM-DD
    is_public = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    share_token = Column(String, unique=True, index=True, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.datetime.utcnow)

    # Relationships
    user = relationship("User", back_populates="trips")
    destination = relationship("Destination", back_populates="trips")
    itineraries = relationship("Itinerary", back_populates="trip", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    expenses = relationship("Expense", back_populates="trip", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    budget_allocation = relationship("BudgetAllocation", uselist=False, back_populates="trip", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    packing_items = relationship("PackingItem", back_populates="trip", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    documents = relationship("TravelDocument", back_populates="trip", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class Itinerary(Base):
    __tablename__ = "itineraries"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    trip_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("trips.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    day_number = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    activities = Column(JSON, nullable=False)        # list of objects: [{"time": "Morning", "title": "...", "cost": 0}]
    description = Column(String, nullable=True)

    # Relationships
    trip = relationship("Trip", back_populates="itineraries")

class Expense(Base):
    __tablename__ = "expenses"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    trip_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("trips.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    date = Column(String, nullable=False)             # YYYY-MM-DD
    category = Column(String, nullable=False)        # Transport, Hotel, Food, Activities, Shopping, Other
    amount = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    notes = Column(String, nullable=True)

    # Relationships
    trip = relationship("Trip", back_populates="expenses")

class BudgetAllocation(Base):
    __tablename__ = "budget_allocations"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    trip_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("trips.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    transport_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    hotel_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    food_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    activities_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    emergency_reserve = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    local_transport_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    taxes_fees_cost = Column(Float, nullable=False)

    # Relationships
    trip = relationship("Trip", back_populates="budget_allocation")

class PackingItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "packing_items"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    trip_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("trips.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    name = Column(String, nullable=False)
    checked = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)

    # Relationships
    trip = relationship("Trip", back_populates="packing_items")

class Notification(Base):
    __tablename__ = "notifications"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    type = Column(String, nullable=False)
    message = Column(String, nullable=False)
    is_read = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.datetime.utcnow)

    # Relationships
    user = relationship("User", back_populates="notifications")

class TravelDocument(Base):
    __tablename__ = "travel_documents"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    trip_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("trips.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    category = Column(String, nullable=False)
    filename = Column(String, nullable=False)
    filepath = Column(String, nullable=False)
    filesize = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    uploaded_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.datetime.utcnow)

    # Relationships
    trip = relationship("Trip", back_populates="documents")

class MultiStopTrip(Base):
    __tablename__ = "multistop_trips"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    trip_name = Column(String, nullable=False)
    total_budget = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    total_days = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    destinations_str = Column(String, nullable=False) # e.g. "Delhi -> Agra"
    raw_json = Column(JSON, nullable=False)           # Stores the exact complex schema from AI
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.datetime.utcnow)

    # Relationships
    user = relationship("User", back_populates="multistop_trips")

FILE: backend/app/schemas.py

from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, Field
from typing import List, Optional, Any
from datetime import datetime

# User Schemas
class UserBase(BaseModel):
    name: str
    email: EmailStr

class UserCreate(UserBase):
    password: str

class UserLogin(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str

class UserOut(UserBase):
    id: int
    created_at: datetime
    photo_url: Optional[str] = None
    default_budget: Optional[float] = None
    preferred_travel_style: Optional[str] = None
    preferred_comfort_level: Optional[int] = None
    default_starting_city: Optional[str] = None
    preferred_theme: str = "light"
    is_admin: bool = False

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

class UserUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    photo_url: Optional[str] = None
    default_budget: Optional[float] = None
    preferred_travel_style: Optional[str] = None
    preferred_comfort_level: Optional[int] = None
    default_starting_city: Optional[str] = None
    preferred_theme: Optional[str] = None

# Token Schemas
class Token(BaseModel):
    access_token: str
    token_type: str
    user: UserOut

class TokenData(BaseModel):
    email: Optional[str] = None

# Destination Schemas
class DestinationBase(BaseModel):
    name: str
    state: str
    avg_travel_cost: float
    avg_hotel_cost: float
    avg_food_cost: float
    avg_activity_cost: float
    rating: float
    category: str
    best_season: str
    description: str
    attractions: List[str]
    
    # Expanded fields
    budget_tier: Optional[str] = "Mid-range"
    recommended_duration: Optional[int] = 3
    comfort_level: Optional[int] = 50
    adventure_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    nature_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    family_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    romantic_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    religious_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    beach_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    city_score: Optional[float] = 0.0
    solo_score: Optional[float] = 0.0

class WeatherIntelligenceOut(BaseModel):
    current_season: str
    best_season: str
    season_rating: str
    crowd_level: str
    temperature_range: str
    rain_probability: str
    recommendation: str

class DestinationOut(DestinationBase):
    id: int
    weather_intelligence: Optional[WeatherIntelligenceOut] = None

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

class DestinationListOut(BaseModel):
    total: int
    destinations: List[DestinationOut]

# Budget Allocation Schemas
class BudgetAllocationBase(BaseModel):
    transport_cost: float
    hotel_cost: float
    food_cost: float
    activities_cost: float
    emergency_reserve: float
    local_transport_cost: float
    taxes_fees_cost: float

class BudgetAllocationOut(BudgetAllocationBase):
    id: int
    trip_id: int

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

# Itinerary Schemas
class ItineraryActivity(BaseModel):
    time: str
    title: str
    cost: float

class ItineraryBase(BaseModel):
    day_number: int
    activities: List[ItineraryActivity]
    description: Optional[str] = None

class ItineraryOut(ItineraryBase):
    id: int
    trip_id: int

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

# Expense Schemas
class ExpenseCreate(BaseModel):
    date: str
    category: str
    amount: float
    notes: Optional[str] = None

class ExpenseOut(ExpenseCreate):
    id: int
    trip_id: int

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

# Trip Plan Request (from slider & planner)
class TripPlanRequest(BaseModel):
    starting_city: str
    budget: float
    days: int
    travel_type: str
    travelers_count: int = 1
    comfort_score: int = 50
    destination_id: Optional[int] = None

class PackingItemOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    trip_id: int
    name: str
    checked: bool

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

class CarbonFootprintOut(BaseModel):
    transport_mode: str
    co2_emissions: float
    carbon_rating: str
    eco_score: int
    alternative_savings: float

class NotificationOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    user_id: int
    type: str
    message: str
    is_read: bool
    created_at: datetime

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

class TravelDocumentOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    trip_id: int
    category: str
    filename: str
    filepath: str
    filesize: int
    uploaded_at: datetime

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

# Trip Output Schemas
class TripOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    user_id: int
    destination_id: int
    budget: float
    days: int
    travelers_count: int
    travel_type: str
    comfort_score: int
    total_estimated_cost: float
    hotel_tier: str
    transport_mode: str
    created_at: datetime
    destination: DestinationOut
    itineraries: List[ItineraryOut] = []
    budget_allocation: Optional[BudgetAllocationOut] = None
    status: str = "Planned"
    start_date: Optional[str] = None
    actual_spending: Optional[float] = 0.0
    savings: Optional[float] = 0.0
    is_public: bool = False
    share_token: Optional[str] = None
    packing_items: List[PackingItemOut] = []
    carbon_footprint: Optional[CarbonFootprintOut] = None
    documents: List[TravelDocumentOut] = []

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

class ScoreBreakdown(BaseModel):
    budget_compatibility: float
    trip_type_match: float
    comfort_match: float
    destination_rating: float
    duration_suitability: float
    explanations: List[str]

# Reverse Search response
class DestinationSearchScore(BaseModel):
    destination: DestinationOut
    estimated_cost: float
    budget_fit: float
    rating: float
    value_score: float
    breakdown: Optional[ScoreBreakdown] = None


# Optimizer Schemas
class OptimizationOption(BaseModel):
    action_key: str
    label: str
    cost_difference: float
    new_allocation: BudgetAllocationBase
    description: str

class OptimizationResponse(BaseModel):
    current_cost: float
    budget: float
    allocations: BudgetAllocationBase
    options: List[OptimizationOption]

# MultiStopTrip Schemas
class MultiStopTripBase(BaseModel):
    trip_name: str
    total_budget: float
    total_days: int
    destinations_str: str

class MultiStopTripCreate(MultiStopTripBase):
    raw_json: Any

class MultiStopTripOut(MultiStopTripBase):
    id: int
    user_id: int
    created_at: datetime
    raw_json: Any

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

def get_expanded_fields(name: str, category: str, avg_hotel_cost: float) -> dict:
    # Budget tier
    if avg_hotel_cost < 1600:
        budget_tier = "Budget"
        comfort_level = 30
    elif avg_hotel_cost <= 2800:
        budget_tier = "Mid-range"
        comfort_level = 60
    else:
        budget_tier = "Premium"
        comfort_level = 85
        
    # Duration
    if name in [
        "Havelock Island", "Neil Island", "Lakshadweep", "Leh Ladakh", "Spiti Valley", "Zanskar Valley",
        "Tawang", "Gulmarg", "Gulmarg Valley", "Srinagar", "Alleppey Houseboat", "Chadar Trek"
    ]:
        recommended_duration = 6
    elif name in [
        "North Goa", "South Goa", "Munnar", "Coorg", "Ooty", "Darjeeling", "Manali", "Rishikesh",
        "Auli", "Wayanad", "Hampi", "Meghalaya Caves", "Kerala Family Backwaters"
    ]:
        recommended_duration = 4
    else:
        recommended_duration = 3
        
    # Scores out of 100
    scores = {
        "adventure_score": 30.0,
        "nature_score": 30.0,
        "family_score": 50.0,
        "romantic_score": 30.0,
        "religious_score": 10.0,
        "beach_score": 10.0,
        "city_score": 10.0,
        "solo_score": 40.0
    }
    
    if category == "Beach":
        scores.update({"beach_score": 90.0, "romantic_score": 75.0, "family_score": 70.0, "adventure_score": 50.0})
    elif category == "Nature":
        scores.update({"nature_score": 95.0, "romantic_score": 70.0, "family_score": 80.0, "adventure_score": 40.0})
    elif category == "Adventure":
        scores.update({"adventure_score": 95.0, "nature_score": 80.0, "solo_score": 85.0, "family_score": 40.0})
    elif category == "Religious":
        scores.update({"religious_score": 95.0, "family_score": 85.0, "nature_score": 40.0})
    elif category == "City Exploration":
        scores.update({"city_score": 90.0, "family_score": 75.0, "romantic_score": 50.0, "nature_score": 20.0})
    elif category == "Romantic":
        scores.update({"romantic_score": 95.0, "nature_score": 80.0, "family_score": 50.0, "beach_score": 40.0})
    elif category == "Family":
        scores.update({"family_score": 95.0, "nature_score": 70.0, "beach_score": 45.0, "adventure_score": 35.0, "solo_score": 20.0})
    elif category == "Solo":
        scores.update({"solo_score": 90.0, "adventure_score": 75.0, "nature_score": 70.0, "family_score": 25.0})

    # Individual variations
    if "Goa" in name:
        scores["beach_score"] = 95.0
        scores["romantic_score"] = 80.0
    if "Rishikesh" in name:
        scores["religious_score"] = 85.0
        scores["adventure_score"] = 90.0
    if "Leh Ladakh" in name or "Spiti" in name:
        scores["adventure_score"] = 98.0
        scores["nature_score"] = 95.0
        scores["solo_score"] = 95.0
    if "Varanasi" in name or "Kedarnath" in name or "Badrinath" in name:
        scores["religious_score"] = 99.0
    if "Hampi" in name:
        scores["solo_score"] = 95.0
        scores["city_score"] = 70.0
        
    return {
        "budget_tier": budget_tier,
        "recommended_duration": recommended_duration,
        "comfort_level": comfort_level,
        **scores
    }

def seed_destinations(db: Session):
    existing_names = {name for (name,) in db.query(Destination.name).all()}
    added = 0

    for dest_data in DESTINATIONS_DATA:
        if dest_data["name"] in existing_names:
            continue
        expanded = get_expanded_fields(dest_data["name"], dest_data["category"], dest_data["avg_hotel_cost"])
        db_destination = Destination(**dest_data, **expanded)
        db.add(db_destination)
        added += 1

    if added:
        db.commit()
        total = db.query(Destination).count()
        print(f"Seeded {added} new destinations ({total} total in database).")
    else:
        total = db.query(Destination).count()
        print(f"Database already up to date with {total} destinations.")

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy import func
from collections import Counter
import csv
import io
from typing import List

from app import models, schemas, auth
from app.database import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/admin/analytics", tags=["Admin Analytics"])

def get_current_admin(current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user)):
    if not current_user.is_admin:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Admin access required."
        )
    return current_user

@router.get("/")
def get_admin_analytics(
    time_filter: str = "all",
    admin: models.User = Depends(get_current_admin),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    # Fetch all data for in-memory analytics
    users = db.query(models.User).all()
    trips = db.query(models.Trip).all()
    expenses = db.query(models.Expense).all()
    
    import datetime
    now = datetime.datetime.utcnow()
    if time_filter == "30days":
        cutoff = now - datetime.timedelta(days=30)
        trips = [t for t in trips if t.created_at >= cutoff]
        cutoff_str = cutoff.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        expenses = [e for e in expenses if e.date >= cutoff_str]
    elif time_filter == "6months":
        cutoff = now - datetime.timedelta(days=180)
        trips = [t for t in trips if t.created_at >= cutoff]
        cutoff_str = cutoff.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        expenses = [e for e in expenses if e.date >= cutoff_str]
        
    total_users = len(users)
    total_trips = len(trips)
    
    completed_trips = sum([1 for t in trips if t.status == "Completed"])
    total_budget = sum([t.budget for t in trips])
    avg_budget = round(total_budget / total_trips, 2) if total_trips > 0 else 0.0
    
    # Build a lookup of filtered expenses by trip_id for consistent date-scoped savings
    filtered_expense_map: dict = {}
    for e in expenses:
        filtered_expense_map.setdefault(e.trip_id, []).append(e.amount)
    
    # avg_savings: use only the filtered expenses for trips in the filtered set
    trip_ids_in_scope = {t.id for t in trips}
    total_savings = 0.0
    for t in trips:
        # Use filtered expenses if available, otherwise 0 spent (no logged expenses in period)
        if t.id in filtered_expense_map:
            actual_spent = sum(filtered_expense_map[t.id])
        else:
            actual_spent = 0.0
        total_savings += max(0.0, t.budget - actual_spent)
    avg_savings = round(total_savings / total_trips, 2) if total_trips > 0 else 0.0
    
    # Comfort level
    avg_comfort = round(sum([t.comfort_score for t in trips]) / total_trips, 1) if total_trips > 0 else 50.0

    # Counter-based metrics
    dest_names = [t.destination.name for t in trips if t.destination]
    categories = [t.destination.category for t in trips if t.destination]
    
    dest_counter = Counter(dest_names)
    cat_counter = Counter(categories)
    
    popular_dest = dest_counter.most_common(1)[0][0] if dest_counter else "â€”"
    popular_cat = cat_counter.most_common(1)[0][0] if cat_counter else "â€”"
    
    # Top 10 Destinations
    top_destinations = [{"name": name, "count": count} for name, count in dest_counter.most_common(10)]
    
    # Monthly creation trends â€” formatted as 'Mon YYYY' for chart readability
    creation_months: dict = {}
    for t in trips:
        m_str = t.created_at.strftime("%Y-%m")
        creation_months[m_str] = creation_months.get(m_str, 0) + 1
    monthly_creation = [
        {"month": datetime.datetime.strptime(m, "%Y-%m").strftime("%b %Y"), "count": creation_months[m]}
        for m in sorted(creation_months.keys())
    ]

    # Monthly expense trends â€” use filtered expenses only, formatted as 'Mon YYYY'
    expense_months: dict = {}
    for e in expenses:
        try:
            m_str = e.date[:7]  # YYYY-MM
            expense_months[m_str] = expense_months.get(m_str, 0.0) + e.amount
        except Exception:
            pass
    monthly_expenses = [
        {"month": datetime.datetime.strptime(m, "%Y-%m").strftime("%b %Y"), "amount": round(expense_months[m], 2)}
        for m in sorted(expense_months.keys())
    ]

    return {
        "total_users": total_users,
        "total_trips": total_trips,
        "completed_trips": completed_trips,
        "avg_budget": avg_budget,
        "avg_savings": avg_savings,
        "popular_destination": popular_dest,
        "popular_category": popular_cat,
        "avg_comfort": avg_comfort,
        "top_destinations": top_destinations,
        "monthly_creation": monthly_creation,
        "monthly_expenses": monthly_expenses
    }

@router.get("/export-csv")
def export_trips_csv(
    admin: models.User = Depends(get_current_admin),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trips = db.query(models.Trip).all()
    
    # Generate CSV stream
    f = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(f)
    writer.writerow([
        "Trip ID", "User ID", "User Name", "User Email", 
        "Destination Name", "Destination Category", "Budget (INR)", 
        "Days", "Travelers Count", "Transport Mode", "Hotel Tier", "Status", "Created At"
    ])
    
    for t in trips:
        writer.writerow([
            t.id, t.user_id, t.user.name, t.user.email,
            t.destination.name if t.destination else "â€”",
            t.destination.category if t.destination else "â€”",
            t.budget, t.days, t.travelers_count, t.transport_mode, t.hotel_tier, t.status,
            t.created_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        ])
    
    f.seek(0)
    csv_bytes = f.read().encode("utf-8")
    
    return StreamingResponse(
        io.BytesIO(csv_bytes),
        media_type="text/csv",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": "attachment; filename=budgetbound_global_trips_report.csv"}
    )

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app import auth, models
from app.database import get_db
from app.services.gemini import gemini_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/ai", tags=["AI Copilot"])


@router.post("/chat")
async def ai_chat(
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
):
    messages = payload.get("messages", [])
    try:
        return await gemini_service.chat_assistant(messages)
    except NotImplementedError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, detail=str(e))
    except RuntimeError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY, detail=str(e))


@router.post("/regenerate")
async def ai_regenerate(
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip_id = payload.get("trip_id")
    instruction = payload.get("instruction", "")
    
    if not trip_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="trip_id is required")
        
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    try:
        return await gemini_service.regenerate_trip(trip, instruction)
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.post("/explain")
async def ai_explain(
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip_id = payload.get("trip_id")
    if not trip_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="trip_id is required")
        
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    try:
        return await gemini_service.explain_itinerary(trip)
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.post("/personalized-plan")
async def ai_personalized_plan(
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
):
    preferences = payload.get("preferences", {})
    keywords = preferences.get("keywords", "")
    budget = float(preferences.get("budget", 25000))
    days = int(preferences.get("days", 5))
    special = preferences.get("special", "")
    
    try:
        return await gemini_service.generate_personalized_plan(keywords, budget, days, special)
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.post("/multistop-plan")
async def ai_multistop_plan(
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
):
    """Generate an AI-powered multi-stop trip itinerary."""
    stops = payload.get("stops", [])
    budget = float(payload.get("budget", 50000))
    special = payload.get("special", "")

    if not stops or len(stops) < 2:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="At least 2 stops are required.")
    if len(stops) > 6:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Maximum 6 stops are allowed.")
    for s in stops:
        if not s.get("destination", "").strip():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Each stop must have a destination name.")
        if int(s.get("days", 0)) < 1:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Each stop must have at least 1 day.")

    try:
        return await gemini_service.generate_multistop_plan(stops, budget, special)
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from datetime import timedelta

from app import models, schemas, auth
from app.database import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/auth", tags=["Authentication"])

@router.post("/register", response_model=schemas.Token, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def register_user(user_in: schemas.UserCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    # Check if user already exists
    existing_user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == user_in.email).first()
    if existing_user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="A user with this email address already exists."
        )
    
    # Hash password and create user
    hashed_password = auth.get_password_hash(user_in.password)
    db_user = models.User(
        name=user_in.name,
        email=user_in.email,
        hashed_password=hashed_password
    )
    db.add(db_user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(db_user)

    # Generate JWT
    access_token = auth.create_access_token(data={"sub": db_user.email})
    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "user": db_user
    }

@router.post("/login", response_model=schemas.Token)
def login_user(form_data: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    # Authenticate user. OAuth2PasswordRequestForm uses 'username' for email.
    user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == form_data.username).first()
    if not user or not auth.verify_password(form_data.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Incorrect email or password",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    
    # Generate JWT
    access_token = auth.create_access_token(data={"sub": user.email})
    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "user": user
    }

# Also support JSON payload login (convenient for API requests from React without Form data)
@router.post("/login-json", response_model=schemas.Token)
def login_user_json(credentials: schemas.UserLogin, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == credentials.email).first()
    if not user or not auth.verify_password(credentials.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Incorrect email or password",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    
    # Generate JWT
    access_token = auth.create_access_token(data={"sub": user.email})
    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "user": user
    }

@router.get("/me", response_model=schemas.UserOut)
def read_users_me(current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user)):
    return current_user

@router.get("/profile")
def get_user_profile_details(
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trips = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).all()
    
    trips_completed = sum([1 for t in trips if t.status == "Completed"])
    total_budget_planned = sum([t.budget for t in trips])
    
    total_savings = 0.0
    for t in trips:
        actual_spent = sum([e.amount for e in t.expenses])
        total_savings += max(0.0, t.budget - actual_spent)
        
    from collections import Counter
    styles = [t.travel_type for t in trips if t.travel_type]
    fav_style = Counter(styles).most_common(1)[0][0] if styles else "â€”"

    return {
        "user": current_user,
        "stats": {
            "trips_completed": trips_completed,
            "total_budget_planned": round(total_budget_planned, 2),
            "total_savings": round(total_savings, 2),
            "favorite_trip_type": fav_style
        }
    }

@router.put("/profile", response_model=schemas.UserOut)
def update_user_profile_details(
    payload: schemas.UserUpdate,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    if payload.name is not None:
        current_user.name = payload.name
    if payload.photo_url is not None:
        current_user.photo_url = payload.photo_url
    if payload.default_budget is not None:
        current_user.default_budget = payload.default_budget
    if payload.preferred_travel_style is not None:
        current_user.preferred_travel_style = payload.preferred_travel_style
    if payload.preferred_comfort_level is not None:
        current_user.preferred_comfort_level = payload.preferred_comfort_level
    if payload.default_starting_city is not None:
        current_user.default_starting_city = payload.default_starting_city
    if payload.preferred_theme is not None:
        current_user.preferred_theme = payload.preferred_theme

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(current_user)
    return current_user

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from typing import List, Optional

from app import models, schemas
from app.database import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/destinations", tags=["Destinations"])

@router.get("/", response_model=schemas.DestinationListOut)
def get_destinations(
    category: Optional[str] = None,
    state: Optional[str] = None,
    min_rating: Optional[float] = None,
    min_budget: Optional[float] = None,
    max_budget: Optional[float] = None,
    search: Optional[str] = None,
    page: int = 1,
    limit: int = 9,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    query = db.query(models.Destination)
    
    if category:
        query = query.filter(models.Destination.category == category)
    if state:
        query = query.filter(models.Destination.state.ilike(f"%{state}%"))
    if min_rating:
        query = query.filter(models.Destination.rating >= min_rating)
        
    daily_cost = models.Destination.avg_hotel_cost + models.Destination.avg_food_cost
    if min_budget is not None:
        query = query.filter(daily_cost >= min_budget)
    if max_budget is not None:
        query = query.filter(daily_cost <= max_budget)
        
    if search:
        query = query.filter(
            models.Destination.name.ilike(f"%{search}%") | 
            models.Destination.description.ilike(f"%{search}%") |
            models.Destination.state.ilike(f"%{search}%")
        )
        
    total = query.count()
    destinations = query.offset((page - 1) * limit).limit(limit).all()
    
    return {
        "total": total,
        "destinations": destinations
    }

@router.get("/states", response_model=List[str])
def get_unique_states(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    states = db.query(models.Destination.state).distinct().all()
    return sorted([s[0] for s in states if s[0]])

@router.get("/categories", response_model=List[str])
def get_unique_categories(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    categories = db.query(models.Destination.category).distinct().all()
    return sorted([c[0] for c in categories if c[0]])

@router.get("/{destination_id}", response_model=schemas.DestinationOut)
def get_destination(destination_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    destination = db.query(models.Destination).filter(models.Destination.id == destination_id).first()
    if not destination:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Destination not found")
        
    from app.routers.trips import calculate_weather_intelligence
    destination.weather_intelligence = calculate_weather_intelligence(destination, None)
    return destination

FILE: backend/app/routers/documents.py

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status, UploadFile, File, Form
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
import os
import uuid
from typing import List

from app import models, schemas, auth
from app.database import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/trips", tags=["Documents"])

UPLOAD_DIR = "uploads"
os.makedirs(UPLOAD_DIR, exist_ok=True)

@router.post("/{trip_id}/documents", response_model=schemas.TravelDocumentOut, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def upload_document(
    trip_id: int,
    category: str = Form(...),
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    # Verify trip ownership
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
    
    valid_categories = ["Hotel Booking", "Train Ticket", "Flight Ticket", "Bus Ticket", "Passport", "Visa", "Insurance", "Other"]
    if category not in valid_categories:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Category must be one of {valid_categories}")

    # Enforce extension check for upload security
    allowed_extensions = {".pdf", ".jpeg", ".jpg", ".png", ".doc", ".docx", ".txt"}
    file_ext = os.path.splitext(file.filename)[1].lower() if file.filename else ""
    if file_ext not in allowed_extensions:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, 
            detail=f"File format {file_ext} not allowed. Supported formats: {list(allowed_extensions)}"
        )

    # Save file contents with max size verification (10MB limit)
    MAX_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024 # 10MB
    try:
        contents = await file.read(MAX_SIZE + 1)
        filesize = len(contents)
        if filesize > MAX_SIZE:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail="File exceeds maximum allowed upload size of 10MB."
            )
            
        unique_name = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{file_ext}"
        filepath = os.path.join(UPLOAD_DIR, unique_name)
        with open(filepath, "wb") as f:
            f.write(contents)
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to write file to storage disk: {e}")

    # Save metadata in DB
    db_doc = models.TravelDocument(
        trip_id=trip_id,
        category=category,
        filename=file.filename,
        filepath=filepath,
        filesize=filesize
    )
    db.add(db_doc)
    
    # Add a success log notification
    db_notif = models.Notification(
        user_id=current_user.id,
        type="Trip Exported" if "ticket" in category.lower() else "Trip Reminder",
        message=f"Uploaded document '{file.filename}' for your trip to {trip.destination.name}."
    )
    db.add(db_notif)
    
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(db_doc)
    return db_doc

@router.get("/{trip_id}/documents", response_model=List[schemas.TravelDocumentOut])
def list_trip_documents(
    trip_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
    return trip.documents

@router.get("/{trip_id}/documents/{doc_id}")
def download_trip_document(
    trip_id: int,
    doc_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    # Verify owner
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
    
    doc = db.query(models.TravelDocument).filter(models.TravelDocument.id == doc_id, models.TravelDocument.trip_id == trip_id).first()
    if not doc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Travel document not found")
    
    if not os.path.exists(doc.filepath):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="File content missing on storage server")
    
    return FileResponse(
        path=doc.filepath,
        filename=doc.filename,
        media_type="application/octet-stream"
    )

@router.put("/{trip_id}/documents/{doc_id}", response_model=schemas.TravelDocumentOut)
def rename_trip_document(
    trip_id: int,
    doc_id: int,
    payload: dict,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    doc = db.query(models.TravelDocument).filter(models.TravelDocument.id == doc_id, models.TravelDocument.trip_id == trip_id).first()
    if not doc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Travel document not found")
        
    new_name = payload.get("filename")
    if not new_name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Filename parameter is required")
        
    doc.filename = new_name
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(doc)
    return doc

@router.delete("/{trip_id}/documents/{doc_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def delete_trip_document(
    trip_id: int,
    doc_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    doc = db.query(models.TravelDocument).filter(models.TravelDocument.id == doc_id, models.TravelDocument.trip_id == trip_id).first()
    if not doc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Travel document not found")
        
    # Delete from local disk
    if os.path.exists(doc.filepath):
        try:
            os.remove(doc.filepath)
        except Exception:
            pass
            
    db.delete(doc)
    db.commit()
    return None
