@router.post("/{trip_id}/checklist", response_model=schemas.PackingItemOut)
def add_checklist_item(

    trip_id: int,
    payload: dict,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):

    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.id == trip_id,
        models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    # Update costs
    costs = payload.get("costs")
    if costs:
        trip.total_estimated_cost = costs.get("total_estimated_cost", trip.total_estimated_cost)
        trip.hotel_tier = costs.get("hotel_tier", trip.hotel_tier)
        trip.transport_mode = costs.get("transport_mode", trip.transport_mode)
        
        # Update allocation
        alloc = trip.budget_allocation
        if alloc:
            alloc.transport_cost = costs.get("transport_cost", alloc.transport_cost)
            alloc.hotel_cost = costs.get("hotel_cost", alloc.hotel_cost)
            alloc.food_cost = costs.get("food_cost", alloc.food_cost)
            alloc.activities_cost = costs.get("activities_cost", alloc.activities_cost)
            alloc.local_transport_cost = costs.get("local_transport_cost", alloc.local_transport_cost)
            alloc.taxes_fees_cost = costs.get("taxes_fees_cost", alloc.taxes_fees_cost)
            alloc.emergency_reserve = costs.get("emergency_reserve", alloc.emergency_reserve)
            
    # Update itinerary
    itinerary_days = payload.get("itineraries")
    if itinerary_days:
        # Delete old itinerary days
        db.query(models.Itinerary).filter(models.Itinerary.trip_id == trip.id).delete()
        
        # Insert new itinerary days
        for it_day in itinerary_days:
            db_itinerary = models.Itinerary(
                trip_id=trip.id,
                day_number=it_day["day_number"],
                activities=it_day["activities"],
                description=it_day.get("description", "")
            )
            db.add(db_itinerary)
            
    db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "Trip updated successfully"}

    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
    name = payload.get("name")
    if not name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Item name is required")
        
    db_item = models.PackingItem(
        trip_id=trip_id,
        name=name,
        checked=False
    )
    db.add(db_item)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(db_item)
    return db_item

@router.put("/{trip_id}/checklist/{item_id}", response_model=schemas.PackingItemOut)
def toggle_checklist_item(
    trip_id: int,
    item_id: int,
    payload: dict,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    item = db.query(models.PackingItem).filter(models.PackingItem.id == item_id, models.PackingItem.trip_id == trip_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Packing item not found")
        
    if "checked" in payload:
        item.checked = bool(payload["checked"])
    if "name" in payload:
        item.name = payload["name"]
        
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)

    # Check for checklist completion notification
    all_items = db.query(models.PackingItem).filter(models.PackingItem.trip_id == trip_id).all()
    if all_items:
        total_items = len(all_items)
        packed_items = sum([1 for pi in all_items if pi.checked])
        if packed_items == total_items:
            comp_msg = f"Congratulations! Your packing checklist for trip to {trip.destination.name} is 100% complete."
            exists = db.query(models.Notification).filter(
                models.Notification.user_id == current_user.id,
                models.Notification.message == comp_msg
            ).first()
            if not exists:
                db.add(models.Notification(
                    user_id=current_user.id,
                    type="Checklist Completed",
                    message=comp_msg
                ))
                db.commit()
        elif packed_items == 1:
            # Packing started
            start_msg = f"Packing checklist for your trip to {trip.destination.name} has been initiated."
            exists = db.query(models.Notification).filter(
                models.Notification.user_id == current_user.id,
                models.Notification.message == start_msg
            ).first()
            if not exists:
                db.add(models.Notification(
                    user_id=current_user.id,
                    type="Packing Reminder",
                    message=start_msg
                ))
                db.commit()
    return item

@router.delete("/{trip_id}/checklist/{item_id}", status_code=204)
def delete_checklist_item(
    trip_id: int,
    item_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    item = db.query(models.PackingItem).filter(models.PackingItem.id == item_id, models.PackingItem.trip_id == trip_id).first()
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Packing item not found")
        
    db.delete(item)
    db.commit()
    return None

@router.post("/{trip_id}/share/toggle", response_model=schemas.TripOut)
def toggle_trip_share(
    trip_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(models.Trip.id == trip_id, models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")
        
    trip.is_public = not trip.is_public
    if not trip.share_token:
        trip.share_token = uuid.uuid4().hex
        
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(trip)
    
    # Log public share setting updates
    if trip.is_public:
        share_msg = f"Your travel plan to {trip.destination.name} is now public and shared successfully."
        db.add(models.Notification(
            user_id=current_user.id,
            type="Trip Shared Successfully",
            message=share_msg
        ))
        db.commit()
        
    attach_trip_intelligence(trip, db)
    return trip

@router.get("/share/{share_token}", response_model=schemas.TripOut)
def get_shared_trip_details(
    share_token: str,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.share_token == share_token,
        models.Trip.is_public == True
    ).first()
    
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Shared trip not found or this trip is set to private.")
        
    # Auto-update status
    if trip.status in ["Planned", "In Progress"] and trip.start_date:
        try:
            start_dt = datetime.datetime.strptime(trip.start_date, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
            today = datetime.date.today()
            end_dt = start_dt + datetime.timedelta(days=trip.days)
            
            new_status = trip.status
            if today >= end_dt:
                new_status = "Completed"
            elif today >= start_dt:
                new_status = "In Progress"
                
            if new_status != trip.status:
                trip.status = new_status
                db.commit()
                db.refresh(trip)
        except Exception:
            pass
            
    # Calculate spending/savings
    actual_spent = sum([e.amount for e in trip.expenses])
    trip.actual_spending = round(actual_spent, 2)
    trip.savings = round(max(0.0, trip.budget - actual_spent), 2)
    
    # Calculate weather intelligence
    month_val = None
    if trip.start_date:
        try:
            month_val = int(trip.start_date.split("-")[1])
        except Exception:
            pass
    trip.destination.weather_intelligence = calculate_weather_intelligence(trip.destination, month_val)
    
    # Calculate carbon footprint
    cost_val = trip.budget_allocation.transport_cost if trip.budget_allocation else (trip.total_estimated_cost * 0.25)
    trip.carbon_footprint = calculate_carbon_footprint(trip.transport_mode, cost_val)
    
    return trip



@router.get("/reverse/search", response_model=List[schemas.DestinationSearchScore])
def reverse_travel_search(
    budget: float = Query(...),
    days: int = Query(...),
    travel_type: str = Query(...),
    travelers_count: int = Query(1),
    starting_city: Optional[str] = Query("Your City"),
    comfort_score: Optional[int] = Query(50),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    # Fetch all destinations
    destinations = db.query(models.Destination).all()
    if not destinations:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Destination dataset is empty. Please seed the database first."
        )

    comfort = comfort_score or 50
    city = starting_city or ""

    all_scored = []
    for dest in destinations:
        costs = calculate_costs(dest, days, travelers_count, comfort, city)
        est_cost = costs["total_estimated_cost"]
        score = calculate_weighted_score(dest, costs, budget, days, travel_type, comfort)
        budget_fit_pct = round((est_cost / budget) * 100, 1)
        breakdown = calculate_score_breakdown(dest, costs, budget, days, travel_type, comfort)
        all_scored.append({
            "destination": dest,
            "estimated_cost": est_cost,
            "budget_fit": budget_fit_pct,
            "rating": dest.rating,
            "value_score": score,
            "breakdown": breakdown
        })

    # --- HARD BUDGET CONSTRAINT ---
    # Step 1: Keep only destinations whose estimated cost is within budget.
    under_budget = [s for s in all_scored if s["estimated_cost"] <= budget]

    # Step 2: If nothing fits, return a 422 with the cheapest available option.
    if not under_budget:
        all_scored.sort(key=lambda x: x["estimated_cost"])
        cheapest = all_scored[0]
        cheapest_cost = round(cheapest["estimated_cost"], 0)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=422,
            detail=(
                f"No destination found within your selected budget of â‚¹{budget:,.0f}. "
                f"The most affordable option is {cheapest['destination'].name} "
                f"at approximately â‚¹{cheapest_cost:,.0f}. "
                f"Try increasing your budget, reducing trip duration, or lowering comfort score."
            )
        )

    # Step 3: Rank the under-budget destinations by value_score.
    under_budget.sort(key=lambda x: x["value_score"], reverse=True)
    return under_budget[:8]

@router.post("/{trip_id}/optimize", response_model=schemas.OptimizationResponse)
def optimize_trip(
    trip_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.id == trip_id,
        models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")

    dest = trip.destination
    alloc = trip.budget_allocation
    if not alloc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Trip does not have budget allocation")

    options = []

    # Option 1: Reduce Hotel Cost (Switch hotel tier down)
    if trip.hotel_tier == "Luxury":
        # New hotel cost at Mid-range
        new_hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 1.0 * trip.days
        diff = alloc.hotel_cost - new_hotel_cost
        if diff > 100:
            options.append(
                schemas.OptimizationOption(
                    action_key="reduce_hotel",
                    label="Downgrade Hotel to Mid-Range",
                    cost_difference=-diff,
                    description="Switch accommodation from luxury resort to a highly rated 3-star hotel.",
                    new_allocation=schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
                        transport_cost=alloc.transport_cost,
                        hotel_cost=new_hotel_cost,
                        food_cost=alloc.food_cost,
                        activities_cost=alloc.activities_cost,
                        local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
                        taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost * 0.9, # reduced taxes
                        emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve * 0.95,
                        emergency_reserve_cost=alloc.emergency_reserve * 0.95 # legacy helper support
                    )
                )
            )
    elif trip.hotel_tier == "Mid-Range":
        # New hotel cost at Budget
        new_hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 0.65 * trip.days
        diff = alloc.hotel_cost - new_hotel_cost
        if diff > 100:
            options.append(
                schemas.OptimizationOption(
                    action_key="reduce_hotel",
                    label="Switch to Budget Hotel / Homestay",
                    cost_difference=-diff,
                    description="Opt for cozy local homestays or premium hostels instead of mid-tier hotels.",
                    new_allocation=schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
                        transport_cost=alloc.transport_cost,
                        hotel_cost=new_hotel_cost,
                        food_cost=alloc.food_cost,
                        activities_cost=alloc.activities_cost,
                        local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
                        taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost * 0.85,
                        emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve * 0.92
                    )
                )
            )

    # Option 2: Switch flight to train (Reduce Travel Cost)
    if trip.transport_mode == "Flight":
        new_train_cost = max(1000.0, dest.avg_travel_cost * 0.4) * trip.travelers_count
        diff = alloc.transport_cost - new_train_cost
        if diff > 100:
            options.append(
                schemas.OptimizationOption(
                    action_key="reduce_travel",
                    label="Switch Transport from Flight to Train",
                    cost_difference=-diff,
                    description="Travel by AC Sleeper trains instead of flights. Adds travel time but heavily cuts costs.",
                    new_allocation=schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
                        transport_cost=new_train_cost,
                        hotel_cost=alloc.hotel_cost,
                        food_cost=alloc.food_cost,
                        activities_cost=alloc.activities_cost,
                        local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
                        taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost * 0.7,
                        emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve * 0.9
                    )
                )
            )

    # Option 3: Increase Comfort (Upgrade hotel if they have lots of savings)
    current_savings = trip.budget - trip.total_estimated_cost
    if current_savings > 3000.0:
        if trip.hotel_tier == "Budget":
            new_hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 1.0 * trip.days
            diff = new_hotel_cost - alloc.hotel_cost
            options.append(
                schemas.OptimizationOption(
                    action_key="increase_comfort",
                    label="Upgrade Hotel to Mid-Range",
                    cost_difference=diff,
                    description="Upgrade your accommodation for better services, private pool and complimentary breakfast.",
                    new_allocation=schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
                        transport_cost=alloc.transport_cost,
                        hotel_cost=new_hotel_cost,
                        food_cost=alloc.food_cost * 1.1, # slightly better meals
                        activities_cost=alloc.activities_cost,
                        local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
                        taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost * 1.15,
                        emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve * 1.05
                    )
                )
            )
        elif trip.hotel_tier == "Mid-Range":
            new_hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 1.85 * trip.days
            diff = new_hotel_cost - alloc.hotel_cost
            options.append(
                schemas.OptimizationOption(
                    action_key="increase_comfort",
                    label="Upgrade Hotel to Premium Luxury Resort",
                    cost_difference=diff,
                    description="Immerse in luxury. Premium room upgrades, spa sessions, and infinity pools.",
                    new_allocation=schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
                        transport_cost=alloc.transport_cost,
                        hotel_cost=new_hotel_cost,
                        food_cost=alloc.food_cost * 1.3,
                        activities_cost=alloc.activities_cost,
                        local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
                        taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost * 1.35,
                        emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve * 1.2
                    )
                )
            )

    # Option 4: Cut down activities
    if alloc.activities_cost > 1000.0:
        new_act = alloc.activities_cost * 0.5
        diff = alloc.activities_cost - new_act
        options.append(
            schemas.OptimizationOption(
                action_key="reduce_activities",
                label="Streamline Activities Schedule",
                cost_difference=-diff,
                description="Reduce pre-booked activities by 50%. Focus on free natural sights and self-guided exploration.",
                new_allocation=schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
                    transport_cost=alloc.transport_cost,
                    hotel_cost=alloc.hotel_cost,
                    food_cost=alloc.food_cost,
                    activities_cost=new_act,
                    local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
                    taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost * 0.95,
                    emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve * 0.95
                )
            )
        )

    # Format current allocations to match BaseSchema
    current_alloc = schemas.BudgetAllocationBase(
        transport_cost=alloc.transport_cost,
        hotel_cost=alloc.hotel_cost,
        food_cost=alloc.food_cost,
        activities_cost=alloc.activities_cost,
        emergency_reserve=alloc.emergency_reserve,
        local_transport_cost=alloc.local_transport_cost,
        taxes_fees_cost=alloc.taxes_fees_cost
    )

    return {
        "current_cost": trip.total_estimated_cost,
        "budget": trip.budget,
        "allocations": current_alloc,
        "options": options
    }

@router.post("/{trip_id}/optimize/apply")
def apply_optimization(
    trip_id: int,
    payload: dict,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.id == trip_id,
        models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")

    alloc = trip.budget_allocation
    if not alloc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Budget allocation not found")

    dest = trip.destination
    action_key = payload.get("action_key")

    if action_key == "reduce_hotel":
        if trip.hotel_tier == "Luxury":
            trip.hotel_tier = "Mid-Range"
            alloc.hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 1.0 * trip.days
        elif trip.hotel_tier == "Mid-Range":
            trip.hotel_tier = "Budget"
            alloc.hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 0.65 * trip.days
        alloc.taxes_fees_cost *= 0.9
        alloc.emergency_reserve *= 0.95
    elif action_key == "reduce_travel":
        if trip.transport_mode == "Flight":
            trip.transport_mode = "Train"
            alloc.transport_cost = max(1000.0, dest.avg_travel_cost * 0.4) * trip.travelers_count
        alloc.taxes_fees_cost *= 0.75
        alloc.emergency_reserve *= 0.9
    elif action_key == "increase_comfort":
        if trip.hotel_tier == "Budget":
            trip.hotel_tier = "Mid-Range"
            alloc.hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 1.0 * trip.days
            alloc.food_cost *= 1.1
        elif trip.hotel_tier == "Mid-Range":
            trip.hotel_tier = "Luxury"
            alloc.hotel_cost = dest.avg_hotel_cost * 1.85 * trip.days
            alloc.food_cost *= 1.3
        alloc.taxes_fees_cost *= 1.2
        alloc.emergency_reserve *= 1.15
    elif action_key == "reduce_activities":
        alloc.activities_cost *= 0.5
        alloc.taxes_fees_cost *= 0.95
        alloc.emergency_reserve *= 0.95

    trip.total_estimated_cost = round(
        alloc.transport_cost + alloc.hotel_cost + alloc.food_cost +
        alloc.activities_cost + alloc.local_transport_cost +
        alloc.taxes_fees_cost + alloc.emergency_reserve,
        2
    )

    db.commit()
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "message": "Optimization applied successfully",
        "new_cost": trip.total_estimated_cost
    }

@router.get("/{trip_id}/pdf")
def download_trip_pdf(
    trip_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.id == trip_id,
        models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")

    # Generate PDF in memory
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    
    # 0.5 inch margins
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
        buffer, 
        pagesize=letter, 
        rightMargin=36, 
        leftMargin=36, 
        topMargin=36, 
        bottomMargin=36
    )
    
    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    
    # Custom styles
    title_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'DocTitle',
        parent=styles['Heading1'],
        fontName='Helvetica-Bold',
        fontSize=24,
        textColor=colors.HexColor('#6d28d9'), # violet-700
        spaceAfter=15
    )
    
    subtitle_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'DocSubtitle',
        parent=styles['Heading2'],
        fontName='Helvetica-Bold',
        fontSize=16,
        textColor=colors.HexColor('#1f2937'), # gray-800
        spaceBefore=12,
        spaceAfter=8,
        keepWithNext=True
    )
    
    body_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'DocBody',
        parent=styles['Normal'],
        fontName='Helvetica',
        fontSize=10,
        leading=14,
        textColor=colors.HexColor('#4b5563') # gray-600
    )

    bold_body_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'DocBoldBody',
        parent=body_style,
        fontName='Helvetica-Bold'
    )
    
    header_col_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'HeadCol',
        parent=body_style,
        fontName='Helvetica-Bold',
        textColor=colors.white
    )

    story = []
    
    # 1. Header Details
    story.append(Paragraph(f"BudgetBound Travel Plan â€” {trip.destination.name}", title_style))
    story.append(Paragraph(f"Generated for {current_user.name} on {datetime.date.today().strftime('%B %d, %Y')}", body_style))
    story.append(Spacer(1, 15))
    
    # 2. Trip Summary Table
    summary_data = [
        [Paragraph("Destination", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"{trip.destination.name}, {trip.destination.state}", body_style),
         Paragraph("Duration", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"{trip.days} Days", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Starting Budget", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {trip.budget:,.2f}", body_style),
         Paragraph("Estimated Cost", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {trip.total_estimated_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Comfort Score", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"{trip.comfort_score}/100", body_style),
         Paragraph("Travelers", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"{trip.travelers_count} traveler(s)", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Hotel Quality", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"{trip.hotel_tier}", body_style),
         Paragraph("Transport Mode", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"{trip.transport_mode}", body_style)]
    ]
    
    t_summary = Table(summary_data, colWidths=[110, 160, 110, 160])
    t_summary.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ('GRID', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.HexColor('#e5e7eb')),
        ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (0,-1), colors.HexColor('#f9fafb')),
        ('BACKGROUND', (2,0), (2,-1), colors.HexColor('#f9fafb')),
        ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'MIDDLE'),
        ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
        ('TOPPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
    ]))
    story.append(t_summary)
    story.append(Spacer(1, 20))
    
    # 3. Budget Allocation Breakdown
    story.append(Paragraph("Estimated Cost Allocation Breakdown", subtitle_style))
    alloc = trip.budget_allocation
    
    alloc_data = [
        [Paragraph("Category", header_col_style), Paragraph("Estimated Cost", header_col_style)],
        [Paragraph("Transportation", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.transport_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Accommodation (Hotel)", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.hotel_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Food & Dining", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.food_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Activities & Entry Fees", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.activities_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Local Travel / Cabs", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.local_transport_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Taxes & Booking Fees", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.taxes_fees_cost:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Emergency Reserve", body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {alloc.emergency_reserve:,.2f}", body_style)],
        [Paragraph("Total Estimated Trip Cost", bold_body_style), Paragraph(f"INR {trip.total_estimated_cost:,.2f}", bold_body_style)]
    ]
    t_alloc = Table(alloc_data, colWidths=[270, 270])
    t_alloc.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (-1,0), colors.HexColor('#6d28d9')),
        ('GRID', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.HexColor('#e5e7eb')),
        ('BACKGROUND', (0,-1), (-1,-1), colors.HexColor('#f3f4f6')),
        ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'MIDDLE'),
        ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 5),
        ('TOPPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 5),
    ]))
    story.append(t_alloc)
    story.append(Spacer(1, 20))
    
    # 4. Daily Itinerary Section
    story.append(Paragraph("Daily Itinerary Plan", subtitle_style))
    
    itineraries = sorted(trip.itineraries, key=lambda x: x.day_number)
    for it in itineraries:
        day_story = []
        day_story.append(Paragraph(f"Day {it.day_number} Plan â€” {it.description or ''}", bold_body_style))
        day_story.append(Spacer(1, 5))
        
        act_data = [[
            Paragraph("Time Slot", header_col_style), 
            Paragraph("Duration", header_col_style), 
            Paragraph("Category", header_col_style), 
            Paragraph("Activity Title", header_col_style), 
            Paragraph("Estimated Expense", header_col_style)
        ]]
        
        activities = it.activities if isinstance(it.activities, list) else []
        for act in activities:
            cost_val = act.get('cost', 0.0)
            cost_str = f"INR {cost_val:,.2f}" if cost_val > 0 else "Included / Free"
            
            time_val = act.get('time', 'Morning')
            if time_val == 'Morning':
                time_val = '08:00'
            elif time_val == 'Afternoon':
                time_val = '13:00'
            elif time_val == 'Evening':
                time_val = '17:00'
            elif time_val == 'Night':
                time_val = '20:00'
                
            title = act.get('title', 'Leisure time')
            
            category = act.get('category')
            if not category:
                title_lower = str(title).lower()
                if 'hotel' in title_lower or 'check-in' in title_lower:
                    category = 'Hotel'
                elif 'lunch' in title_lower or 'dinner' in title_lower or 'food' in title_lower or 'breakfast' in title_lower or 'dine' in title_lower or 'restaurant' in title_lower:
                    category = 'Food'
                elif 'travel' in title_lower or 'arrival' in title_lower or 'departure' in title_lower or 'flight' in title_lower or 'train' in title_lower or 'cab' in title_lower:
                    category = 'Travel'
                else:
                    category = 'Attraction'
                    
            duration = act.get('duration') or "2.0h"
            
            act_data.append([
                Paragraph(time_val, body_style),
                Paragraph(duration, body_style),
                Paragraph(category, body_style),
                Paragraph(title, body_style),
                Paragraph(cost_str, body_style)
            ])
            
        t_act = Table(act_data, colWidths=[70, 70, 75, 205, 120])
        t_act.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (-1,0), colors.HexColor('#4b5563')),
            ('GRID', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.HexColor('#e5e7eb')),
            ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'TOP'),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 4),
            ('TOPPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 4),
        ]))
        
        day_story.append(t_act)
        day_story.append(Spacer(1, 12))
        
        story.append(KeepTogether(day_story))

    doc.build(story)
    buffer.seek(0)
    
    return StreamingResponse(
        buffer,
        media_type="application/pdf",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename=budgetbound_{trip.destination.name.replace(' ', '_').lower()}.pdf"}
    )

@router.put("/{trip_id}/allocations", response_model=schemas.TripOut)
def update_trip_allocations(
    trip_id: int,
    payload: dict,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.id == trip_id,
        models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")

    alloc = trip.budget_allocation
    if not alloc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Trip does not have budget allocation")

    # Categories from allocation editor
    alloc.transport_cost = float(payload.get("transport_cost", alloc.transport_cost))
    alloc.hotel_cost = float(payload.get("hotel_cost", alloc.hotel_cost))
    alloc.food_cost = float(payload.get("food_cost", alloc.food_cost))
    alloc.activities_cost = float(payload.get("activities_cost", alloc.activities_cost))
    alloc.emergency_reserve = float(payload.get("emergency_reserve", alloc.emergency_reserve))
    
    # Reset helper columns to 0.0 if custom edit is applied
    alloc.local_transport_cost = float(payload.get("local_transport_cost", 0.0))
    alloc.taxes_fees_cost = float(payload.get("taxes_fees_cost", 0.0))

    # Recalculate trip total estimated cost
    trip.total_estimated_cost = round(
        alloc.transport_cost + alloc.hotel_cost + alloc.food_cost +
        alloc.activities_cost + alloc.emergency_reserve +
        alloc.local_transport_cost + alloc.taxes_fees_cost,
        2
    )

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(trip)
    return trip

@router.post("/{trip_id}/allocations/reset", response_model=schemas.TripOut)
def reset_trip_allocations(
    trip_id: int,
    payload: Optional[dict] = None,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    trip = db.query(models.Trip).filter(
        models.Trip.id == trip_id,
        models.Trip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    if not trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Trip not found")

    alloc = trip.budget_allocation
    if not alloc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Trip does not have budget allocation")

    starting_city = ""
    if payload:
        starting_city = payload.get("starting_city", "")

    # Re-calculate default costs
    dest = trip.destination
    costs = calculate_costs(
        dest=dest,
        days=trip.days,
        travelers=trip.travelers_count,
        comfort_score=trip.comfort_score,
        starting_city=starting_city
    )

    # Apply default values
    alloc.transport_cost = costs["transport_cost"]
    alloc.hotel_cost = costs["hotel_cost"]
    alloc.food_cost = costs["food_cost"]
    alloc.activities_cost = costs["activities_cost"]
    alloc.local_transport_cost = costs["local_transport_cost"]
    alloc.taxes_fees_cost = costs["taxes_fees_cost"]
    alloc.emergency_reserve = costs["emergency_reserve"]

    trip.total_estimated_cost = costs["total_estimated_cost"]

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(trip)
    return trip

# ==========================================
# MULTI-STOP TRIP ENDPOINTS
# ==========================================

@router.post("/multistop/save", response_model=schemas.MultiStopTripOut)
def save_multistop_trip(
    req: schemas.MultiStopTripCreate,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Saves an AI-generated multi-stop trip to the user's account.
    Stores the raw JSON result deeply so it can be rehydrated directly on the frontend.
    """
    new_ms_trip = models.MultiStopTrip(
        user_id=current_user.id,
        trip_name=req.trip_name,
        total_budget=req.total_budget,
        total_days=req.total_days,
        destinations_str=req.destinations_str,
        raw_json=req.raw_json
    )
    db.add(new_ms_trip)
    
    # Optional: could create a notification
    notif = models.Notification(
        user_id=current_user.id,
        type="Trip Saved",
        message=f"Your multi-stop journey '{req.trip_name}' has been securely saved."
    )
    db.add(notif)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(new_ms_trip)
    
    return new_ms_trip


@router.get("/multistop", response_model=List[schemas.MultiStopTripOut])
def get_user_multistop_trips(
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Returns all dynamically saved multi-stop trips for the authenticated user.
    """
    trips = db.query(models.MultiStopTrip).filter(models.MultiStopTrip.user_id == current_user.id).order_by(models.MultiStopTrip.created_at.desc()).all()
    return trips

@router.delete("/multistop/{trip_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def delete_multistop_trip(
    trip_id: int,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    ms_trip = db.query(models.MultiStopTrip).filter(
        models.MultiStopTrip.id == trip_id,
        models.MultiStopTrip.user_id == current_user.id
    ).first()
    
    if not ms_trip:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Multi-stop trip not found")
        
    db.delete(ms_trip)
    db.commit()
    return None

import os
from typing import List, Dict, Any

class AITravelService:
    """
    Interface boundary for future OpenAI/LLM integration.
    Currently runs in mock mode returning the service integration status.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
        self.is_connected = bool(self.api_key)

    def verify_connection(self):
        if not self.is_connected:
            raise NotImplementedError("AI service not connected yet. This feature will be available after API integration.")

    async def regenerate_trip(self, trip_id: int, user_instruction: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Regenerates a trip itinerary based on natural language instructions (e.g. 'Make it more historical' or 'Avoid walking').
        """
        self.verify_connection()
        # Future OpenAI API call logic goes here
        return {"status": "success"}

    async def explain_budget_choices(self, trip_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Provides natural language reasoning for the budget decisions made (e.g. 'Why is flight cost high?' or 'Why was Gokarna selected?').
        """
        self.verify_connection()
        return {"status": "success"}

    async def chat_assistant(self, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Interactive chat companion helping users pick states, adjust dining habits, or find hidden tourist spots.
        """
        self.verify_connection()
        return {"status": "success"}

    async def generate_personalized_plan(self, preferences: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Fully custom planner generating itineraries without standard seed boundaries.
        """
        self.verify_connection()
        return {"status": "success"}

ai_travel_service = AITravelService()

# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://github.com/Rel1cx/eslint-react/tree/main/packages/plugins/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://github.com/Rel1cx/eslint-react/tree/main/packages/plugins/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

FILE: frontend/Dockerfile

# Build stage
FROM node:20-alpine AS build

WORKDIR /app

COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm install

COPY . .
RUN npm run build

# Production stage
FROM nginx:alpine

COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
# COPY custom nginx conf if routing needs history fallback (default redirect for SPAs)
RUN echo 'server { \
    listen 80; \
    location / { \
        root /usr/share/nginx/html; \
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html; \
    } \
}' > /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf

EXPOSE 80

CMD ["nginx", "-g", "daemon off;"]

FILE: frontend/src/App.tsx
================================================
import React, { lazy, Suspense } from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './context/AuthContext';
import { Layout } from './components/Layout';
import { Loader } from 'lucide-react';

// Dynamic lazy page imports for optimized code-splitting (Phase 4 Performance)
const LandingPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/LandingPage').then(m => ({ default: m.LandingPage })));
const Login = lazy(() => import('./pages/Login').then(m => ({ default: m.Login })));
const Register = lazy(() => import('./pages/Register').then(m => ({ default: m.Register })));
const Dashboard = lazy(() => import('./pages/Dashboard').then(m => ({ default: m.Dashboard })));
const BudgetPlanner = lazy(() => import('./pages/BudgetPlanner').then(m => ({ default: m.BudgetPlanner })));
const DestinationExplorer = lazy(() => import('./pages/DestinationExplorer').then(m => ({ default: m.DestinationExplorer })));
const DestinationDetail = lazy(() => import('./pages/DestinationDetail').then(m => ({ default: m.DestinationDetail })));
const BudgetOptimizer = lazy(() => import('./pages/BudgetOptimizer').then(m => ({ default: m.BudgetOptimizer })));
const ExpenseTracker = lazy(() => import('./pages/ExpenseTracker').then(m => ({ default: m.ExpenseTracker })));
const AICompanion = lazy(() => import('./pages/AICompanion').then(m => ({ default: m.AICompanion })));
const SavedTrips = lazy(() => import('./pages/SavedTrips').then(m => ({ default: m.SavedTrips })));
const CompareDestinations = lazy(() => import('./pages/CompareDestinations').then(m => ({ default: m.CompareDestinations })));
const SharedTripView = lazy(() => import('./pages/SharedTripView').then(m => ({ default: m.SharedTripView })));
const NotificationsPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/NotificationsPage').then(m => ({ default: m.NotificationsPage })));
const UserProfilePage = lazy(() => import('./pages/UserProfilePage').then(m => ({ default: m.UserProfilePage })));
const AdminAnalyticsPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/AdminAnalyticsPage').then(m => ({ default: m.AdminAnalyticsPage })));
const MultiStopPlanner = lazy(() => import('./pages/MultiStopPlanner').then(m => ({ default: m.MultiStopPlanner })));

// Loading Spinner Indicator (Phase 3 Loader State)
const RouteFallbackLoader = () => (
  <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 gap-4">
    <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-650 animate-spin" />
    <span className="text-sm font-bold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase">Synchronizing BudgetBound...</span>
  </div>
);

// Protected Route Wrapper Component
const ProtectedRoute: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { isAuthenticated, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <RouteFallbackLoader />;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  return <Layout>{children}</Layout>;
};

// Anonymous Route Wrapper Component (Redirects to dashboard if already logged in)
const AnonymousRoute: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { isAuthenticated, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <RouteFallbackLoader />;
  }

  if (isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
};

function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <Suspense fallback={<RouteFallbackLoader />}>
          <Routes>
            {/* Public / Auth Routes */}
            <Route path="/" element={<AnonymousRoute><LandingPage /></AnonymousRoute>} />
            <Route path="/login" element={<AnonymousRoute><Login /></AnonymousRoute>} />
            <Route path="/register" element={<AnonymousRoute><Register /></AnonymousRoute>} />

            {/* Protected Dashboard/App Routes */}
            <Route path="/dashboard" element={<ProtectedRoute><Dashboard /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/planner" element={<ProtectedRoute><BudgetPlanner /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/my-trips" element={<ProtectedRoute><SavedTrips /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/compare" element={<ProtectedRoute><CompareDestinations /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/reverse-search" element={<ProtectedRoute><BudgetPlanner initialMode="discover" /></ProtectedRoute>} />

            <Route path="/explorer" element={<ProtectedRoute><DestinationExplorer /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/explorer/:id" element={<ProtectedRoute><DestinationDetail /></ProtectedRoute>} />

            <Route path="/optimizer/:tripId" element={<ProtectedRoute><BudgetOptimizer /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/expenses/:tripId" element={<ProtectedRoute><ExpenseTracker /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/ai-companion" element={<ProtectedRoute><AICompanion /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/multi-stop" element={<ProtectedRoute><MultiStopPlanner /></ProtectedRoute>} />

            <Route path="/notifications" element={<ProtectedRoute><NotificationsPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/profile" element={<ProtectedRoute><UserProfilePage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
            <Route path="/admin" element={<ProtectedRoute><AdminAnalyticsPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />

            {/* Public Shared Trip Route */}
            <Route path="/share/:shareToken" element={<SharedTripView />} />

            {/* Catch-all route */}
            <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
          </Routes>
        </Suspense>
      </BrowserRouter>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/BudgetAllocationEditor.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Sliders, RefreshCw, Save, Check } from 'lucide-react';

interface AllocationData {
  transport_cost: number;
  hotel_cost: number;
  food_cost: number;
  activities_cost: number;
  emergency_reserve: number;
  local_transport_cost?: number;
  taxes_fees_cost?: number;
}

interface BudgetAllocationEditorProps {
  budget: number;
  initialAllocations: AllocationData;
  onSave: (updatedAllocations: AllocationData, isCustom: boolean) => void;
  onReset?: () => void;
  isSaving?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export const BudgetAllocationEditor: React.FC<BudgetAllocationEditorProps> = ({
  budget,
  initialAllocations,
  onSave,
  onReset,
  isSaving = false,
  className = '',
}) => {
  // 5 main categories
  const [transportPercent, setTransportPercent] = useState(0);
  const [accommodationPercent, setAccommodationPercent] = useState(0);
  const [foodPercent, setFoodPercent] = useState(0);
  const [activitiesPercent, setActivitiesPercent] = useState(0);
  const [emergencyPercent, setEmergencyPercent] = useState(0);

  const [isCustomMode, setIsCustomMode] = useState(false);
  const [saveSuccess, setSaveSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Grouped recommended values helper
  const getGroupedRecommended = () => {
    const t = initialAllocations.transport_cost + (initialAllocations.local_transport_cost || 0);
    const a = initialAllocations.hotel_cost;
    const f = initialAllocations.food_cost;
    const ac = initialAllocations.activities_cost;
    const e = initialAllocations.emergency_reserve + (initialAllocations.taxes_fees_cost || 0);
    const sum = t + a + f + ac + e;
    return { t, a, f, ac, e, sum };
  };

  const syncSlidersWithProps = () => {
    const { t, a, f, ac, e, sum } = getGroupedRecommended();
    
    // If it's a custom allocation or we want to normalize it to 100% of the budget
    const denominator = isCustomMode ? budget : sum;
    const safeDenominator = denominator || 1;

    setTransportPercent((t / safeDenominator) * 100);
    setAccommodationPercent((a / safeDenominator) * 100);
    setFoodPercent((f / safeDenominator) * 100);
    setActivitiesPercent((ac / safeDenominator) * 100);
    setEmergencyPercent((e / safeDenominator) * 100);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    syncSlidersWithProps();
  }, [initialAllocations, isCustomMode, budget]);

  const getPercentagesMap = () => {
    return {
      transport: transportPercent,
      accommodation: accommodationPercent,
      food: foodPercent,
      activities: activitiesPercent,
      emergency: emergencyPercent,
    };
  };

  const handleSliderChange = (key: string, newValue: number) => {
    if (!isCustomMode) {
      // Switch to custom mode first, which normalizes recommended to 100% of the budget
      setIsCustomMode(true);
      return;
    }

    const percentages = getPercentagesMap();
    const oldValue = percentages[key as keyof typeof percentages];
    const diff = newValue - oldValue;

    const otherKeys = Object.keys(percentages).filter((k) => k !== key);
    const sumOthers = otherKeys.reduce(
      (sum, k) => sum + percentages[k as keyof typeof percentages],
      0
    );

    const updated = { ...percentages, [key]: newValue };

    if (sumOthers > 0) {
      otherKeys.forEach((k) => {
        const val = percentages[k as keyof typeof percentages];
        const delta = diff * (val / sumOthers);
        updated[k as keyof typeof percentages] = Math.max(0, val - delta);
      });
    } else {
      otherKeys.forEach((k) => {
        updated[k as keyof typeof percentages] = Math.max(
          0,
          (100 - newValue) / otherKeys.length
        );
      });
    }

    // Normalize to ensure total is exactly 100%
    const totalSum = Object.values(updated).reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0);
    if (Math.abs(totalSum - 100) > 0.01) {
      const error = 100 - totalSum;
      const adjustKey = otherKeys[0];
      updated[adjustKey as keyof typeof percentages] = Math.max(
        0,
        updated[adjustKey as keyof typeof percentages] + error
      );
    }

    setTransportPercent(updated.transport);
    setAccommodationPercent(updated.accommodation);
    setFoodPercent(updated.food);
    setActivitiesPercent(updated.activities);
    setEmergencyPercent(updated.emergency);
  };

  // Recalculated values for preview display
  const currentDenominator = isCustomMode ? budget : getGroupedRecommended().sum;
  const currentTotal = isCustomMode ? budget : getGroupedRecommended().sum;

  const previewTransport = (transportPercent / 100) * currentDenominator;
  const previewAccommodation = (accommodationPercent / 100) * currentDenominator;
  const previewFood = (foodPercent / 100) * currentDenominator;
  const previewActivities = (activitiesPercent / 100) * currentDenominator;
  const previewEmergency = (emergencyPercent / 100) * currentDenominator;

  const currentSumPercent =
    transportPercent +
    accommodationPercent +
    foodPercent +
    activitiesPercent +
    emergencyPercent;

  const handleSave = () => {
    const updated: AllocationData = {
      transport_cost: previewTransport,
      hotel_cost: previewAccommodation,
      food_cost: previewFood,
      activities_cost: previewActivities,
      emergency_reserve: previewEmergency,
      local_transport_cost: 0.0, // merged
      taxes_fees_cost: 0.0, // merged
    };
    onSave(updated, isCustomMode);
    setSaveSuccess(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaveSuccess(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleResetClick = () => {
    setIsCustomMode(false);
    if (onReset) {
      onReset();
    }
  };

  const sliderClass = "w-full h-2 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-violet-600 focus:outline-none disabled:opacity-50";

  return (
    <div className={`bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-6 ${className}`}>
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Sliders className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
            Budget Allocation Editor
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Manually customize how your trip budget is distributed
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">
            Mode:
          </span>
          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
            isCustomMode 
              ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400' 
              : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400'
          }`}>
            {isCustomMode ? 'Custom' : 'Recommended'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sliders Container */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {[
          { key: 'transport', label: 'Transportation', value: transportPercent, cost: previewTransport, color: 'accent-blue-500' },
          { key: 'accommodation', label: 'Accommodation', value: accommodationPercent, cost: previewAccommodation, color: 'accent-violet-500' },
          { key: 'food', label: 'Food & Dining', value: foodPercent, cost: previewFood, color: 'accent-emerald-500' },
          { key: 'activities', label: 'Activities', value: activitiesPercent, cost: previewActivities, color: 'accent-pink-500' },
          { key: 'emergency', label: 'Emergency Reserve', value: emergencyPercent, cost: previewEmergency, color: 'accent-amber-500' },
        ].map((slider) => (
          <div key={slider.key} className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
              <span>{slider.label}</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">{slider.value.toFixed(1)}%</span>
                <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{slider.cost.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <input
              type="range"
              min="0"
              max="100"
              step="0.1"
              value={slider.value}
              onChange={(e) => handleSliderChange(slider.key, parseFloat(e.target.value))}
              onClick={() => {
                if (!isCustomMode) {
                  // Initialize custom mode by locking to budget and scaling
                  setIsCustomMode(true);
                }
              }}
              className={`${sliderClass} ${slider.color}`}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Total Verification */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">
        <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Total Allocations</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className={Math.abs(currentSumPercent - 100) < 0.1 ? "text-emerald-500" : "text-rose-500"}>
            {currentSumPercent.toFixed(0)}%
          </span>
          <span className="text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
            â‚¹{currentTotal.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Action Buttons */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleResetClick}
          disabled={!isCustomMode}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={13} />
          Reset to Recommended
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSave}
          disabled={isSaving || (isCustomMode && Math.abs(currentSumPercent - 100) > 0.5)}
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {isSaving ? (
            'Saving...'
          ) : saveSuccess ? (
            <>
              <Check size={14} />
              Saved!
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Save size={14} />
              Save Custom Allocation
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/components/BudgetHealthMeter.tsx
================================================
import React from 'react';

interface BudgetHealthMeterProps {
  budget: number;
  estimatedCost: number;
  actualExpenses?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export const BudgetHealthMeter: React.FC<BudgetHealthMeterProps> = ({
  budget,
  estimatedCost,
  actualExpenses,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const isActualAvailable = typeof actualExpenses === 'number' && actualExpenses > 0;
  const currentSpent = isActualAvailable ? actualExpenses : estimatedCost;
  
  // Prevent division by zero
  const safeBudget = budget || 1;
  const percentage = (currentSpent / safeBudget) * 100;
  const remaining = Math.max(0, budget - currentSpent);

  // Status colors & messages
  let statusColor = 'text-emerald-500';
  let progressColor = 'stroke-emerald-500';
  let bgColor = 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/20';
  let borderColor = 'border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/30';
  let message = 'Excellent budget utilization';

  if (percentage >= 70 && percentage <= 90) {
    statusColor = 'text-amber-500';
    progressColor = 'stroke-amber-500';
    bgColor = 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/20';
    borderColor = 'border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/30';
    message = 'Approaching budget limit';
  } else if (percentage > 90) {
    statusColor = 'text-rose-500';
    progressColor = 'stroke-rose-500';
    bgColor = 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20';
    borderColor = 'border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/30';
    message = 'Budget exceeded';
  }

  // SVG Circular math
  const radius = 40;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const strokeDashoffset = circumference - (Math.min(100, percentage) / 100) * circumference;

  return (
    <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border ${bgColor} ${borderColor} flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-6 shadow-sm transition-all ${className}`}>
      
      {/* Circular Progress Gauge */}
      <div className="relative w-28 h-28 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center">
        <svg className="w-full h-full -rotate-90">
          {/* Background Circle */}
          <circle
            cx="56"
            cy="56"
            r={radius}
            className="stroke-slate-200 dark:stroke-slate-700 fill-none"
            strokeWidth="8"
          />
          {/* Progress Circle */}
          <circle
            cx="56"
            cy="56"
            r={radius}
            className={`${progressColor} fill-none transition-all duration-500 ease-out`}
            strokeWidth="8"
            strokeDasharray={circumference}
            strokeDashoffset={strokeDashoffset}
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        </svg>
        {/* Inner Text Label */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
            {percentage.toFixed(0)}%
          </span>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
            Used
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Description */}
      <div className="flex-1 space-y-3.5 w-full text-left">
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block">
            Budget Health
          </span>
          <h4 className={`text-base font-extrabold ${statusColor} flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5`}>
            {message}
          </h4>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Target Budget</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">â‚¹{budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Remaining</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">â‚¹{remaining.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Estimated Cost</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">â‚¹{estimatedCost.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>

          {isActualAvailable && (
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Actual Spent</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">â‚¹{actualExpenses.toLocaleString()}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
};



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/DocumentManager.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import api from '../services/api';
import { 
  FileText, 
  Upload, 
  Download, 
  Trash2, 
  Edit3, 
  Loader, 
  Eye, 
  FolderOpen,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  X,
  File
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Document {
  id: number;
  trip_id: number;
  category: string;
  filename: string;
  filepath: string;
  filesize: number;
  uploaded_at: string;
}

interface DocumentManagerProps {
  tripId: number;
}

export const DocumentManager: React.FC<DocumentManagerProps> = ({ tripId }) => {
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<Document[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('Hotel Booking');
  const [selectedFile, setSelectedFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [previewDoc, setPreviewDoc] = useState<Document | null>(null);

  const fetchDocuments = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get(`/api/trips/${tripId}/documents`);
      setDocuments(response.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load documents:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchDocuments();
  }, [tripId]);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
      setSelectedFile(e.target.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleUploadSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedFile) return;

    try {
      setUploading(true);
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', selectedFile);
      formData.append('category', category);

      const response = await api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/documents`, formData, {
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' }
      });
      
      setDocuments(prev => [...prev, response.data]);
      setSelectedFile(null);
      
      // Reset input element
      const fileInput = document.getElementById('document-file-input') as HTMLInputElement;
      if (fileInput) fileInput.value = '';

    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to upload file:', err);
      alert('Error uploading document. Check size and parameters.');
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRename = async (doc: Document) => {
    const nextName = prompt('Enter a new filename:', doc.filename);
    if (!nextName || !nextName.trim() || nextName === doc.filename) return;

    try {
      const response = await api.put(`/api/trips/${tripId}/documents/${doc.id}`, {
        filename: nextName.trim()
      });
      setDocuments(prev => prev.map(d => d.id === doc.id ? response.data : d));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to rename document.');
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (docId: number) => {
    if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this document? This cannot be undone.')) return;

    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/trips/${tripId}/documents/${docId}`);
      setDocuments(prev => prev.filter(d => d.id !== docId));
      if (previewDoc?.id === docId) setPreviewDoc(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to delete document.');
    }
  };

  const handleDownload = (doc: Document) => {
    // Open FastAPI FileResponse in a new window/tab to trigger immediate browser save
    const downloadUrl = `${api.defaults.baseURL || ''}/api/trips/${tripId}/documents/${doc.id}`;
    
    // Append Authorization token manually if standard GET requires headers
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token) {
      window.open(`${downloadUrl}?token=${token}`, '_blank');
    } else {
      window.open(downloadUrl, '_blank');
    }
  };

  const formatFileSize = (bytes: number) => {
    if (bytes >= 1048576) {
      return `${(bytes / 1048576).toFixed(1)} MB`;
    }
    return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`;
  };

  const getCategoryColor = (cat: string) => {
    switch (cat) {
      case 'Hotel Booking': return 'bg-sky-100 text-sky-850 dark:bg-sky-950/40 dark:text-sky-400 border-sky-200';
      case 'Flight Ticket': return 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-850 dark:bg-violet-950/40 dark:text-violet-400 border-violet-200';
      case 'Train Ticket': return 'bg-emerald-105 text-emerald-850 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 border-emerald-200';
      case 'Passport': return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-850 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400 border-amber-200';
      case 'Visa': return 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-850 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400 border-rose-200';
      case 'Insurance': return 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-850 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 dark:text-indigo-400 border-indigo-200';
      default: return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300 border-slate-200';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
          <FolderOpen size={16} className="text-violet-500" />
          Trip Document Manager
        </h3>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">
          {documents.length} Attachments
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Upload Box */}
      <form onSubmit={handleUploadSubmit} className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-750 rounded-xl space-y-3">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <label className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[9px]">Document Category</label>
            <select
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-white focus:outline-none"
            >
              {["Hotel Booking", "Train Ticket", "Flight Ticket", "Bus Ticket", "Passport", "Visa", "Insurance", "Other"].map((cat) => (
                <option key={cat} value={cat}>{cat}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1">
            <label className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[9px]">Upload File Attachment</label>
            <input
              type="file"
              id="document-file-input"
              onChange={handleFileChange}
              className="w-full text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 file:mr-3 file:py-1.5 file:px-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:text-[10px] file:font-bold file:uppercase file:bg-violet-50 file:text-violet-600 dark:file:bg-violet-950/40 dark:file:text-violet-400 hover:file:bg-violet-100"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={uploading || !selectedFile}
          className="w-full bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-extrabold text-[10px] uppercase py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {uploading ? <Loader size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : <Upload size={12} />}
          {uploading ? 'Uploading File...' : 'Upload Document'}
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* Attachments list */}
      {loading && documents.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-6 text-slate-400 gap-2 text-xs">
          <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin text-violet-500" />
          <span>Syncing files...</span>
        </div>
      ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-8 text-slate-400 text-xs italic">
          No files attached to this trip yet.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {documents.map((doc) => (
            <div 
              key={doc.id}
              className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 p-3 rounded-xl hover:border-slate-200 dark:hover:border-slate-600 transition-all text-xs"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3 min-w-0">
                <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-violet-50 dark:bg-violet-950/40 flex items-center justify-center text-violet-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                  <FileText size={16} />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 block truncate" title={doc.filename}>
                    {doc.filename}
                  </span>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center mt-1 text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold">
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border text-[8px] font-extrabold uppercase shrink-0 ${getCategoryColor(doc.category)}`}>
                      {doc.category}
                    </span>
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5 shrink-0"><Calendar size={10} /> {new Date(doc.uploaded_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    <span className="shrink-0">â€¢ {formatFileSize(doc.filesize)}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Actions row */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 justify-end sm:border-0 border-t border-slate-50 dark:border-slate-800 pt-2 sm:pt-0">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleRename(doc)}
                  className="p-1.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-350 transition-colors"
                  title="Rename File"
                >
                  <Edit3 size={13} />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleDownload(doc)}
                  className="p-1.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-555 dark:text-slate-350 transition-colors"
                  title="Download / View"
                >
                  <Download size={13} />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleDelete(doc.id)}
                  className="p-1.5 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 rounded-lg text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-100 transition-colors"
                  title="Delete Document"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={13} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/components/FeedbackStates.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FolderOpen, AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

interface EmptyStateProps {
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  actionLabel?: string;
  onAction?: () => void;
}

export const EmptyState: React.FC<EmptyStateProps> = ({
  icon,
  title,
  description,
  actionLabel,
  onAction
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-10 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-4 max-w-md mx-auto shadow-sm">
      <div className="h-14 w-14 rounded-full bg-violet-50 dark:bg-violet-950 flex items-center justify-center text-violet-650 dark:text-violet-400">

          {title}

          {description}

        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
