FILE: frontend/src/components/Layout.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import api from '../services/api';
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Compass,
  Map,
  MapPin,
  Sparkles,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  Sun,
  Moon,
  User,
  Wallet,
  Briefcase,
  GitCompare,
  Bell,
  Settings,
  ShieldAlert
} from 'lucide-react';

interface SidebarLinkProps {
  to: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  active: boolean;
  onClick?: () => void;
  badgeCount?: number;
}

const SidebarLink: React.FC<SidebarLinkProps> = ({ to, icon, label, active, onClick, badgeCount }) => {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 ${active
          ? 'bg-violet-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-violet-500/20'
          : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
        }`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {icon}
        <span className="font-medium text-sm">{label}</span>
      </div>
      {badgeCount !== undefined && badgeCount > 0 && (
        <span className="h-5 min-w-5 px-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500 text-white text-[10px] font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center animate-pulse">
          {badgeCount}
        </span>
      )}
    </Link>
  );
};

export const Layout: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { darkMode, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);

  const fetchUnreadCount = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/api/notifications/unread-count');
      setUnreadCount(response.data.unread_count);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch unread count:', err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      fetchUnreadCount();
      const interval = setInterval(fetchUnreadCount, 30000); // Poll every 30s
      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
  }, [user, location]);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
  };

  // Determine user avatar image
  const renderAvatar = () => {
    if (user?.photo_url) {
      return (
        <img
          src={user.photo_url}
          alt="Avatar"
          className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full object-cover border border-violet-200"
          onError={(e) => {
            // fallback if URL fails
            (e.target as HTMLImageElement).src = '';
            (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none';
          }}
        />
      );
    }
    return (
      <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950 flex items-center justify-center text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-300 font-extrabold uppercase text-sm">
        {user?.name ? user.name[0] : 'T'}
      </div>
    );
  };

  const navLinks = [
    { to: '/dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: <LayoutDashboard size={20} /> },
    { to: '/planner', label: 'Trip Flow', icon: <Compass size={20} /> },
    { to: '/my-trips', label: 'Saved Trips', icon: <Briefcase size={20} /> },
    { to: '/compare', label: 'Compare', icon: <GitCompare size={20} /> },
    { to: '/explorer', label: 'Destination Explorer', icon: <Map size={20} /> },
    { to: '/ai-companion', label: 'AI Companion', icon: <Sparkles size={20} /> },
    { to: '/multi-stop', label: 'Multi-Stop Planner', icon: <MapPin size={20} /> },
    { to: '/notifications', label: 'Notifications', icon: <Bell size={20} />, badgeCount: unreadCount },
    { to: '/profile', label: 'Preferences', icon: <Settings size={20} /> },
  ];

  // If user is admin, append Admin Analytics link
  if (user?.is_admin) {
    navLinks.push({ to: '/admin', label: 'Admin Analytics', icon: <ShieldAlert size={20} /> });
  }

  // Skip Layout rendering for public shared trip views
  if (location.pathname.startsWith('/share/')) {
    return <>{children}</>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="h-screen overflow-hidden bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex flex-col md:flex-row transition-colors duration-200">

      {/* Mobile Header */}
      <header className="md:hidden flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-sm sticky top-0 z-50">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Wallet className="h-6 w-6 text-violet-600" />
          <span className="font-bold text-xl tracking-tight bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
            BudgetBound
          </span>
        </Link>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <button
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200"
          >
            {darkMode ? <Sun size={18} /> : <Moon size={18} />}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(!sidebarOpen)}
            className="p-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
          >
            {sidebarOpen ? <X size={24} /> : <Menu size={24} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Sidebar Navigation */}
      <aside
        className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 w-64 h-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-r border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex flex-col justify-between p-6 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out md:translate-x-0 ${sidebarOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'
          }`}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 overflow-y-auto max-h-[80%] pr-1">
          {/* Logo */}
          <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet className="h-7 w-7 text-violet-600" />
            <span className="font-bold text-2xl tracking-tight bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              BudgetBound
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Navigation Links */}
          <nav className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {navLinks.map((link) => (
              <SidebarLink
                key={link.to}
                to={link.to}
                icon={link.icon}
                label={link.label}
                active={location.pathname.startsWith(link.to)}
                badgeCount={(link as any).badgeCount}
                onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}
              />
            ))}
          </nav>
        </div>

        {/* User profile & Actions */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 pt-6 mt-auto">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {renderAvatar()}
            <div className="flex flex-col min-w-0">
              <span className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{user?.name || 'Traveler'}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">{user?.email}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 justify-between">
            <button
              onClick={toggleTheme}
              className="flex-1 p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700/50 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium transition-all"
            >
              {darkMode ? (
                <>
                  <Sun size={14} /> Light Mode
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Moon size={14} /> Dark Mode
                </>
              )}
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={handleLogout}
              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-100 dark:hover:bg-rose-900/30 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-medium transition-all"
              title="Logout"
            >
              <LogOut size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden p-6 md:p-10 md:ml-64 w-full">
        {/* Overlay for mobile sidebar */}
        {sidebarOpen && (
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-30 md:hidden"
            onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}
          />
        )}
        {children}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/components/LocationPickerModal.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { MapContainer, TileLayer, Marker, useMapEvents } from 'react-leaflet';
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import { Search, MapPin, X, Loader } from 'lucide-react';
import axios from 'axios';

// Fix for default marker icon in react-leaflet
import icon from 'leaflet/dist/images/marker-icon.png';
import iconShadow from 'leaflet/dist/images/marker-shadow.png';

let DefaultIcon = L.icon({
    iconUrl: icon,
    shadowUrl: iconShadow,
    iconAnchor: [12, 41]
});

L.Marker.prototype.options.icon = DefaultIcon;

interface LocationPickerModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSelectLocation: (city: string) => void;
  initialCity?: string;
}

// Center of India roughly
const DEFAULT_CENTER: [number, number] = [20.5937, 78.9629];
const DEFAULT_ZOOM = 4;

const LocationMarker = ({ position, setPosition, setCityName, setLoadingCity }: any) => {
  const map = useMapEvents({
    click(e) {
      setPosition(e.latlng);
      fetchCityFromCoords(e.latlng.lat, e.latlng.lng, setCityName, setLoadingCity);
    },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (position) {
      map.flyTo(position, map.getZoom());
    }
  }, [position, map]);

  return position === null ? null : (
    <Marker position={position} />
  );
};

const fetchCityFromCoords = async (lat: number, lon: number, setCityName: any, setLoadingCity: any) => {
  setLoadingCity(true);
  try {
    const response = await axios.get(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?format=json&lat=${lat}&lon=${lon}&zoom=10&addressdetails=1`);
    const address = response.data.address;
    const city = address.city || address.town || address.village || address.state_district || address.county;
    if (city) {
      setCityName(city);
    } else {
      setCityName('Unknown Location');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error reverse geocoding:", error);
    setCityName('Error resolving location');
  } finally {
    setLoadingCity(false);
  }
};


export const LocationPickerModal: React.FC<LocationPickerModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSelectLocation, initialCity }) => {
  const [position, setPosition] = useState<L.LatLng | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [cityName, setCityName] = useState(initialCity || '');
  const [loadingSearch, setLoadingSearch] = useState(false);
  const [loadingCity, setLoadingCity] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
     if (isOpen && initialCity && !position) {
        setSearchQuery(initialCity);
        handleSearch(null, initialCity);
     }
  }, [isOpen]);


  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSearch = async (e: React.FormEvent | null, queryOverride?: string) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    const query = queryOverride || searchQuery;
    if (!query.trim()) return;

    setLoadingSearch(true);
    try {
      const response = await axios.get(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=json&q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=1`);
      if (response.data && response.data.length > 0) {
        const result = response.data[0];
        const newPos = new L.LatLng(parseFloat(result.lat), parseFloat(result.lon));
        setPosition(newPos);
        fetchCityFromCoords(newPos.lat, newPos.lng, setCityName, setLoadingCity);
      } else {
         // handle not found
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error searching location:", error);
    } finally {
      setLoadingSearch(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    if (cityName && cityName !== 'Unknown Location' && cityName !== 'Error resolving location') {
      onSelectLocation(cityName);
      onClose();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-xl w-full max-w-3xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col h-[80vh] max-h-[800px]">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
            <div className="p-1.5 bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-900/40 text-violet-600 rounded-lg">
              <MapPin size={18} />
            </div>
            <h2 className="font-bold text-lg">Select Starting Location</h2>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <div className="p-4 flex flex-col gap-4 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
          
          {/* Search bar */}
          <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="flex gap-2">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search className="absolute left-3.5 top-3 text-slate-400" size={16} />
              <input 
                type="text"
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search for a city..."
                className="w-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white"
              />
            </div>
            <button 
              type="submit"
              disabled={loadingSearch}
              className="bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition-all disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              {loadingSearch ? <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : 'Search'}
            </button>
          </form>

          {/* Map */}
          <div className="flex-1 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 relative z-0">
             <MapContainer center={DEFAULT_CENTER} zoom={DEFAULT_ZOOM} scrollWheelZoom={true} style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }}>
                <TileLayer
                  attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors'
                  url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
                />
                <LocationMarker 
                    position={position} 
                    setPosition={setPosition} 
                    setCityName={setCityName}
                    setLoadingCity={setLoadingCity}
                />
             </MapContainer>
          </div>

          {/* Result Footer */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl">
             <div className="flex flex-col">
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Selected City</span>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {loadingCity ? (
                        <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin text-violet-500" />
                    ) : (
                        <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
                            {cityName || 'Click on map to select'}
                        </span>
                    )}
                </div>
             </div>
             
             <button
               onClick={handleConfirm}
               disabled={!cityName || cityName === 'Unknown Location' || cityName === 'Error resolving location' || loadingCity}
               className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shadow-md shadow-violet-600/20"
             >
               Confirm Location
             </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import { Check, Info } from 'lucide-react';

interface ScoreBreakdownData {
  budget_compatibility: number;
  trip_type_match: number;
  comfort_match: number;
  destination_rating: number;
  duration_suitability: number;
  explanations: string[];
}

interface RecommendationExplainabilityProps {

}

export const RecommendationExplainability: React.FC<RecommendationExplainabilityProps> = ({

FILE: frontend/src/components/ShareModal.tsx
================================================
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';
import { 
  X, 
  Globe, 
  Lock, 
  Copy, 
  Check, 
  Download, 
  QrCode, 
  Link2,
  Loader
} from 'lucide-react';
import api from '../services/api';

interface ShareModalProps {
  tripId: number;
  isPublic: boolean;
  shareToken: string | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdate: (updatedTrip: any) => void;
}

export const ShareModal: React.FC<ShareModalProps> = ({
  tripId,
  isPublic: initialIsPublic,
  shareToken: initialShareToken,
  onClose,
  onUpdate
}) => {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const [isPublic, setIsPublic] = useState(initialIsPublic);
  const [shareToken, setShareToken] = useState(initialShareToken);
  const [toggling, setToggling] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const shareUrl = shareToken 
    ? `${window.location.origin}/share/${shareToken}`
    : '';

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsPublic(initialIsPublic);
    setShareToken(initialShareToken);
  }, [initialIsPublic, initialShareToken]);

  // Generate QR Code locally when isPublic and shareUrl are active
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isPublic && shareUrl && canvasRef.current) {
      QRCode.toCanvas(
        canvasRef.current,
        shareUrl,
        {
          width: 180,
          margin: 1.5,
          color: {
            dark: '#1e1b4b', // Deep indigo
            light: '#ffffff'
          }
        },
        (error) => {
          if (error) console.error('Error drawing local QR code:', error);
        }
      );
    }
  }, [isPublic, shareUrl]);

  const handleToggleShare = async () => {
    try {
      setToggling(true);
      const response = await api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/share/toggle`);
      setIsPublic(response.data.is_public);
      setShareToken(response.data.share_token);
      onUpdate(response.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to update trip visibility settings.');
    } finally {
      setToggling(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCopyLink = () => {
    if (!shareUrl) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleDownloadQR = () => {
    if (!canvasRef.current) return;
    const dataUrl = canvasRef.current.toDataURL('image/png');
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.download = `budgetbound_trip_share_${tripId}.png`;
    a.href = dataUrl;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-6 z-50 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
        {/* Close Button */}
        <button 
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-650 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <X size={18} />
        </button>

        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <QrCode className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
            Share Travel Plan
          </h3>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Generate read-only shared links and QR codes to share with friends.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Sharing Settings Toggle Panel */}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {/* Status Row */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-750 rounded-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className={`h-9 w-9 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center ${isPublic ? 'bg-emerald-105 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/20' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 dark:bg-slate-900'}`}>
                {isPublic ? <Globe size={18} /> : <Lock size={18} />}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs">
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white block">
                  {isPublic ? 'Public View Enabled' : 'Private Travel Plan'}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                  {isPublic ? 'Accessible by anyone with the link' : 'Owner authentication required'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <button
              onClick={handleToggleShare}
              disabled={toggling}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all shadow-sm ${
                isPublic 
                  ? 'bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 dark:bg-rose-950/20 dark:hover:bg-rose-900/30' 
                  : 'bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {toggling ? <Loader size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : (isPublic ? 'Make Private' : 'Publish')}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Public Sharing Elements */}
          {isPublic && shareToken ? (
            <div className="space-y-5 animate-fade-in text-xs">
              
              {/* Copy URL Row */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Public Sharing Link</label>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <div className="flex-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 truncate select-all flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Link2 size={12} className="text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                    {shareUrl}
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleCopyLink}
                    className="bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white p-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-600 shrink-0"
                    title="Copy Link"
                  >
                    {copied ? <Check size={16} className="text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy size={16} />}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* QR Code Canvas Card */}
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-5 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-750 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/10 rounded-2xl gap-4">
                <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                  <canvas ref={canvasRef} />
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleDownloadQR}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-extrabold text-[10px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl uppercase transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/10"
                >
                  <Download size={12} />
                  Download QR Code
                </button>
              </div>

            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-center py-6 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-550 text-xs italic flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
              <Lock size={20} className="text-slate-350" />
              <span>Enable public sharing mode to generate QR codes and reader links.</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/TimelineItinerary.tsx
================================================
import React from 'react';
import { 
  Plane, 
  Building, 
  Utensils, 
  Camera, 
  Clock, 
  Compass, 
  ChevronDown, 
  ChevronUp,
  MapPin,
  Car
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Activity {
  time: string;
  title: string;
  cost: number;
  duration?: string;
  category?: string;
}

interface ItineraryDay {
  day_number: number;
  description?: string;
  activities: Activity[];
}

interface TimelineItineraryProps {
  itineraries: ItineraryDay[];
  expandedDays: Record<number, boolean>;
  toggleDay: (dayNumber: number) => void;
  className?: string;
}

export const TimelineItinerary: React.FC<TimelineItineraryProps> = ({
  itineraries,
  expandedDays,
  toggleDay,
  className = '',
}) => {

  const getCategoryIcon = (category?: string, title: string = '') => {
    const term = (category || title).toLowerCase();
    if (term.includes('hotel') || term.includes('stay') || term.includes('check-in') || term.includes('accommodation')) {
      return { icon: <Building size={14} />, bg: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-600 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400' };
    }
    if (term.includes('lunch') || term.includes('dinner') || term.includes('food') || term.includes('breakfast') || term.includes('eat') || term.includes('restaurant')) {
      return { icon: <Utensils size={14} />, bg: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400' };
    }
    if (term.includes('flight') || term.includes('train') || term.includes('arrival') || term.includes('departure') || term.includes('travel') || term.includes('cab')) {
      return { icon: <Plane size={14} />, bg: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-600 dark:bg-blue-950/40 dark:text-blue-400' };
    }
    return { icon: <Camera size={14} />, bg: 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-600 dark:bg-violet-950/40 dark:text-violet-400' };
  };

  const formatActivityTime = (timeStr: string) => {
    if (timeStr === 'Morning') return '08:00';
    if (timeStr === 'Afternoon') return '13:00';
    if (timeStr === 'Evening') return '17:00';
    if (timeStr === 'Night') return '20:00';
    return timeStr;
  };

  return (
    <div className={`space-y-4 w-full ${className}`}>
      {itineraries.map((day) => {
        const isOpen = !!expandedDays[day.day_number];
        return (
          <div 
            key={day.day_number} 
            className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm transition-all hover:shadow"
          >
            {/* Header Accordion Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => toggleDay(day.day_number)}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/10 text-left font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="h-6 w-6 rounded-lg bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/50 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center font-extrabold text-[10px]">
                  {day.day_number}
                </span>
                <span>Day {day.day_number} Timeline</span>
              </div>
              {isOpen ? <ChevronUp size={16} /> : <ChevronDown size={16} />}
            </button>

            {/* Expanded Day Details */}
            {isOpen && (
              <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 flex flex-col gap-4">
                {day.description && (
                  <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs italic leading-relaxed border-l-2 border-violet-400 pl-3">
                    {day.description}
                  </p>
                )}

                {/* Vertical Timeline Thread */}
                <div className="relative pl-6 space-y-6 mt-2">
                  {/* Vertical Connection Line */}
                  <div className="absolute left-[11px] top-2 bottom-2 w-0.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700" />

                  {day.activities.map((act, actIdx) => {
                    const formattedTime = formatActivityTime(act.time);
                    const { icon, bg } = getCategoryIcon(act.category, act.title);
                    const duration = act.duration || "2.0h";
                    const costStr = act.cost > 0 ? `â‚¹${act.cost.toLocaleString()}` : 'Included / Free';

                    return (
                      <div key={actIdx} className="relative flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs group">
                        
                        {/* Timeline Node Point */}
                        <div className={`absolute -left-6 transform -translate-x-[1px] h-6.5 w-6.5 rounded-full ${bg} flex items-center justify-center border-4 border-white dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm shrink-0 z-10`}>
                          {icon}
                        </div>

                        {/* Title & Timing Info */}
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 sm:max-w-[70%]">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="font-extrabold text-[10px] tracking-wider text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1 uppercase">
                              <Clock size={11} />
                              {formattedTime}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 rounded">
                              {duration}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-bold leading-normal text-xs group-hover:text-violet-600 dark:group-hover:text-violet-400 transition-colors">
                            {act.title}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Budget Tag info */}
                        <div className="self-start sm:self-center font-bold text-[10px] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl whitespace-nowrap">
                          {costStr}
                        </div>

                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/components/TripChecklist.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import api from '../services/api';
import { 
  CheckSquare, 
  Square, 
  Plus, 
  Trash2, 
  Loader, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle,
  Package
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PackingItem {
  id: number;
  trip_id: number;
  name: string;
  checked: boolean;
}

interface TripChecklistProps {
  tripId: number;
  readOnly?: boolean;
  initialItems?: PackingItem[];
  onChange?: () => void;
}

export const TripChecklist: React.FC<TripChecklistProps> = ({
  tripId,
  readOnly = false,
  initialItems,
  onChange
}) => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<PackingItem[]>(initialItems || []);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [newItemName, setNewItemName] = useState('');
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);

  const fetchChecklist = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get(`/api/trips/${tripId}/checklist`);
      setItems(response.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch checklist:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!initialItems) {
      fetchChecklist();
    } else {
      setItems(initialItems);
    }
  }, [tripId, initialItems]);

  const handleToggle = async (item: PackingItem) => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    const nextChecked = !item.checked;
    
    // Optimistic update
    setItems(prev => prev.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, checked: nextChecked } : i));
    
    try {
      await api.put(`/api/trips/${tripId}/checklist/${item.id}`, {
        checked: nextChecked
      });
      if (onChange) onChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      // Revert on error
      setItems(prev => prev.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, checked: !nextChecked } : i));
      alert('Failed to save checklist state.');
    }
  };

  const handleAddItem = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newItemName.trim() || readOnly) return;
    
    try {
      setAdding(true);
      const response = await api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/checklist`, {
        name: newItemName.trim()
      });
      setItems(prev => [...prev, response.data]);
      setNewItemName('');
      if (onChange) onChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to add checklist item.');
    } finally {
      setAdding(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteItem = async (itemId: number) => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/trips/${tripId}/checklist/${itemId}`);
      setItems(prev => prev.filter(i => i.id !== itemId));
      if (onChange) onChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to delete checklist item.');
    }
  };

  // Completion calculation
  const totalCount = items.length;
  const packedCount = items.filter(i => i.checked).length;
  const percentage = totalCount > 0 ? Math.round((packedCount / totalCount) * 100) : 0;

  if (loading && items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center p-6 text-slate-400 gap-2 text-xs">
        <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin text-violet-500" />
        <span>Loading packing checklist...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
      {/* Title */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
          <Package size={16} className="text-violet-500" />
          Smart Packing Checklist
        </h3>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400">
          {packedCount}/{totalCount} Items Packed
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Bar */}
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] font-extrabold text-slate-500 uppercase">
          <span>Packing Progress</span>
          <span className={percentage === 100 ? "text-emerald-500" : ""}>{percentage}% Complete</span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-2 w-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div 
            className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ${percentage === 100 ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-violet-600'}`}
            style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Checklist items */}
      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-6 text-slate-400 text-xs italic">
          No packing items yet. Add custom ones below.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1 space-y-2.5">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div 
              key={item.id} 
              className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs p-2 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
                item.checked 
                  ? 'bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/10 border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-400' 
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={readOnly}
                onClick={() => handleToggle(item)}
                className={`flex items-start gap-2.5 text-left font-medium transition-colors ${readOnly ? 'cursor-default' : 'hover:text-violet-600'}`}
              >
                <span className="shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-violet-500">
                  {item.checked ? <CheckSquare size={16} fill="currentColor" className="text-white dark:text-slate-800" /> : <Square size={16} />}
                </span>
                <span className={item.checked ? 'line-through' : ''}>
                  {item.name}
                </span>
              </button>

              {!readOnly && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleDeleteItem(item.id)}
                  className="text-slate-450 hover:text-rose-500 transition-colors p-1"
                  title="Delete item"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={13} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Add Custom Item */}
      {!readOnly && (
        <form onSubmit={handleAddItem} className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Add custom packing item..."
            value={newItemName}
            onChange={(e) => setNewItemName(e.target.value)}
            disabled={adding}
            className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white"
          />
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={adding || !newItemName.trim()}
            className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold p-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all disabled:opacity-50 shrink-0"
          >
            {adding ? <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : <Plus size={14} />}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/context/AuthContext.tsx
================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import api from '../services/api';

interface User {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  created_at: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  default_budget?: number | null;
  preferred_travel_style?: string | null;
  preferred_comfort_level?: number | null;
  default_starting_city?: string | null;
  preferred_theme: string;
  is_admin: boolean;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  token: string | null;
  loading: boolean;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (name: string, email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
  clearError: () => void;
  error: string | null;
  updateUser: (updatedUser: User) => void;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(localStorage.getItem('token'));
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchUser = async () => {
      const storedToken = localStorage.getItem('token');
      if (storedToken) {
        try {
          const response = await api.get('/api/auth/me');
          setUser(response.data);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("Token verification failed", err);
          logout();
        }
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };

    fetchUser();
  }, []);

  const login = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      // Use JSON login endpoint
      const response = await api.post('/api/auth/login-json', { email, password });
      const { access_token, user: userData } = response.data;
      
      localStorage.setItem('token', access_token);
      localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(userData));
      setToken(access_token);
      setUser(userData);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.detail || 
        'Login failed. Please check your credentials.'
      );
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const register = async (name: string, email: string, password: string) => {
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.post('/api/auth/register', { name, email, password });
      const { access_token, user: userData } = response.data;
      
      localStorage.setItem('token', access_token);
      localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(userData));
      setToken(access_token);
      setUser(userData);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.detail || 
        'Registration failed. Email might already be taken.'
      );
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
    setError(null);
    window.location.href = '/';
  };

  const clearError = () => setError(null);

  const updateUser = (updatedUser: User) => {
    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(updatedUser));
    setUser(updatedUser);
  };

  const value = {
    user,
    token,
    loading,
    isAuthenticated: !!token,
    login,
    register,
    logout,
    error,
    clearError,
    updateUser
  };

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

FILE: frontend/src/context/ThemeContext.tsx
================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface ThemeContextType {
  darkMode: boolean;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const ThemeProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [darkMode, setDarkMode] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('theme');
    if (saved) return saved === 'dark';
    return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (darkMode) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
      localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
      console.log('Theme changed to: dark');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
      localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
      console.log('Theme changed to: light');
    }
  }, [darkMode]);

  const toggleTheme = () => setDarkMode(!darkMode);

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ darkMode, toggleTheme }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useTheme = () => {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
  return context;
};
