FILE: frontend/src/pages/AdminAnalyticsPage.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import api from '../services/api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import {
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  AreaChart,
  Area
} from 'recharts';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Users, 
  Map, 
  TrendingUp, 
  Wallet, 
  Download, 
  Loader, 
  ChevronRight,
  TrendingDown,
  Activity,
  Heart
} from 'lucide-react';

interface SummaryData {
  total_users: number;
  total_trips: number;
  completed_trips: number;
  avg_budget: number;
  avg_savings: number;
  popular_destination: string;
  popular_category: string;
  avg_comfort: number;
  top_destinations: { name: string; count: number }[];
  monthly_creation: { month: string; count: number }[];
  monthly_expenses: { month: string; amount: number }[];
}

export const AdminAnalyticsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  // All hooks MUST be declared before any conditional return (React Rules of Hooks)
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [data, setData] = useState<SummaryData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false);

  const fetchAnalytics = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get(`/api/admin/analytics?time_filter=${filter}`);
      setData(response.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load admin analytics:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    // Only fetch if the user is authenticated and is an admin
    if (user && user.is_admin) {
      fetchAnalytics();
    }
  }, [filter, user]);

  // Guard clause for admin authentication â€” placed AFTER all hook declarations
  if (!user || !user.is_admin) {
    return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />;
  }

  const handleCsvExport = async () => {
    try {
      setExporting(true);
      
      const downloadUrl = `${api.defaults.baseURL || ''}/api/admin/analytics/export-csv`;
      const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
      
      // Trigger browser download by opening the window
      if (token) {
        window.open(`${downloadUrl}?token=${token}`, '_blank');
      } else {
        window.open(downloadUrl, '_blank');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to generate CSV export.');
    } finally {
      setExporting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading && !data) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-24 text-slate-400 gap-3">
        <Loader className="animate-spin text-violet-500 h-8 w-8" />
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Compiling global travel reports...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <motion.div 
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: 'easeOut' }}
      className="space-y-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto pb-10"
    >
      
      {/* Header controls card */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShieldAlert size={20} className="text-violet-500" />
            Admin Analytics Control Dashboard
          </h2>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Evaluate global creation metrics, destinations popularity, and aggregate user savings
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Date Filter & CSV download row */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <select
            value={filter}
            onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
            aria-label="Filter timeline results"
            className="px-3.5 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 text-xs font-bold focus:outline-none dark:text-white"
          >
            <option value="all">All Time</option>
            <option value="6months">Last 6 Months</option>
            <option value="30days">Last 30 Days</option>
          </select>

          <button
            onClick={handleCsvExport}
            disabled={exporting}
            aria-label="Export reports as CSV spreadsheet"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-extrabold text-[10px] px-4.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl uppercase transition-all shadow-md shrink-0"
          >
            {exporting ? <Loader size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : <Download size={12} />}
            Export CSV
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Aggregate Stats Cards */}
      {data && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4 text-xs font-semibold">
          
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-1">
            <Users size={18} className="text-violet-500" />
            <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-405 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold block">Total Accounts</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white block">{data.total_users} Users</span>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-1">
            <Map size={18} className="text-sky-500" />
            <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-405 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold block">Total Trips Saved</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white block">
              {data.total_trips} Trips <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-500 font-bold">({data.completed_trips} Done)</span>
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-1">
            <Wallet size={18} className="text-emerald-500" />
            <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-405 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold block">Average Segment Budget</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white block">â‚¹{data.avg_budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-1">
            <TrendingUp size={18} className="text-rose-500" />
            <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-405 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold block">Average Savings</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-500 block">â‚¹{data.avg_savings.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>

        </div>
      )}

      {/* Visualizations Grid */}
      {data && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">

          {/* Monthly creations area chart */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Trip Creation Velocity</h3>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Global itinerary generation rates grouped by month</p>
            </div>
            <div className="h-64">
              {data.monthly_creation.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No trip history yet.</div>
              ) : (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <AreaChart data={data.monthly_creation}>
                    <defs>
                      <linearGradient id="colorCreation" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                        <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#7c3aed" stopOpacity={0.2}/>
                        <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#7c3aed" stopOpacity={0}/>
                      </linearGradient>
                    </defs>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#f1f5f9" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="month" stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={10} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={10} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                    <Tooltip contentStyle={{ fontSize: 11, borderRadius: 8 }} />
                    <Area type="monotone" dataKey="count" stroke="#7c3aed" fillOpacity={1} fill="url(#colorCreation)" strokeWidth={2} name="Trips Created" />
                  </AreaChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Monthly expenditures bar chart */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Monthly Expenditure Trajectory</h3>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Sum of aggregate actual logged expense costs by month</p>
            </div>
            <div className="h-64">
              {data.monthly_expenses.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No expenditures logged.</div>
              ) : (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={data.monthly_expenses}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#f1f5f9" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="month" stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={10} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={10} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                    <Tooltip contentStyle={{ fontSize: 11, borderRadius: 8 }} formatter={(v) => `â‚¹${Number(v).toLocaleString()}`} />
                    <Bar dataKey="amount" fill="#0ea5e9" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} name="Expense Spent" />
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Popular destinations list bar chart */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4 lg:col-span-2">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Popularity Matrix (Top 10 Locations)</h3>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Distribution count of total travel plans generated by destination</p>
            </div>
            <div className="h-72">
              {data.top_destinations.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No plans generated.</div>
              ) : (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={data.top_destinations} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 20 }}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" horizontal={false} stroke="#f1f5f9" />
                    <XAxis type="number" stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={10} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis dataKey="name" type="category" stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={10} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} width={80} />
                    <Tooltip contentStyle={{ fontSize: 11, borderRadius: 8 }} />
                    <Bar dataKey="count" fill="#10b981" radius={[0, 4, 4, 0]} name="Planned Count" />
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>
      )}

      {/* Overview stats table */}
      {data && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Overview Distributions</h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-6 text-xs bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 font-bold">
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Most Popular Target</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{data.popular_destination}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Most Popular Category</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{data.popular_category}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Average Comfort standard</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{data.avg_comfort}% Preference</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Total Trips Logged</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{data.total_trips} Plans</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </motion.div>
  );
};



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/AICompanion.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import {
  Sparkles,
  MessageSquare,
  Compass,
  Shuffle,
  HelpCircle,
  Loader,
  AlertCircle,
  Send,
  User,
  Bot,
  MapPin,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp
} from 'lucide-react';

interface SavedTrip {
  id: number;
  destination: {
    name: string;
  };
  budget: number;
  total_estimated_cost: number;
}

export const AICompanion: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'chat' | 'planner' | 'regenerator' | 'explain' | 'multistop'>('chat');
  const [savedTrips, setSavedTrips] = useState<SavedTrip[]>([]);

  // Error overlay popup state
  const [showAIErrorModal, setShowAIErrorModal] = useState(false);
  const [modalMessage, setModalMessage] = useState('');

  // 1. Chat Assistant states
  const [chatMessages, setChatMessages] = useState<Array<{ sender: 'user' | 'bot'; text: string }>>([
    { sender: 'bot', text: 'Hello! I am your BudgetBound AI Companion. Ask me anything about planning trips, local cuisines, or saving tips!' }
  ]);
  const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState('');
  const [chatLoading, setChatLoading] = useState(false);

  // 2. Personalized Planner states
  const [prefKeywords, setPrefKeywords] = useState('');
  const [prefBudget, setPrefBudget] = useState(25000);
  const [prefDays, setPrefDays] = useState(5);
  const [prefSpecial, setPrefSpecial] = useState('');
  const [planningLoading, setPlanningLoading] = useState(false);
  const [plannedResult, setPlannedResult] = useState<any | null>(null);

  // 3. Trip Regenerator states
  const [regenTripId, setRegenTripId] = useState('');
  const [regenInstruction, setRegenInstruction] = useState('');
  const [regeneratingLoading, setRegeneratingLoading] = useState(false);
  const [regeneratedResult, setRegeneratedResult] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [applyingRegen, setApplyingRegen] = useState(false);

  // 4. Explainability states
  const [explainTripId, setExplainTripId] = useState('');
  const [explainingLoading, setExplainingLoading] = useState(false);
  const [explanationText, setExplanationText] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Fetch saved trips for dropdowns on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchTrips = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/api/trips/');
        setSavedTrips(response.data);
        if (response.data.length > 0) {
          setRegenTripId(response.data[0].id.toString());
          setExplainTripId(response.data[0].id.toString());
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Failed to load user trips", e);
      }
    };

    fetchTrips();
  }, []);

  const triggerAIError = (message: string) => {
    setModalMessage(message);
    setShowAIErrorModal(true);
  };

  // Submit Handlers (All capture service unavailable messages from API)
  const handleChatSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!chatInput.trim() || chatLoading) return;

    const userMsg = chatInput;
    const updatedMessages: Array<{ sender: 'user' | 'bot'; text: string }> = [...chatMessages, { sender: 'user', text: userMsg }];
    setChatMessages(updatedMessages);
    setChatInput('');
    setChatLoading(true);

    try {
      const apiMessages = updatedMessages.map(msg => ({
        role: msg.sender === 'user' ? 'user' : 'assistant',
        content: msg.text
      }));
      const response = await api.post('/api/ai/chat', { messages: apiMessages });
      setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, { sender: 'bot', text: response.data.reply }]);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      const errMsg = err.response?.data?.detail || "AI service not connected yet. This feature will be available after API integration.";
      setChatMessages(prev => [...prev, { sender: 'bot', text: errMsg }]);
    } finally {
      setChatLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePlannerSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPlanningLoading(true);
    setPlannedResult(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.post('/api/ai/personalized-plan', {
        preferences: {
          keywords: prefKeywords,
          budget: prefBudget,
          days: prefDays,
          special: prefSpecial
        }
      });
      setPlannedResult(response.data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      triggerAIError(err.response?.data?.detail || "AI service not connected yet. API integration pending.");
    } finally {
      setPlanningLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRegenerateSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!regenTripId) return;
    setRegeneratingLoading(true);
    setRegeneratedResult(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.post('/api/ai/regenerate', {
        trip_id: Number(regenTripId),
        instruction: regenInstruction
      });
      setRegeneratedResult(response.data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      triggerAIError(err.response?.data?.detail || "AI service not connected yet. API integration pending.");
    } finally {
      setRegeneratingLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleApplyRegen = async () => {
    if (!regenTripId || !regeneratedResult) return;
    setApplyingRegen(true);
    try {
      await api.put(`/api/trips/${regenTripId}`, regeneratedResult);
      alert('Trip schedule and budget regenerated successfully!');
      setRegeneratedResult(null);
      setRegenInstruction('');
      navigate(`/expenses/${regenTripId}`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to apply regeneration update.');
    } finally {
      setApplyingRegen(false);
    }
  };

  const handleExplainSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!explainTripId) return;
    setExplainingLoading(true);
    setExplanationText(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.post('/api/ai/explain', {
        trip_id: Number(explainTripId)
      });
      setExplanationText(response.data.explanation);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      triggerAIError(err.response?.data?.detail || "AI service not connected yet. API integration pending.");
    } finally {
      setExplainingLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 relative">

      {/* Title */}
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
          <Sparkles className="text-violet-600" />
          AI Copilot Workspace
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          Supercharge your travel planning with our LLM modules. Explore personalized planners, chat buddies, and itinerary explainers.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Tabs Row */}
      <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 overflow-x-auto gap-4 scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-slate-200">

        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('chat')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border-b-2 transition-all focus:outline-none shrink-0 ${activeTab === 'chat'
            ? 'border-violet-600 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 dark:border-violet-400'
            : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
        >
          <MessageSquare size={16} />
          AI Chat Assistant
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('planner')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border-b-2 transition-all focus:outline-none shrink-0 ${activeTab === 'planner'
            ? 'border-violet-600 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 dark:border-violet-400'
            : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
        >
          <Compass size={16} />
          AI Personalized Planner
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('regenerator')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border-b-2 transition-all focus:outline-none shrink-0 ${activeTab === 'regenerator'
            ? 'border-violet-600 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 dark:border-violet-400'
            : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
        >
          <Shuffle size={16} />
          AI Trip Regenerator
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('explain')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border-b-2 transition-all focus:outline-none shrink-0 ${activeTab === 'explain'
            ? 'border-violet-600 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 dark:border-violet-400'
            : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
        >
          <HelpCircle size={16} />
          AI Explainability
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Tab Panels */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm min-h-[400px] flex flex-col">

        {/* Panel 1: Chat Assistant */}
        {activeTab === 'chat' && (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between h-[500px]">
            {/* Messages Display */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-4 mb-4 pr-2 text-xs no-scrollbar">
              {chatMessages.map((msg, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className={`flex items-start gap-3 max-w-[80%] ${msg.sender === 'user' ? 'ml-auto flex-row-reverse' : 'mr-auto'
                    }`}
                >
                  <div className={`h-8 w-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-bold ${msg.sender === 'user'
                    ? 'bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400'
                    }`}>
                    {msg.sender === 'user' ? <User size={14} /> : <Bot size={14} />}
                  </div>

                  <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl leading-relaxed border ${msg.sender === 'user'
                    ? 'bg-violet-600 border-violet-600 text-white rounded-tr-none'
                    : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/60 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 rounded-tl-none'
                    }`}>
                    {msg.text}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}

              {chatLoading && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mr-auto max-w-[80%]">
                  <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/60 text-slate-400 rounded-tl-none">
                    Analyzing routes...
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Input Bar */}
            <form onSubmit={handleChatSubmit} className="border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pt-4 flex gap-3">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={chatInput}
                onChange={(e) => setChatInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ask about destination recommendations, hotel buffers or general hacks..."
                className="flex-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3.5 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={chatLoading}
                className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white p-3.5 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10 shrink-0"
              >
                <Send size={16} />
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Panel 2: Personalized Planner */}
        {activeTab === 'planner' && (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col max-w-2xl mx-auto w-full py-4 justify-center">
            <div className="text-center mb-8">
              <Compass className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 mx-auto mb-2 animate-bounce" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Custom OpenAI Personalized Planner</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto mt-1 leading-normal">
                Bypass database seeding limits and ask our AI to create custom schedules for anywhere in the world.
              </p>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handlePlannerSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Destination Stays / Keywords</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={prefKeywords}
                  onChange={(e) => setPrefKeywords(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Kashmir, Backwaters, Ladakh glaciers"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Max Budget (INR)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    value={prefBudget}
                    onChange={(e) => setPrefBudget(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Days Limit</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    value={prefDays}
                    onChange={(e) => setPrefDays(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Special Stays / Dietary Needs</label>
                <textarea
                  value={prefSpecial}
                  onChange={(e) => setPrefSpecial(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Vegetarian food only, wheelchair accessible paths, focus on history museums."
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 resize-none leading-relaxed"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={planningLoading}
                className="w-full mt-2 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10"
              >
                {planningLoading ? <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                Generate Custom Itinerary
              </button>
            </form>

            {plannedResult && (
              <div className="mt-8 border-t border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700/60 pt-6 animate-fade-in">
                <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/30 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6">
                  <h4 className="text-lg font-bold text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 mb-1">{plannedResult.plan_name}</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-4">Estimated Total Expense: <strong className="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">â‚¹{plannedResult.estimated_cost?.toLocaleString()}</strong></p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-350 leading-relaxed mb-6 bg-white dark:bg-slate-850 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700">{plannedResult.summary}</p>

                  <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">Planned Schedule</h5>
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    {plannedResult.days?.map((day: any) => (
                      <div key={day.day_number} className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-850 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-4">
                        <div className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-805 dark:text-slate-200 mb-2 pb-1 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                          Day {day.day_number} - {day.description}
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-2">
                          {day.activities?.map((act: any, aIdx: number) => (
                            <div key={aIdx} className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                              <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold w-16">{act.time}</span>
                              <span className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 flex-1 px-2">{act.title}</span>
                              <span className="text-slate-500 font-bold">â‚¹{act.cost}</span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Panel 3: Trip Regenerator */}
        {activeTab === 'regenerator' && (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col max-w-2xl mx-auto w-full py-4 justify-center">
            <div className="text-center mb-8">
              <Shuffle className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 mx-auto mb-2 animate-spin-slow" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">AI Trip Regenerator</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto mt-1 leading-normal">
                Optimize or completely reschedule an existing trip using natural language commands.
              </p>
            </div>

            {savedTrips.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-6 text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/35 border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-6">
                No trips logged in history. Plan a trip before simulating regeneration.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleRegenerateSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Select Target Trip</label>
                  <select
                    value={regenTripId}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegenTripId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 appearance-none"
                  >
                    {savedTrips.map(t => (
                      <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                        {t.destination.name} (Budget: â‚¹{t.budget.toLocaleString()} â€” Cost: â‚¹{t.total_estimated_cost.toLocaleString()})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Regeneration Prompt / Commands</label>
                  <textarea
                    required
                    value={regenInstruction}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegenInstruction(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Make it nature-heavy. Replace flights with trains to save money. Add a free trekking activity on Day 3."
                    rows={4}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 resize-none leading-relaxed"
                  />
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={regeneratingLoading}
                  className="w-full mt-2 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10"
                >
                  {regeneratingLoading ? <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                  Regenerate Trip Schedule
                </button>
              </form>
            )}

            {regeneratedResult && (
              <div className="mt-8 border-t border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700/60 pt-6 animate-fade-in font-sans">
                <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/30 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6">
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-4">Proposed Itinerary & Allocation Changes</h4>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-6 bg-white dark:bg-slate-850 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Estimated Accommodation</p>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">â‚¹{regeneratedResult.costs?.hotel_cost?.toLocaleString()} ({regeneratedResult.costs?.hotel_tier})</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Estimated Transit</p>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">â‚¹{regeneratedResult.costs?.transport_cost?.toLocaleString()} ({regeneratedResult.costs?.transport_mode})</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Activity Cost</p>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">â‚¹{regeneratedResult.costs?.activities_cost?.toLocaleString()}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">New Total Cost</p>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400">â‚¹{regeneratedResult.costs?.total_estimated_cost?.toLocaleString()}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <h5 className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-3">New Proposed Itinerary Days</h5>
                  <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
                    {regeneratedResult.itineraries?.map((day: any) => (
                      <div key={day.day_number} className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-850 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-4">
                        <div className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-850 dark:text-slate-200 mb-2 pb-1 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                          Day {day.day_number} - {day.description}
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-2">
                          {day.activities?.map((act: any, aIdx: number) => (
                            <div key={aIdx} className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                              <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold w-16">{act.time}</span>
                              <span className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-350 flex-1 px-2">{act.title}</span>
                              <span className="text-slate-505 font-bold">â‚¹{act.cost}</span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={handleApplyRegen}
                    disabled={applyingRegen}
                    className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/10"
                  >
                    {applyingRegen ? <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                    Apply Regeneration to Trip
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Panel 4: Explainability */}
        {activeTab === 'explain' && (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col max-w-2xl mx-auto w-full py-4 justify-center">
            <div className="text-center mb-8">
              <HelpCircle className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 mx-auto mb-2 animate-pulse" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">AI Itinerary Explainability</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto mt-1 leading-normal">
                Want to know why the engine selected a certain resort tier, dining budget or local transit mode? Ask our AI for transparency.
              </p>
            </div>

            {savedTrips.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-6 text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/35 border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-6">
                No trips logged in history. Plan a trip before simulating budget explanation.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleExplainSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-2">Select Trip to Analyze</label>
                  <select
                    value={explainTripId}
                    onChange={(e) => setExplainTripId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 appearance-none"
                  >
                    {savedTrips.map(t => (
                      <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                        {t.destination.name} (Budget: â‚¹{t.budget.toLocaleString()})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={explainingLoading}
                  className="w-full mt-2 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10"
                >
                  {explainingLoading ? <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                  Analyze Budget Selections
                </button>
              </form>
            )}

            {explanationText && (
              <div className="mt-8 border-t border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700/60 pt-6 animate-fade-in font-sans">
                <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/30 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6">
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-4">Budget Selection Analysis</h4>
                  <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap font-sans">
                    {explanationText}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

      </div>

      {/* Floating Error Dialog Overlay for AI integration placeholders */}
      {showAIErrorModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-6 z-50 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 shadow-2xl relative text-center">

            <AlertCircle className="h-12 w-12 text-rose-500 mx-auto mb-4 animate-bounce" />

            <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-2">API Connection Pending</h3>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-6">
              {modalMessage}
            </p>

            <button
              onClick={() => setShowAIErrorModal(false)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all"
            >
              Acknowledge
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/BudgetOptimizer.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  TrendingDown, 
  Check, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  Loader, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Award,
  ChevronRight
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Allocation {
  transport_cost: number;
  hotel_cost: number;
  food_cost: number;
  activities_cost: number;
  emergency_reserve: number;
  local_transport_cost: number;
  taxes_fees_cost: number;
}

interface OptimizeOption {
  action_key: string;
  label: string;
  cost_difference: number;
  new_allocation: Allocation;
  description: string;
}

interface OptimizeResponse {
  current_cost: number;
  budget: number;
  allocations: Allocation;
  options: OptimizeOption[];
}

interface TripInfo {
  destination: {
    name: string;
    state: string;
  };
}

export const BudgetOptimizer: React.FC = () => {
  const { tripId } = useParams<{ tripId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const [data, setData] = useState<OptimizeResponse | null>(null);
  const [trip, setTrip] = useState<TripInfo | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const [selectedOptionIdx, setSelectedOptionIdx] = useState<number>(0);
  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const [optRes, tripRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/optimize`),
        api.get(`/api/trips/${tripId}`)
      ]);
      setData(optRes.data);
      setTrip(tripRes.data);
      if (optRes.data.options.length > 0) {
        setSelectedOptionIdx(0);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Could not fetch optimization insights.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tripId) {
      fetchData();
    }
  }, [tripId]);

  const handleApplyOptimization = async () => {
    if (!data || data.options.length === 0 || applying) return;
    
    setApplying(true);
    const option = data.options[selectedOptionIdx];
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/optimize/apply`, {
        action_key: option.action_key
      });
      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        navigate('/dashboard');
      }, 2000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Failed to apply optimized changes.');
      setApplying(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 gap-4">
        <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 animate-spin" />
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Running budget simulations...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !data || !trip) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ShieldAlert size={24} />
          <h3 className="font-bold">Optimization Error</h3>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs">{error || 'Failed to load optimization metrics.'}</p>
        <Link to="/dashboard" className="text-xs font-bold underline mt-2 block">
          Return to Dashboard
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const hasOptions = data.options.length > 0;
  const currentOption = hasOptions ? data.options[selectedOptionIdx] : null;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <Link 
          to="/dashboard" 
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-violet-600 dark:hover:text-violet-400 uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors mb-2"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={14} />
          Back to Dashboard
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
          Budget Optimizer: {trip.destination.name}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          Analyze and adjust your trip parameters to save money or increase luxury dynamically.
        </p>
      </div>

      {success && (
        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-3 animate-pulse">
          <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          Optimization applied successfully! Redirecting to Dashboard...
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Overview Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        
        {/* Current Total */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">Current Total Cost</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{data.current_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-lg bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center">
            â‚¹
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Budget limit */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">Target Budget</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{data.budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 flex items-center justify-center">
            L
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Health status */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">Target Variance</span>
            <span className={`text-xl font-extrabold block ${data.current_cost <= data.budget ? 'text-emerald-500' : 'text-rose-500'}`}>
              {data.current_cost <= data.budget 
                ? `Under Budget by â‚¹${(data.budget - data.current_cost).toLocaleString()}` 
                : `Over Budget by â‚¹${(data.current_cost - data.budget).toLocaleString()}`}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {!hasOptions ? (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-3xl p-12 text-center shadow-sm flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <Award className="h-16 w-16 text-emerald-500 mb-4" />
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-2">Trip Highly Optimized</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-sm mb-6">
            Your current plan is already at budget limitations with sleeper transits and budget properties. No further optimizer guidelines are required.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
          
          {/* Optimization Options List */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-3">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Optimization Strategies</h3>
            
            {data.options.map((option, index) => {
              const isSelected = index === selectedOptionIdx;
              const isSaving = option.cost_difference < 0;
              return (
                <button
                  key={option.action_key}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedOptionIdx(index)}
                  className={`w-full text-left p-4 rounded-xl border transition-all flex items-center justify-between gap-4 ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-violet-600 border-violet-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-violet-600/10'
                      : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">{option.label}</span>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] ${isSelected ? 'text-violet-200' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>
                      {option.action_key.replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                    <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                        : isSaving ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500' : 'bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-500'
                    }`}>
                      {isSaving 
                        ? `-â‚¹${Math.abs(option.cost_difference).toLocaleString()}` 
                        : `+â‚¹${option.cost_difference.toLocaleString()}`}
                    </span>
                    <ChevronRight size={16} className={isSelected ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-400'} />
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Option Comparison details */}
          {currentOption && (
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-6">
              
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-2">{currentOption.label}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                  {currentOption.description}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Side by side comparison */}
              <div className="border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl overflow-hidden text-xs">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-12 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 p-3 font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
                  <div className="col-span-6">Category</div>
                  <div className="col-span-3 text-right">Current</div>
                  <div className="col-span-3 text-right text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400">Optimized</div>
                </div>

                <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-700/60 font-medium">
                  {/* Transportation */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Transportation</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.transport_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.transport_cost.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Accommodation */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Lodging (Hotel)</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.hotel_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.hotel_cost.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Food */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Food & Dining</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.food_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.food_cost.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Activities */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Activities</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.activities_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.activities_cost.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Local Trans */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Local Travel</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.local_transport_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.local_transport_cost.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Taxes */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Taxes & Fees</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.taxes_fees_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.taxes_fees_cost.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Emergency */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 items-center">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Emergency Reserve</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.allocations.emergency_reserve.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      â‚¹{currentOption.new_allocation.emergency_reserve.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Total */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-12 p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/30 items-center font-bold text-sm">
                    <div className="col-span-6 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">Total Simulated Cost</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right">â‚¹{data.current_cost.toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 text-right text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400">
                      â‚¹{
                        (
                          currentOption.new_allocation.transport_cost +
                          currentOption.new_allocation.hotel_cost +
                          currentOption.new_allocation.food_cost +
                          currentOption.new_allocation.activities_cost +
                          currentOption.new_allocation.local_transport_cost +
                          currentOption.new_allocation.taxes_fees_cost +
                          currentOption.new_allocation.emergency_reserve
                        ).toLocaleString()
                      }
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex gap-4 justify-between items-center border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pt-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <TrendingDown className="text-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {currentOption.cost_difference < 0 
                      ? `Applying will save INR ${Math.abs(currentOption.cost_difference).toLocaleString()}`
                      : `Applying will cost INR ${currentOption.cost_difference.toLocaleString()} more`}
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <button
                  onClick={handleApplyOptimization}
                  disabled={applying || success}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {applying ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                      Applying changes...
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Check size={14} />
                      Apply Strategy
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>

            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/BudgetPlanner.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import api from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { DocumentManager } from '../components/DocumentManager';
import { TripFlowGuide } from '../components/TripFlowGuide';
import {
  Compass,
  MapPin,
  Calendar,
  Users,
  Wallet,
  Sliders,
  Sparkles,
  Loader,
  Check,
  Download,
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowRight,
  Search,
  Star,
  Plane,
  Train,
  Building,
  Utensils,
  Camera,
  Percent,
  LifeBuoy,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Map,
  Share2,
  CloudSun,
  Leaf
} from 'lucide-react';
import { LocationPickerModal } from '../components/LocationPickerModal';
import { BudgetHealthMeter } from '../components/BudgetHealthMeter';
import { RecommendationExplainability } from '../components/RecommendationExplainability';
import { BudgetAllocationEditor } from '../components/BudgetAllocationEditor';
