import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';

import { BudgetHealthMeter } from '../components/BudgetHealthMeter';
import { 
  BarChart, 
  Bar, 
  XAxis, 
  YAxis, 
  CartesianGrid, 
  Tooltip, 
  ResponsiveContainer, 
  PieChart, 
  Pie, 
  Cell, 
  Legend 
} from 'recharts';
import {
  Wallet,
  Compass,
  TrendingUp,
  Trash2,
  ArrowRight,
  AlertCircle,
  Eye,
  PlusCircle,
  Percent,
  Loader,
  Download
} from 'lucide-react';

interface RecentTrip {
  id: number;
  destination: string;
  state: string;
  budget: number;
  estimated_cost: number;
  days: number;
  category: string;
  date: string;
  status?: string;
  start_date?: string;
}

interface BudgetUsagePoint {
  name: string;
  budget: number;
  estimated: number;
  actual: number;
}

interface CategoryExpensePoint {
  name: string;
  value: number;
}

interface DashboardStats {
  total_trips: number;
  planned_budget: number;
  actual_expenses: number;
  savings: number;
  trips_planned: number;
  trips_in_progress: number;
  trips_completed: number;
  trips_cancelled: number;
  trips_draft: number;
  recent_trips: RecentTrip[];
  upcoming_trips: RecentTrip[];
  budget_usage_chart: BudgetUsagePoint[];
  category_chart: CategoryExpensePoint[];
}

export const Dashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { darkMode } = useTheme();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [stats, setStats] = useState<DashboardStats | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [downloadingTripId, setDownloadingTripId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [latestTripStats, setLatestTripStats] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [latestTripDetails, setLatestTripDetails] = useState<any | null>(null);

  const fetchLatestTripData = async (tripId: number) => {
    try {
      const [detailsRes, statsRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.get(`/api/trips/${tripId}`),
        api.get(`/api/expenses/trip/${tripId}/stats`)
      ]);
      setLatestTripDetails(detailsRes.data);
      setLatestTripStats(statsRes.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to load latest trip details for dashboard forecast:", err);
    }
  };

  // Fetch Dashboard Stats on load
  const fetchStats = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get('/api/dashboard/stats');
      setStats(response.data);
      if (response.data.recent_trips && response.data.recent_trips.length > 0) {
        await fetchLatestTripData(response.data.recent_trips[0].id);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Failed to load dashboard metrics.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchStats();
  }, []);

  const handleDownloadPDF = async (tripId: number, destName: string) => {
    setDownloadingTripId(tripId);
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/api/trips/${tripId}/pdf`, {
        responseType: 'blob'
      });
      const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([response.data], { type: 'application/pdf' }));
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.setAttribute('download', `budgetbound_${destName.replace(/\s+/g, '_').toLowerCase()}.pdf`);
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.parentNode?.removeChild(link);
      window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to download PDF:', err);
    } finally {
      setDownloadingTripId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteTrip = async (tripId: number) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this trip? All logged expenses and itineraries will be permanently removed.')) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/trips/${tripId}`);
      // Refresh statistics
      fetchStats();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to delete trip.');
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[60vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 gap-4">
        <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 animate-spin" />
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Analyzing your travel dashboard...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !stats) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-3">
        <AlertCircle size={24} />
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-bold">Error loading dashboard</h3>
          <p className="text-xs mt-0.5">{error || 'Something went wrong.'}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Visual Colors for Charts
  const COLORS = ['#6d28d9', '#4f46e5', '#06b6d4', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#ef4444'];

  return (
    <motion.div 
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: 'easeOut' }}
      className="flex flex-col gap-8"
    >
      
      {/* Welcome header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Travel Hub</span>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-1">
            Welcome Back, {user?.name || 'Explorer'}!
          </h1>
        </div>
        <Link
          to="/planner"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/15"
        >
          <PlusCircle size={16} />
          Plan New Trip
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Core Stats Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">

        {/* Total Trips */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Total Trips</span>
            <h3 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{stats.total_trips}</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Compass size={24} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Planned Budget */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Allocated Budget</span>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">â‚¹{stats.planned_budget.toLocaleString()}</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Wallet size={24} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Actual Expenses */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Spent So Far</span>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">â‚¹{stats.actual_expenses.toLocaleString()}</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center">
            <TrendingUp size={24} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Savings */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Calculated Savings</span>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">â‚¹{stats.savings.toLocaleString()}</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Percent size={24} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Status Metrics Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-6">
        {/* Planned */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold block uppercase">Planned</span>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">{stats.trips_planned || 0}</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-950/40 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px]">
            PL
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* In Progress */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold block uppercase">In Progress</span>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">{stats.trips_in_progress || 0}</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px]">
            IP
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* Completed */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold block uppercase">Completed</span>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">{stats.trips_completed || 0}</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px]">
            CO
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* Cancelled */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold block uppercase">Cancelled</span>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">{stats.trips_cancelled || 0}</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px]">
            CA
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* Draft */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between col-span-2 lg:col-span-1">

            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold block uppercase">Drafts</span>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">{stats.trips_draft || 0}</h4>

            DR

        <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-3xl p-6 flex flex-col gap-6 shadow-sm animate-fade-in">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
            <h2 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider">
              Latest Travel Forecast: {latestTripDetails.destination.name}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Budget health and predictions for your upcoming trip</p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            <BudgetHealthMeter
              budget={latestTripDetails.budget}
              estimatedCost={latestTripDetails.total_estimated_cost}
              actualExpenses={latestTripStats.total_actual}
              className="w-full bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700"
            />
            
            {/* Predictions widget */}
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between gap-4">

                <h3 className="font-extrabold text-sm uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
                  Live Financial Forecast
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Real-time spend pacing and projections</p>
              </div>
              
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">

                  <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Remaining Budget</span>
                  <span className={`font-bold ${latestTripStats.remaining_budget >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-500' : 'text-rose-500'}`}>
                    â‚¹{latestTripStats.remaining_budget ? latestTripStats.remaining_budget.toLocaleString() : '0'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Current Burn Rate</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                    â‚¹{latestTripStats.daily_burn_rate ? latestTripStats.daily_burn_rate.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 1 }) : '0'}/day
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Projected Spend</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                    â‚¹{latestTripStats.projected_final_spend ? latestTripStats.projected_final_spend.toLocaleString() : '0'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Forecast Status</span>
                  <span className={`inline-flex items-center text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full mt-1 ${
                    latestTripStats.prediction_status === "Likely to stay within budget"
                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400'
                      : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400'
                  }`}>
                    {latestTripStats.prediction_status || 'Pacing okay'}
                  </span>

      {stats.total_trips === 0 ? (
        /* Empty State */

            {/* Chart 1: Budget Usage */}
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6">Budget vs Cost Comparison</h3>
              <div className="h-72 w-full text-xs">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={stats.budget_usage_chart} margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#e2e8f0" className="dark:stroke-slate-700" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="name" stroke="#94a3b8" />
                    <YAxis stroke="#94a3b8" />
                    <Tooltip
                      contentStyle={{
                        background: darkMode ? '#1e293b' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
                        border: darkMode ? '1px solid #475569' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        color: darkMode ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a',
                        borderRadius: '12px'
                      }}
                      itemStyle={{
                        color: darkMode ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a'
                      }}
                    />

                    <Bar name="Estimated Cost" dataKey="estimated" fill="#8b5cf6" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                    <Bar name="Actual Expenses" dataKey="actual" fill="#ef4444" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6">Recent Travel Plans</h3>

              <div className="overflow-x-auto">

                      <th className="pb-4">Est. Cost</th>

                      <th className="pb-4 text-right">Actions</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-700/60 font-medium">
                    {stats.recent_trips.map((trip) => (

                          <div className="flex flex-col">
                            <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{trip.destination}</span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{trip.state}</span>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-4 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">â‚¹{trip.budget.toLocaleString()}</td>
                        <td className="py-4 text-slate-900 dark:text-white font-semibold">â‚¹{trip.estimated_cost.toLocaleString()}</td>
                        <td className="py-4 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{trip.days} Days</td>
                        <td className="py-4">
                          <span className="bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-400 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                            {trip.category}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-4 text-right">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
                            <button
                              onClick={() => {
                                // Prefecture prefills to planner
                                sessionStorage.setItem('prefilled_planner', JSON.stringify({
                                  startingCity: 'Your City',
                                  budget: trip.budget,
                                  days: trip.days,
                                  travelType: trip.category,
                                  travelersCount: 1,
                                  destName: trip.destination
                                }));
                                navigate('/planner');
                              }}
                              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700/40 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 transition-all"
                              title="View Details / Re-simulate"
                            >
                              <Eye size={14} />
                            </button>

                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleDownloadPDF(trip.id, trip.destination)}
                              disabled={downloadingTripId === trip.id}
                              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 dark:hover:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
                              title="Download Itinerary PDF"
                            >
                              {downloadingTripId === trip.id ? (
                                <Loader size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                              ) : (
                                <Download size={14} />
                              )}
                            </button>

                            <button
                              onClick={() => navigate(`/expenses/${trip.id}`)}
                              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-violet-50 hover:bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/20 dark:hover:bg-violet-900/30 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 transition-all font-bold"
                              title="Expenses Tracker"
                            >
                              Track
                            </button>

                            <button
                              onClick={() => navigate(`/optimizer/${trip.id}`)}
                              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950/20 dark:hover:bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 transition-all font-bold"
                              title="Optimizer"
                            >
                              Optimize
                            </button>

                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleDeleteTrip(trip.id)}
                              className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/20 dark:hover:bg-rose-900/30 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 transition-all"
                              title="Delete Plan"
                            >
                              <Trash2 size={14} />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Right Panel: Expense Category Breakdown */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-4 flex flex-col gap-8">

            {/* Pie Chart: Expense Categories */}
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6">Expense Distribution</h3>

              <div className="h-64 w-full text-xs">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie
                      data={stats.category_chart}
                      cx="50%"
                      cy="50%"
                      innerRadius={60}
                      outerRadius={80}
                      paddingAngle={4}
                      dataKey="value"
                    >
                      {stats.category_chart.map((_, index) => (
                        <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={COLORS[index % COLORS.length]} />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip
                      contentStyle={{
                        background: darkMode ? '#1e293b' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
                        border: darkMode ? '1px solid #475569' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        color: darkMode ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a',
                        borderRadius: '12px'
                      }}
                      itemStyle={{
                        color: darkMode ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a'
                      }}
                      formatter={(value) => [`â‚¹${Number(value).toLocaleString()}`, 'Spent']}
                    />
                    <Legend verticalAlign="bottom" height={36} iconType="circle" />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>

              {/* Legend Summary */}
              {stats.actual_expenses > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 flex flex-col gap-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 uppercase">Expense Summary</span>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <span>Average trip spent:</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">â‚¹{(stats.actual_expenses / stats.total_trips).toFixed(0)}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Upcoming Trips List */}
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-4 uppercase tracking-wider">Upcoming Trips</h3>
              
              {(!stats.upcoming_trips || stats.upcoming_trips.length === 0) ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No upcoming planned trips found.</p>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                  {stats.upcoming_trips.map((trip) => (
                    <div 
                      key={trip.id} 
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/planner?tripId=${trip.id}`)}
                      className="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl flex justify-between items-center cursor-pointer transition-all hover:translate-x-1"
                    >
                      <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                        <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{trip.destination}</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                          {trip.start_date ? `Starts: ${trip.start_date}` : `${trip.days} Days / ${trip.category}`}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-right flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{trip.budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
                        <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/20 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-450 rounded">
                          {trip.status}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Quick Tips Box */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-950 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-md relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-24 h-24 bg-violet-600/20 rounded-full blur-xl pointer-events-none" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-3 text-violet-300 uppercase tracking-wide">
                <AlertCircle size={15} />
                Budget Planner Tip
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                Reduce expenses by sliding your comfort settings below 30. This switches transportation to sleeper rails and housing to locally hosted homestays, cutting baseline trip cost by over 45%.
              </p>
            </div>

          </div>

        </div>
      )}

    </motion.div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/DestinationDetail.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { TripFlowGuide } from '../components/TripFlowGuide';
import { 
  ArrowLeft, 
  MapPin, 
  Loader, 
  AlertCircle, 
  Compass,
  Plane,
  Building,
  Utensils,
  Camera,
  Calendar,
  CloudSun
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Destination {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  state: string;
  avg_travel_cost: number;
  avg_hotel_cost: number;
  avg_food_cost: number;
  avg_activity_cost: number;
  rating: number;
  category: string;
  best_season: string;
  description: string;
  attractions: string[];
  weather_intelligence?: {
    current_season: string;
    best_season: string;
    season_rating: string;
    crowd_level: string;
    temperature_range: string;
    rain_probability: string;
    recommendation: string;
  };
}

export const DestinationDetail: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const [dest, setDest] = useState<Destination | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchDetail = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        const response = await api.get(`/api/destinations/${id}`);
        setDest(response.data);
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error(err);
        setError('Failed to load destination profile details.');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    if (id) {
      fetchDetail();
    }
  }, [id]);

  const handlePlanHere = () => {
    if (!dest) return;
    
    // Set Prefilled session storage
    sessionStorage.setItem('prefilled_planner', JSON.stringify({
      startingCity: 'Your City',
      budget: 25000,
      days: 4,
      travelType: dest.category,
      travelersCount: 1,
      destName: dest.name,
      destinationId: dest.id,
      flowMode: 'plan'
    }));
    navigate('/planner');
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 gap-4">
        <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 animate-spin" />
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Retrieving destination records...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !dest) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertCircle size={24} />
          <h3 className="font-bold">Error loading profile</h3>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs">{error || 'Destination not found.'}</p>
        <Link to="/explorer" className="text-xs font-bold underline mt-2 block">
          Return to Explorer
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Generate a mock itinerary for details view
  const mockDays = [
    {
      day: 1,
      title: 'Arrival & Local Orientation',
      activities: [`Arrival at transit station, check-in to hotel`, `Orientation walk around the main city hub`, `Dinner at a highly rated local culinary spot`]
    },
    {
      day: 2,
      title: 'Sightseeing Highlights',
      activities: [`Guided exploration of ${dest.attractions[0] || 'popular sites'}`, `Lunch including traditional delicacies`, `Evening sunset viewpoint visit at ${dest.attractions[1] || 'scenic spots'}`]
    },
    {
      day: 3,
      title: 'Deeper Exploration',
      activities: [`Tour of the historic ${dest.attractions[2] || 'monumental areas'}`, `Short trek or adventure excursion`, `Leisure time for local shopping and street food tour`]
    },
    {
      day: 4,
      title: 'Souvenirs & Departure',
      activities: [`Morning walk & photography session`, `Last minute shopping at central bazaars`, `Checkout and board return transit`]
    }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">

      <TripFlowGuide
        eyebrow="One destination, one shortcut"
        title="This page is for inspecting a destination before you plan it"
        description="Read the average costs, attractions, and season info first. If it looks right, the button below sends those values to the Budget Planner so you can build a full itinerary around this destination type."
        ctaLabel="Send this to planner"
        steps={[
          { title: 'Review the destination', description: 'Check costs, attractions, and the best season before you commit.' },
          { title: 'Use the plan button', description: 'Plan Itinerary Here pre-fills the planner with this destination category.' },
          { title: 'Generate the trip', description: 'Adjust budget and duration, then let the planner build the final trip.' }
        ]}
      />
      
      {/* Header back */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <Link 
            to="/explorer" 
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-violet-600 dark:hover:text-violet-400 uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors mb-2"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={14} />
            Back to Explorer
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              {dest.name}
            </h1>
            <span className="bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-400 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase shrink-0">
              {dest.category}
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <MapPin size={14} />
            {dest.state}, India
          </span>
        </div>

        <button 
          onClick={handlePlanHere}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/15"
        >
          <Compass size={16} />
          Plan Itinerary Here
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Profile splits */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8">
        
        {/* Left Side: General profile, costs and attractions */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 flex flex-col gap-6">
          
          {/* Main Description */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-3">Overview</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm leading-relaxed mb-6">
              {dest.description}
            </p>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 p-3.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
              <CloudSun className="text-violet-500 shrink-0" size={18} />
              <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Best Season to Visit:</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-bold">{dest.best_season}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Average Baseline Costs Grid */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-4">Estimated Average Base Costs</h3>
            
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              
              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2">
                <Plane className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
                <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Transit Cost</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{dest.avg_travel_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <span className="text-[8px] text-slate-400">Round trip base</span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2">
                <Building className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
                <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Lodging Cost</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{dest.avg_hotel_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <span className="text-[8px] text-slate-400">Per night stay</span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2">
                <Utensils className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
                <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Food / Day</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{dest.avg_food_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <span className="text-[8px] text-slate-400">Meals & beverages</span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2">
                <Camera className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
                <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Activity Fare</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{dest.avg_activity_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <span className="text-[8px] text-slate-400">Average entry fee</span>
              </div>

            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Attractions */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-4">Curated Local Attractions</h3>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 font-medium">
              {dest.attractions.map((a, idx) => (
                <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs">
                  <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-md bg-violet-50 dark:bg-violet-950/40 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 shrink-0">
                    {idx + 1}
                  </div>
                  <span>{a}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Right Side: Sample Itinerary & Weather */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-6">

          {/* Weather Intelligence Widget */}
          {dest.weather_intelligence && (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">

                  Season Intelligence
                </h3>
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                  dest.weather_intelligence.season_rating === 'Excellent' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-200/30' :
                  dest.weather_intelligence.season_rating === 'Good' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-950/40 dark:text-blue-400 border border-blue-200/30' :
                  dest.weather_intelligence.season_rating === 'Fair' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-200/30' :
                  'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400 border border-rose-200/30'
                }`}>
                  {dest.weather_intelligence.season_rating}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 font-bold">
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Current Season</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{dest.weather_intelligence.current_season}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Temp Range</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{dest.weather_intelligence.temperature_range}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Crowd Density</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{dest.weather_intelligence.crowd_level}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Rain Chance</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{dest.weather_intelligence.rain_probability}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed italic border-l-2 border-violet-400 pl-3">
                {dest.weather_intelligence.recommendation}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Sample Itinerary */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-4 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Calendar size={18} className="text-violet-500" />
              Sample 4-Day Itinerary Plan
            </h3>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 text-xs">
              {mockDays.map((d) => (
                <div key={d.day} className="flex gap-4">
                  {/* Timeline bar */}
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center shrink-0">
                    <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-violet-600 text-white font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-[10px]">
                      D{d.day}
                    </div>
                    {d.day < 4 && <div className="w-0.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 flex-1 my-1" />}
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-2 pb-2">
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{d.title}</h4>
                    <ul className="list-disc pl-4 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 space-y-1.5">
                      {d.activities.map((act, actIdx) => (
                        <li key={actIdx} className="leading-relaxed">{act}</li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/DestinationExplorer.tsx
================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { TripFlowGuide } from '../components/TripFlowGuide';
import { 
  Map, 
  Search, 
  Star, 
  MapPin, 
  Loader, 
  AlertCircle,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Destination {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  state: string;
  avg_travel_cost: number;
  avg_hotel_cost: number;
  avg_food_cost: number;
  avg_activity_cost: number;
  rating: number;
  category: string;
  best_season: string;
  description: string;
  attractions: string[];
}

export const DestinationExplorer: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [destinations, setDestinations] = useState<Destination[]>([]);
  const [states, setStates] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<string[]>([]);
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Pagination states
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
  const limit = 9;

  // Filters
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('');
  const [state, setState] = useState('');
  const [maxBudget, setMaxBudget] = useState<number | ''>('');

  const fetchFilters = async () => {
    try {
      const [statesRes, catsRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.get('/api/destinations/states'),
        api.get('/api/destinations/categories')
      ]);
      setStates(statesRes.data);
      setCategories(catsRes.data);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to load filter choices", e);
    }
  };

  const fetchDestinations = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/api/destinations/', {
        params: {
          search: search || undefined,
          category: category || undefined,
          state: state || undefined,
          max_budget: maxBudget || undefined,
          page: page,
          limit: limit
        }
      });
      setDestinations(response.data.destinations);
      setTotal(response.data.total);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Failed to fetch destinations.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchFilters();
  }, []);

  // Reset page to 1 whenever filters change
  useEffect(() => {
    setPage(1);
  }, [search, category, state, maxBudget]);

  // Refetch when filters or page changes
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchDestinations();
  }, [search, category, state, maxBudget, page]);

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / limit) || 1;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      
      {/* Title */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <Map className="text-violet-600 animate-pulse" />
            Destination Explorer
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">
            Browse through our curated list of handpicked Indian destinations with average costs and attractions.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-violet-50 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-400 font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2 border border-violet-100 dark:border-violet-900/60 self-start sm:self-center">
          <span>Total Seeded:</span>
          <span className="bg-violet-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px]">{total}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <TripFlowGuide
        eyebrow="Browse the catalog first"
        title="Destination Explorer shows the seeded travel catalog"
        description="These are predefined destinations stored in the database. Use search and filters to explore what exists today, then open one destination or jump into planner from its detail page."
        ctaLabel="Open any destination"
        steps={[
          { title: 'Search the database', description: 'Filter by name, state, category, or cost to narrow the list.' },
          { title: 'Open the detail view', description: 'Read costs, attractions, and a sample itinerary for that destination.' },
          { title: 'Plan from here', description: 'Use the detail page to prefill the Budget Planner with the selected destination type.' }
        ]}
      />

      {/* Filters Dashboard */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-4 items-center">
          
          {/* Search */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-4 relative">
            <Search className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search destination, description or state..."
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-colors"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Category selection */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-3">
            <select
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white appearance-none"
            >
              <option value="">All Categories</option>
              {categories.map(c => (
                <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* State selection */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-3">
            <select
              value={state}
              onChange={(e) => setState(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white appearance-none"
            >
              <option value="">All States</option>
              {states.map(s => (
                <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Max Stay Cost per day */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2">
            <input 
              type="number"
              value={maxBudget}
              onChange={(e) => setMaxBudget(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
              placeholder="Max Stay Cost (Daily)"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-colors"
            />
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid List */}
      {loading && (
        <div className="min-h-[40vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 gap-4">
          <Loader className="h-8 w-8 text-violet-500 animate-spin" />
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Loading destinations...</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertCircle size={16} />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && destinations.length === 0 && (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl h-80 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 bg-slate-50/20 dark:bg-slate-800/10">
          <Map className="h-12 w-12 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mb-3" />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">No Matching Destinations</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs leading-normal">
            No Indian getaways fit your current search query. Try clearing your filters or widening search parameters.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && destinations.length > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* Destination Cards Grid */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {destinations.map((dest) => (
              <div 
                key={dest.id}
                onClick={() => navigate(`/explorer/${dest.id}`)}
                className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:border-violet-500/40 cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between transition-all duration-200 group"
              >
                
                {/* Top banner representing category */}
                <div className="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900/60 p-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {dest.category}
                  </span>
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-amber-500 text-xs">
                    <Star size={13} fill="currentColor" />
                    <span className="font-bold">{dest.rating}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Middle details */}
                <div className="p-5 flex-1 space-y-3">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-1">
                      <MapPin size={11} className="text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                      {dest.state}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white group-hover:text-violet-600 dark:group-hover:text-violet-400 transition-colors">
                      {dest.name}
                    </h3>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed">
                    {dest.description}
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* Pricing footprint */}
                <div className="px-5 py-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/20 flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block font-bold uppercase">Lodging / Night (Avg)</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{dest.avg_hotel_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block font-bold uppercase">Transit Fare (Avg)</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{dest.avg_travel_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Pagination Controls */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 pt-6">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
              Showing <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-white">{destinations.length}</span> of{' '}
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-white">{total}</span> destinations (Page {page} of {totalPages})
            </span>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => setPage(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, 1))}
                disabled={page === 1}
                className="p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/50 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={16} />
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => setPage(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, totalPages))}
                disabled={page === totalPages}
                className="p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/50 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all"
              >
                <ChevronRight size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/ExpenseTracker.tsx
