import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { 
  BarChart, 
  Bar, 
  XAxis, 
  YAxis, 
  CartesianGrid, 
  Tooltip, 
  Legend, 
  ResponsiveContainer 
} from 'recharts';
import { 
  ArrowLeft, 
  Plus, 
  Trash2, 
  Edit3, 
  Wallet, 
  TrendingDown, 
  TrendingUp, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Loader, 
  Calendar,
  Save,
  X
} from 'lucide-react';
import { BudgetHealthMeter } from '../components/BudgetHealthMeter';
import { BudgetAllocationEditor } from '../components/BudgetAllocationEditor';

interface Expense {
  id: number;
  trip_id: number;
  date: string;
  category: string;
  amount: number;
  notes: string;
}

interface CategoryDiff {
  category: string;
  estimated: number;
  actual: number;
  difference: number;
}

interface ExpenseStats {
  total_estimated: number;
  total_actual: number;
  savings: number;
  categories: CategoryDiff[];
  remaining_budget?: number;
  daily_burn_rate?: number;
  projected_final_spend?: number;
  prediction_status?: string;
  estimated_overspend?: number;
  estimated_savings?: number;
  budget?: number;
  days?: number;
}

interface TripDetails {
  id: number;
  destination: {
    name: string;
    state: string;
  };
  budget: number;
  total_estimated_cost: number;
  budget_allocation?: any;
}

export const ExpenseTracker: React.FC = () => {
  const { tripId } = useParams<{ tripId: string }>();
  const { darkMode } = useTheme();
  const [trip, setTrip] = useState<TripDetails | null>(null);
  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState<Expense[]>([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<ExpenseStats | null>(null);
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Form states for adding/editing expenses
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingExpenseId, setEditingExpenseId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('Food');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [savingAllocations, setSavingAllocations] = useState(false);

  const categories = ['Transport', 'Hotel', 'Food', 'Activities', 'Shopping', 'Other'];

  const handleAllocationSave = async (updated: any) => {
    if (!tripId) return;
    try {
      setSavingAllocations(true);
      await api.put(`/api/trips/${tripId}/allocations`, {
        transport_cost: updated.transport_cost,
        hotel_cost: updated.hotel_cost,
        food_cost: updated.food_cost,
        activities_cost: updated.activities_cost,
        emergency_reserve: updated.emergency_reserve
      });
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to save manual budget allocations.');
    } finally {
      setSavingAllocations(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAllocationReset = async () => {
    if (!tripId) return;
    try {
      setSavingAllocations(true);
      await api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/allocations/reset`);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to reset allocations.');
    } finally {
      setSavingAllocations(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const [tripRes, expenseRes, statsRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.get(`/api/trips/${tripId}`),
        api.get(`/api/expenses/trip/${tripId}`),
        api.get(`/api/expenses/trip/${tripId}/stats`)
      ]);
      setTrip(tripRes.data);
      setExpenses(expenseRes.data);
      setStats(statsRes.data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Failed to fetch expense details.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tripId) {
      fetchData();
    }
  }, [tripId]);

  const handleOpenAdd = () => {
    setEditingExpenseId(null);
    setDate(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
    setCategory('Food');
    setAmount(0);
    setNotes('');
    setFormError(null);
    setShowForm(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEdit = (exp: Expense) => {
    setEditingExpenseId(exp.id);
    setDate(exp.date);
    setCategory(exp.category);
    setAmount(exp.amount);
    setNotes(exp.notes || '');
    setFormError(null);
    setShowForm(true);
  };

  const handleSaveExpense = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);

    if (amount <= 0) {
      setFormError('Amount must be greater than 0.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const payload = { date, category, amount, notes };
      
      if (editingExpenseId) {
        await api.put(`/api/expenses/${editingExpenseId}`, payload);
      } else {
        await api.post(`/api/expenses/?trip_id=${tripId}`, payload);
      }
      
      setShowForm(false);
      // Reload stats & table
      fetchData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setFormError('Failed to save expense details.');
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteExpense = async (id: number) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this expense entry?')) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/expenses/${id}`);
      fetchData();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to delete expense entry.');
    }
  };

  if (loading && !trip) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 gap-4">
        <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 animate-spin" />
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Loading ledger details...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !trip || !stats) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-3">
        <AlertTriangle size={24} />
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-bold">Error loading expense ledger</h3>
          <p className="text-xs mt-0.5">{error || 'Trip details could not be retrieved.'}</p>
          <Link to="/dashboard" className="text-xs font-semibold underline mt-2 block">
            Return to Dashboard
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 relative">
      
      {/* Header Back navigation */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <Link 
            to="/dashboard" 
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-violet-600 dark:hover:text-violet-400 uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors mb-2"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={14} />
            Back to Dashboard
          </Link>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            Expense Tracker: {trip.destination.name}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            Log your expenditures and compare actual expenses against the initial budget plan.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button 
          onClick={handleOpenAdd}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/15"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Log Expense
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Summary Panel */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        
        {/* Allocated Budget */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Wallet size={22} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">Allocated Budget</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{trip.budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Spent Actual */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <TrendingUp size={22} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">Actual Total Spent</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{stats.total_actual.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Savings Remaining */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <TrendingDown size={22} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">Plan Remaining Savings</span>
            <span className={`text-xl font-extrabold block ${stats.savings >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-500' : 'text-rose-500'}`}>
              â‚¹{stats.savings.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Budget Health Meter & Live Predictions Panel */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <BudgetHealthMeter
          budget={trip.budget}
          estimatedCost={trip.total_estimated_cost}
          actualExpenses={stats.total_actual}
          className="w-full"
        />
        
        {/* Live Budget Predictions Card */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-sm uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
              Live Financial Forecast
            </h3>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Real-time spend pacing and projections</p>
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Remaining Budget</span>
              <span className={`font-bold ${(stats.remaining_budget ?? 0) >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-500' : 'text-rose-500'}`}>
                â‚¹{stats.remaining_budget ? stats.remaining_budget.toLocaleString() : '0'}
              </span>
            </div>
            
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Current Burn Rate</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                â‚¹{stats.daily_burn_rate ? stats.daily_burn_rate.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 1 }) : '0'}/day
              </span>
            </div>
            
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Projected Spend</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                â‚¹{stats.projected_final_spend ? stats.projected_final_spend.toLocaleString() : '0'}
              </span>
            </div>
            
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Forecast Status</span>
              <span className={`inline-flex items-center text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full mt-1 ${
                stats.prediction_status === "Likely to stay within budget"
                  ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400'
                  : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400'
              }`}>
                {stats.prediction_status || 'Pacing okay'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main split */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
        
        {/* Left Side: Table Ledger */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6">Expense Ledger</h2>

          {expenses.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="h-64 border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/10">
              <Calendar className="h-12 w-12 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mb-3 animate-pulse" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">No Entries Recorded</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs leading-normal">
                You haven't logged any live expenses for this trip. Click the "Log Expense" button to begin tracking.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    <th className="pb-4">Date</th>
                    <th className="pb-4">Category</th>
                    <th className="pb-4">Notes</th>
                    <th className="pb-4">Amount</th>
                    <th className="pb-4 text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-700/60 font-medium">
                  {expenses.map((exp) => (
                    <tr key={exp.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/50 dark:hover:bg-slate-900/30 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-4 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{exp.date}</td>
                      <td className="py-4">
                        <span className="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                          {exp.category}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-4 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-[150px] truncate" title={exp.notes}>
                        {exp.notes || 'â€”'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-4 text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-bold">â‚¹{exp.amount.toLocaleString()}</td>
                      <td className="py-4 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
                          <button 
                            onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(exp)}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-white transition-all"
                            title="Edit"
                          >
                            <Edit3 size={13} />
                          </button>
                          <button 
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteExpense(exp.id)}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/20 text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-500 dark:hover:text-rose-400 transition-all"
                            title="Delete"
                          >
                            <Trash2 size={13} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Right Side: Estimated vs Actual chart comparison */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-6">
          
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-6">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
                  {expenses.length === 0 ? 'Planned Budget Allocation' : 'Planned vs Actual Spend'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                  {expenses.length === 0
                    ? 'Log expenses to see actual vs planned comparison'
                    : 'Category-level comparison of estimated vs actual costs'}
                </p>
              </div>
              {expenses.length === 0 && (
                <span className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-700 dark:text-violet-400 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-violet-200 dark:border-violet-900/30">
                  Planned
                </span>
              )}
              {expenses.length > 0 && (
                <span className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/30">
                  Live
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            
            <div className="h-64 w-full text-xs">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <BarChart
                  data={stats.categories.filter(c => c.estimated > 0 || c.actual > 0)}
                  margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
                >
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#e2e8f0" className="dark:stroke-slate-700" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="category" stroke="#94a3b8" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
                  <YAxis stroke="#94a3b8" tick={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
                  <Tooltip 
                    contentStyle={{ 
                      background: darkMode ? '#1e293b' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)', 
                      border: darkMode ? '1px solid #475569' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                      color: darkMode ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a',
                      borderRadius: '12px' 
                    }} 
                    itemStyle={{ color: darkMode ? '#f8fafc' : '#0f172a' }}
                    formatter={(value: number) => [`â‚¹${Number(value).toLocaleString()}`, undefined]}
                  />
                  <Legend wrapperStyle={{ paddingTop: '10px', fontSize: '11px' }} />
                  <Bar name="Plan Estimate" dataKey="estimated" fill="#8b5cf6" radius={[3, 3, 0, 0]} />
                  {expenses.length > 0 && (
                    <Bar name="Actual Spent" dataKey="actual" fill="#ef4444" radius={[3, 3, 0, 0]} />
                  )}
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>

            {/* Difference Analysis â€” only shown when actual expenses exist */}
            {expenses.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 flex flex-col gap-3 text-xs">
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 uppercase block mb-1">Difference Analysis</span>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 font-medium">
                  {stats.categories.map((cat, idx) => {
                    const isSaved = cat.difference >= 0;
                    if (cat.estimated === 0 && cat.actual === 0) return null;
                    return (
                      <div key={idx} className="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        <span>{cat.category}</span>
                        <span className={isSaved ? 'text-emerald-500 font-bold' : 'text-rose-500 font-bold'}>
                          {isSaved ? `+â‚¹${cat.difference.toLocaleString()}` : `-â‚¹${Math.abs(cat.difference).toLocaleString()}`}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-400 text-center py-2">
                Log your first expense to see actual vs planned differences.
              </div>
            )}

          </div>

          {/* Manual Budget Allocation Editor â€” wrap in right column div */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-6">
            {trip.budget_allocation && (
              <BudgetAllocationEditor
                budget={trip.budget}
                initialAllocations={trip.budget_allocation}
                onSave={handleAllocationSave}
                onReset={handleAllocationReset}
                isSaving={savingAllocations}
              />
            )}
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Floating Add/Edit Modal */}
      {showForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-6 z-50 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
            <button 
              onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-white"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>

            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-6">
              {editingExpenseId ? 'Edit Expense Entry' : 'Log New Expense'}
            </h3>

            {formError && (
              <p className="mb-4 text-rose-500 text-xs font-semibold bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <AlertTriangle size={14} />
                {formError}
              </p>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleSaveExpense} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Transaction Date</label>
                <input 
                  type="date"
                  required
                  value={date}
                  onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Category</label>
                  <select 
                    value={category}
                    onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 appearance-none"
                  >
                    {categories.map(cat => (
                      <option key={cat} value={cat}>{cat}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Amount (INR)</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={1}
                    value={amount}
                    onChange={(e) => setAmount(Number(e.target.value))}
                    placeholder="1500"
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Description / Notes</label>
                <input 
                  type="text"
                  value={notes}
                  onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Dinner at beachfront shack"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex gap-3 justify-end mt-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pt-4">
                <button 
                  type="button" 
                  onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}
                  className="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 dark:text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-all"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button 
                  type="submit"
                  className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center gap-1 shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10"
                >
                  <Save size={14} />
                  Save Entry
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-8">

          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-lg bg-violet-600/10 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Compass size={24} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Reverse Search Engine</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm leading-relaxed">Submit your budget and receive the top 5 locations in India sorted by custom value and rating algorithms.</p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-lg bg-violet-600/10 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <ShieldCheck size={24} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Budget Optimizer</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm leading-relaxed">Identify expensive line items and switch from luxury stays or flights to save 30% to 50% instantly.</p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-lg bg-violet-600/10 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <MapPin size={24} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Daily Itinerary & PDF</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 text-sm leading-relaxed">Generate complete day-by-day sightseeing programs and download high-quality PDF reports for offline viewing.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Sample Plans Section */}
      <section className="bg-slate-100/50 dark:bg-slate-950/60 py-24 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 transition-colors">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between mb-16">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-4">Sample Trip Packages</h2>
              <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">Sneak peek at what BudgetBound creates for different categories.</p>
            </div>
            <Link to="/register" className="mt-4 md:mt-0 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 hover:text-violet-500 font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-2">
              Explore 50+ Indian Destinations
              <ArrowRight size={16} />
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {samplePlans.map((plan, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden group hover:border-slate-350 dark:hover:border-slate-700 transition-all duration-300 shadow-sm">
                <div className="h-48 overflow-hidden relative">
                  <img 
                    src={plan.img} 
                    alt={plan.name} 
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-all duration-500"
                  />
                  <span className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-violet-600 text-white text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                    {plan.tag}
                  </span>
                  <span className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur text-white text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md">
                    {plan.category}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="p-6">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-bold">{plan.state}</span>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-500">
                      <Star size={14} fill="currentColor" />
                      <span className="text-xs font-extrabold">{plan.rating}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-2">{plan.name}</h3>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pt-4 mt-4">
                    <span className="text-slate-550 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs">{plan.days} Days / Person</span>
                    <span className="text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 font-extrabold text-lg">{plan.cost}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

FILE: frontend/src/pages/Login.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { Wallet, AlertCircle, Loader, Sun, Moon } from 'lucide-react';

export const Login: React.FC = () => {
  const { login, error, clearError } = useAuth();
  const { darkMode, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLocalError(null);
    clearError();

    if (!email || !password) {
      setLocalError('Please fill in all fields.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await login(email, password);
      navigate('/dashboard');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center p-6 selection:bg-violet-500 selection:text-white transition-colors duration-200 relative">
      
      {/* Floating Theme Switcher */}
      <div className="absolute top-6 right-6 z-20">
        <button 
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          className="p-2.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-sm"
          title={darkMode ? "Switch to Light Mode" : "Switch to Dark Mode"}
        >
          {darkMode ? <Sun size={16} /> : <Moon size={16} />}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[400px] h-[400px] bg-violet-600/5 rounded-full blur-[80px] pointer-events-none" />
      
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md relative z-10">
        
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 mb-8 text-center">
          <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet className="h-8 w-8 text-violet-600" />
            <span className="font-bold text-2xl tracking-tight bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              BudgetBound
            </span>
          </Link>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">Plan trips that fit your wallet</p>
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6 text-center">Welcome Back</h2>

        {/* Errors */}
        {(error || localError) && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 text-xs flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <AlertCircle size={16} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{localError || error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-350 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Email Address</label>
            <input 
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="name@example.com"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-350 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Password</label>
            <input 
              type="password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={submitting}
            className="w-full mt-2 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-lg shadow-violet-600/20 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {submitting ? (
              <>
                <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                Signing in...
              </>
            ) : (
              'Sign In'
            )}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          New to BudgetBound?{' '}
          <Link to="/register" className="text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 hover:underline font-semibold">
            Create an Account
          </Link>
        </p>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/NotificationsPage.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import api from '../services/api';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { EmptyState, SkeletonList } from '../components/FeedbackStates';
import { 
  Bell, 
  Check, 
  Trash2, 
  Calendar, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Package, 
  Share2, 
  FileText, 
  CheckCircle,
  Loader,
  Clock
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Notification {
  id: number;
  type: string;
  message: string;
  is_read: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

export const NotificationsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [actioning, setActioning] = useState(false);

  const fetchNotifications = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get('/api/notifications');
      setNotifications(response.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load notifications:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNotifications();
  }, []);

  const handleMarkRead = async (id: number) => {
    try {
      const response = await api.put(`/api/notifications/${id}/read`);
      setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => n.id === id ? response.data : n));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkAllRead = async () => {
    try {
      setActioning(true);
      await api.post('/api/notifications/mark-all-read');
      setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => ({ ...n, is_read: true })));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setActioning(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: number) => {
    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/notifications/${id}`);
      setNotifications(prev => prev.filter(n => n.id !== id));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  const getNotificationIcon = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'Upcoming Trip':
      case 'Trip Reminder':
        return <Calendar className="text-sky-500" size={18} />;
      case 'Budget Warning':
      case 'Overspending Alert':
        return <AlertTriangle className="text-amber-500" size={18} />;
      case 'Packing Reminder':
        return <Package className="text-violet-500" size={18} />;
      case 'Checklist Completed':
        return <CheckCircle className="text-emerald-500" size={18} />;
      case 'Trip Shared Successfully':
        return <Share2 className="text-indigo-500" size={18} />;
      case 'Trip Exported':
        return <FileText className="text-teal-500" size={18} />;
      default:
        return <Bell className="text-slate-400" size={18} />;
    }
  };

  const getNotificationColorClass = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'Overspending Alert':
        return 'border-rose-100 dark:border-rose-900/10 bg-rose-50/20 dark:bg-rose-950/5';
      case 'Budget Warning':
        return 'border-amber-105 dark:border-amber-900/10 bg-amber-50/15 dark:bg-amber-950/5';
      case 'Checklist Completed':
        return 'border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/10 bg-emerald-50/10 dark:bg-emerald-950/5';
      default:
        return 'border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800';
    }
  };

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter(n => !n.is_read).length;

  return (
    <motion.div 
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: 'easeOut' }}
      className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6"
    >
      
      {/* Title Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <Bell size={20} className="text-violet-500" />
            BudgetBound Notification Center
          </h2>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Stay updated with your travel reminders, checklists, and expenditure warnings
          </p>
        </div>

        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <button
            onClick={handleMarkAllRead}
            disabled={actioning}
            className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-extrabold text-[10px] px-4.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl uppercase transition-all shadow-md flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
          >
            {actioning ? <Loader size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : <Check size={12} />}
            Mark All Read
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Notifications list */}
      {loading && notifications.length === 0 ? (
        <SkeletonList />
      ) : notifications.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Bell size={24} />}
          title="No Notifications"
          description="You are all caught up! There are no system alerts, checklist reminders, or overspending warnings."
          actionLabel="Refresh Notifications"
          onAction={fetchNotifications}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3.5">
          <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
            {notifications.map((notif) => (
              <motion.div 
                key={notif.id}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0, overflow: 'hidden' }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                className={`border p-4.5 rounded-2xl flex items-start gap-4 transition-all ${
                  notif.is_read 
                    ? 'bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/10 border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-450 dark:text-slate-400' 
                    : 'shadow-sm text-slate-750 dark:text-slate-250 font-medium'
                } ${getNotificationColorClass(notif.type)}`}
              >
                {/* Category indicator icon */}
                <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-750 border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  {getNotificationIcon(notif.type)}
                </div>

                {/* Message text content */}
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-[9px] tracking-wide text-slate-400 block">
                      {notif.type}
                    </span>
                    
                    {/* Unread circle badge */}
                    {!notif.is_read && (
                      <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-rose-500 mt-1 animate-pulse" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  
                  <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed">{notif.message}</p>
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-slate-400 pt-1 font-semibold">
                    <Clock size={10} />
                    <span>{new Date(notif.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    <span>{new Date(notif.created_at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Action buttons */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 self-center">
                  {!notif.is_read && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleMarkRead(notif.id)}
                      className="p-1.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/60 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 rounded-lg text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-350 transition-colors"
                      title="Mark Read"
                    >
                      <Check size={13} />
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleDelete(notif.id)}
                    className="p-1.5 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 hover:bg-rose-100 rounded-lg text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 transition-colors"
                    title="Delete Alert"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={13} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            ))}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      )}
    </motion.div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/Register.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { Wallet, AlertCircle, Loader, Sun, Moon } from 'lucide-react';

export const Register: React.FC = () => {
  const { register, error, clearError } = useAuth();
  const { darkMode, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLocalError(null);
    clearError();

    if (!name || !email || !password || !confirmPassword) {
      setLocalError('Please fill in all fields.');
      return;
    }

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setLocalError('Passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    if (password.length < 6) {
      setLocalError('Password must be at least 6 characters long.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await register(name, email, password);
      navigate('/dashboard');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center p-6 selection:bg-violet-500 selection:text-white transition-colors duration-200 relative">
      
      {/* Floating Theme Switcher */}
      <div className="absolute top-6 right-6 z-20">
        <button 
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          className="p-2.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors shadow-sm"
          title={darkMode ? "Switch to Light Mode" : "Switch to Dark Mode"}
        >
          {darkMode ? <Sun size={16} /> : <Moon size={16} />}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[400px] h-[400px] bg-violet-600/5 rounded-full blur-[80px] pointer-events-none" />
      
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md relative z-10">
        
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 mb-8 text-center">
          <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet className="h-8 w-8 text-violet-600" />
            <span className="font-bold text-2xl tracking-tight bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              BudgetBound
            </span>
          </Link>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">Plan trips that fit your wallet</p>
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6 text-center">Create Your Account</h2>

        {/* Errors */}
        {(error || localError) && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 text-xs flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <AlertCircle size={16} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{localError || error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-355 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Full Name</label>
            <input 
              type="text"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="John Doe"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-355 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Email Address</label>
            <input 
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="name@example.com"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-355 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Password</label>
            <input 
              type="password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢ (Min 6 chars)"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-355 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Confirm Password</label>
            <input 
              type="password"
              value={confirmPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={submitting}
            className="w-full mt-2 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-lg shadow-violet-600/20 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {submitting ? (
              <>
                <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                Creating Account...
              </>
            ) : (
              'Create Account'
            )}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-550 dark:text-slate-400">
          Already have an account?{' '}
          <Link to="/login" className="text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 hover:underline font-semibold">
            Sign In
          </Link>
        </p>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/ReverseSearch.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { TripFlowGuide } from '../components/TripFlowGuide';
import {
  Search,
  Wallet,
  Calendar,
  Compass,
  Loader,
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowRight,
  Star,
  Sliders,
  MapPin,
  Map
} from 'lucide-react';
import { LocationPickerModal } from '../components/LocationPickerModal';


interface DestinationResult {
  destination: {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    state: string;
    description: string;
    rating: number;
    category: string;
    best_season: string;
    attractions: string[];
    avg_travel_cost: number;
    avg_hotel_cost: number;
    avg_food_cost: number;
    avg_activity_cost: number;
  };
  estimated_cost: number;
  budget_fit: number;
  rating: number;
  value_score: number;
}

export const ReverseSearch: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [startingCity, setStartingCity] = useState('Bangalore');
  const [budget, setBudget] = useState<number>(25000);
  const [days, setDays] = useState<number>(5);
  const [travelType, setTravelType] = useState('Nature');
  const [travelersCount, setTravelersCount] = useState<number>(1);
  const [comfortScore, setComfortScore] = useState<number>(50);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<DestinationResult[]>([]);
  const [hasSearched, setHasSearched] = useState(false);
  const [isMapModalOpen, setIsMapModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const travelTypes = [
    'Nature', 'Adventure', 'Family', 'Religious',
    'Beach', 'City Exploration', 'Romantic', 'Solo'
  ];

  const handleSearch = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setResults([]);
    setHasSearched(false);

    if (!startingCity.trim()) {
      setError('Please enter a starting city.');
      return;
    }
    if (budget <= 0) {
      setError('Please enter a valid budget.');
      return;
    }
    if (days < 1 || days > 14) {
      setError('Duration must be between 1 and 14 days.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/api/trips/reverse/search', {
        params: {
          budget,
          days,
          travel_type: travelType,
          travelers_count: travelersCount,
          starting_city: startingCity,
          comfort_score: comfortScore
        }
      });
      setResults(response.data);
      setHasSearched(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Failed to fetch reverse search results.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePlanTrip = (destName: string) => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('prefilled_planner', JSON.stringify({
      startingCity,
      budget,
      days,
      travelType,
      travelersCount,
      comfortScore,
      destName
    }));
    navigate('/planner');
  };

  const getValueScoreColor = (score: number) => {
    if (score >= 80) return 'text-emerald-505 dark:text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20';
    if (score >= 50) return 'text-amber-505 dark:text-amber-400 bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20';
    return 'text-rose-505 dark:text-rose-400 bg-rose-500/10 border-rose-500/20';
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">

      {/* Title */}
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
          <Search className="text-violet-600" />
          Reverse Travel Search
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          Enter your budget constraints, starting city and comfort choices. We rank the entire destination database dynamically to match.
        </p>
      </div>

      <TripFlowGuide
        eyebrow="Best for users who are still deciding where to go"
        title="Use Reverse Search when you know your budget, but not the destination"
        description="This screen scans the destination database and ranks the best options for your budget. Pick one result, and it sends the same values into the planner so you can build the final itinerary."
        ctaLabel="Find matching destinations"
        steps={[
          { title: 'Set your limits', description: 'Tell the app your budget, starting city, days, and comfort level.' },
          { title: 'Compare top matches', description: 'The app ranks destinations by value score instead of locking you into one place.' },
          { title: 'Move to planner', description: 'Select a destination and continue into the Budget Planner to generate the full trip.' }
        ]}
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">

        {/* Form panel */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sliders className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
            Search Constraints
          </h2>

          <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Starting From</label>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <div className="relative flex-1">
                  <MapPin className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={startingCity}
                    onChange={(e) => setStartingCity(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Bangalore, Delhi"
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsMapModalOpen(true)}
                  className="bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 px-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center"
                  title="Pick on map"
                >
                  <Map size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Total Budget (INR)</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Wallet className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
                <input
                  type="number"
                  required
                  min={1000}
                  value={budget}
                  onChange={(e) => setBudget(Number(e.target.value))}
                  placeholder="25000"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Duration (Days)</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Calendar className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={1}
                    max={14}
                    value={days}
                    onChange={(e) => setDays(Number(e.target.value))}
                    placeholder="5"
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Travelers</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  required
                  min={1}
                  value={travelersCount}
                  onChange={(e) => setTravelersCount(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Trip Category</label>
              <select
                value={travelType}
                onChange={(e) => setTravelType(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 text-slate-900 dark:text-white appearance-none"
              >
                {travelTypes.map(t => (
                  <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                <span className="uppercase tracking-wider">Comfort vs Savings</span>
                <span className="text-violet-600 font-bold">{comfortScore}%</span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="range"
                min="0"
                max="100"
                value={comfortScore}
                onChange={(e) => setComfortScore(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-violet-600"
              />
              <div className="flex justify-between text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold mt-1 uppercase">
                <span>Save Money</span>
                <span>Max Comfort</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <p className="text-rose-500 text-xs font-medium bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 p-3 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertTriangle size={14} className="shrink-0" />
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Search size={16} />}
              Find Matching Trips
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Results panel */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-8 space-y-6">
          {loading && (
            <div className="border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl h-80 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800">
              <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 animate-spin mb-4" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-350">Scanning All Indian Gateways...</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-xs leading-normal">
                Applying weighted scoring model parameters against lodging tiers, regional multipliers and attractions...
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && !hasSearched && (
            <div className="border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl h-80 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 bg-slate-50/20 dark:bg-slate-800/10">
              <Compass className="h-12 w-12 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-655 mb-3 animate-pulse" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Set Constraints to Begin</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs leading-normal">
                Enter your total trip budget and location preferences on the left, and our scoring matrix will reveal the best getaways.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && hasSearched && results.length === 0 && (
            <div className="border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl h-80 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 bg-slate-50/20 dark:bg-slate-800/10">
              <AlertTriangle className="h-12 w-12 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-655 mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-655 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">No Matches Found</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs leading-normal">
                Try widening your budget limit or lowering travel days to discover suitable packages.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && hasSearched && results.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-550 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Top 5 Best Matches</h3>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                {results.map((res, index) => (
                  <div
                    key={res.destination.id}
                    className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-5 items-start justify-between relative group hover:border-violet-500/40 transition-colors"
                  >

                    {/* Left details */}
                    <div className="space-y-3 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
                        <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-violet-650/10 dark:bg-violet-950/40 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 font-extrabold text-[10px] flex items-center justify-center">
                          #{index + 1}
                        </div>
                        <h4 className="font-extrabold text-lg text-slate-900 dark:text-white group-hover:text-violet-600 dark:group-hover:text-violet-400 transition-colors">
                          {res.destination.name}
                        </h4>
                        <span className="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-550 dark:text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase">
                          {res.destination.category}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                        <MapPin size={11} className="text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                        {res.destination.state}
                      </span>

                      <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-xs leading-relaxed max-w-xl line-clamp-2">
                        {res.destination.description}
                      </p>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-655 dark:text-slate-400 pt-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-amber-500">
                          <Star size={13} fill="currentColor" />
                          <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{res.destination.rating}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span>Best season: {res.destination.best_season}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Right cost & action */}
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-start md:items-end gap-3 justify-between md:border-l md:border-slate-100 md:dark:border-slate-700/60 md:pl-6 shrink-0 w-full md:w-auto">
                      <div className="flex flex-col md:items-end">
                        <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Estimated Cost</span>
                        <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">â‚¹{res.estimated_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                        <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-medium">({res.budget_fit}% of budget)</span>
                      </div>

                      <div className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border text-[10px] font-bold uppercase ${getValueScoreColor(res.value_score)}`}>
                        Match Score: {res.value_score}
                      </div>

                      <button
                        onClick={() => handlePlanTrip(res.destination.name)}
                        className="w-full md:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-white font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all"
                      >
                        Select & Plan
                        <ArrowRight size={13} />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      <LocationPickerModal
        isOpen={isMapModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsMapModalOpen(false)}
        onSelectLocation={(city) => setStartingCity(city)}
        initialCity={startingCity}
      />
    </div>
  );
};
