FILE: frontend/src/pages/SavedTrips.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { 
  Search, 
  ArrowUpDown, 
  Briefcase, 
  Calendar, 
  Wallet, 
  TrendingUp, 
  TrendingDown, 
  Download, 
  Trash2, 
  Copy, 
  Edit3, 
  Eye, 
  AlertCircle, 
  Loader, 
  X,
  Compass,
  ArrowRight,
  Share2,
  CloudSun,
  Receipt
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ShareModal } from '../components/ShareModal';
import { EmptyState, SkeletonCard } from '../components/FeedbackStates';

interface Destination {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  state: string;
  category: string;
  weather_intelligence?: {
    current_season: string;
    season_rating: string;
  };
}

interface Trip {
  id: number;
  budget: number;
  days: number;
  travelers_count: number;
  travel_type: string;
  comfort_score: number;
  total_estimated_cost: number;
  status: string;
  start_date?: string;
  created_at: string;
  destination: Destination;
  actual_spending?: number;
  savings?: number;
  is_public?: boolean;
  share_token?: string | null;
}

export const SavedTrips: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [trips, setTrips] = useState<Trip[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Search & Filters state
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState('newest');

  // Edit Modal state
  const [editingTrip, setEditingTrip] = useState<Trip | null>(null);
  const [editBudget, setEditBudget] = useState(20000);
  const [editDays, setEditDays] = useState(4);
  const [editStartDate, setEditStartDate] = useState('');
  const [editStatus, setEditStatus] = useState('Planned');
  const [editTravelers, setEditTravelers] = useState(1);
  const [editType, setEditType] = useState('Nature');
  const [editComfort, setEditComfort] = useState(50);
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);
  const [activeShareTrip, setActiveShareTrip] = useState<Trip | null>(null);

  const fetchTrips = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get('/api/trips/');
      setTrips(response.data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError('Failed to fetch your saved trip logs.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTrips();
  }, []);

  const handleDelete = async (tripId: number) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Are you sure you want to permanently delete this travel plan? All logged expenses and itinerary logs will be deleted.')) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/trips/${tripId}`);
      setTrips(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== tripId));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to delete trip.');
    }
  };

  const handleDuplicate = async (tripId: number) => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      await api.post(`/api/trips/${tripId}/duplicate`);
      await fetchTrips();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to duplicate trip.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadPDF = (tripId: number, destName: string) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    window.open(`http://localhost:8000/api/trips/${tripId}/pdf?token=${token}`, '_blank');
  };

  const openEditModal = (trip: Trip) => {
    setEditingTrip(trip);
    setEditBudget(trip.budget);
    setEditDays(trip.days);
    setEditStartDate(trip.start_date || '');
    setEditStatus(trip.status);
    setEditTravelers(trip.travelers_count);
    setEditType(trip.travel_type);
    setEditComfort(trip.comfort_score);
  };

  const handleSaveEdit = async () => {
    if (!editingTrip) return;
    try {
      setUpdating(true);
      const response = await api.put(`/api/trips/${editingTrip.id}`, {
        budget: editBudget,
        days: editDays,
        start_date: editStartDate || null,
        status: editStatus,
        travelers_count: editTravelers,
        travel_type: editType,
        comfort_score: editComfort
      });
      
      // Update local state list
      setTrips(prev => prev.map(t => t.id === editingTrip.id ? response.data : t));
      setEditingTrip(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to update trip.');
    } finally {
      setUpdating(false);
    }
  };

  const getStatusBadgeClass = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'Draft':
        return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900/50 dark:text-slate-400 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800';
      case 'Planned':
        return 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-950/20 dark:text-blue-400 border border-blue-100 dark:border-blue-900/30';
      case 'In Progress':
        return 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950/20 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-900/30';
      case 'Completed':
        return 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/20 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/30';
      case 'Cancelled':
        return 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950/20 dark:text-rose-400 border border-rose-100 dark:border-rose-900/30';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700';
    }
  };

  // Filter & Sort computation
  const filteredTrips = trips.filter(trip => {
    const search = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
    return (
      trip.destination.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      trip.destination.state.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      trip.travel_type.toLowerCase().includes(search)
    );
  });

  const sortedTrips = [...filteredTrips].sort((a, b) => {
    switch (sortBy) {
      case 'newest':
        return new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime();
      case 'oldest':
        return new Date(a.created_at).getTime() - new Date(b.created_at).getTime();
      case 'highest_budget':
        return b.budget - a.budget;
      case 'lowest_budget':
        return a.budget - b.budget;
      case 'dest_name':
        return a.destination.name.localeCompare(b.destination.name);
      default:
        return 0;
    }
  });

  if (loading && trips.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto p-6 md:p-10 animate-pulse">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-2 w-1/3">
            <div className="h-4 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-md w-1/2" />
            <div className="h-6 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-md w-3/4" />
          </div>
          <div className="h-10 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-md w-1/4" />
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          <SkeletonCard />
          <SkeletonCard />
          <SkeletonCard />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <motion.div 
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: 'easeOut' }}
      className="space-y-8 relative"
    >
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Travel History</span>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-1">My Saved Trips</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            View, duplicate, edit, or track expenses for all your finalized travel plans.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Link 
          to="/planner"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/15"
        >
          Plan New Destination
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Search & Sort Panel */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 justify-between items-center">
        {/* Search */}
        <div className="relative w-full sm:max-w-xs">
          <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center text-slate-400 pointer-events-none">
            <Search size={16} />
          </span>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search by destination..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Sort dropdown */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 self-end sm:self-center">
          <ArrowUpDown size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={sortBy}
            onChange={(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs px-3 py-2 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-bold focus:outline-none transition-all"
          >
            <option value="newest">Sort: Newest</option>
            <option value="oldest">Sort: Oldest</option>
            <option value="highest_budget">Sort: Highest Budget</option>
            <option value="lowest_budget">Sort: Lowest Budget</option>
            <option value="dest_name">Sort: Destination Name</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Trips Grid list */}
      {sortedTrips.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Compass size={24} />}
          title="No Saved Trips Found"
          description="Create or generate budget-allocated schedules to populate this log ledger list. You can start planning or discovery from the Planner."
          actionLabel="Plan a Trip Now"
          onAction={() => navigate('/planner')}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {sortedTrips.map((trip) => {
            const actualSpent = trip.actual_spending || 0.0;
            const savings = trip.savings || 0.0;
            
            return (
              <div 
                key={trip.id} 
                className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
              >
                {/* Image / Destination details */}
                <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 flex justify-between items-start">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">{trip.destination.state}</span>
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">{trip.destination.name}</h3>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 items-center mt-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-semibold uppercase">
                        {trip.travel_type}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide">
                        {trip.days} Days / {trip.travelers_count} Travelers
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${getStatusBadgeClass(trip.status)}`}>
                    {trip.status}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Travel Date */}
                <div className="px-5 py-3.5 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/10 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <Calendar size={14} className="text-violet-500 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="font-medium">
                    {trip.start_date ? `Starts: ${trip.start_date}` : 'Planned dates not set'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Weather Quick Badge */}
                {trip.destination.weather_intelligence && (
                  <div className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50/20 dark:bg-slate-900/5 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CloudSun size={13} className="text-violet-500" />
                      Season: {trip.destination.weather_intelligence.current_season}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[9px] border ${
                      trip.destination.weather_intelligence.season_rating === 'Excellent' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 border-emerald-205' :
                      trip.destination.weather_intelligence.season_rating === 'Good' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-950/40 dark:text-blue-400 border-blue-205' :
                      trip.destination.weather_intelligence.season_rating === 'Fair' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400 border-amber-205' :
                      'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400 border-rose-205'
                    }`}>
                      {trip.destination.weather_intelligence.season_rating}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Costs Summary Grid */}
                <div className="p-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800">
                  <div className="space-y-0.5">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block font-semibold">Total Target Budget</span>
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{trip.budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-0.5">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block font-semibold">Estimated Cost</span>
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">â‚¹{trip.total_estimated_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-0.5">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block font-semibold">Actual Spending</span>
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-rose-500">â‚¹{actualSpent.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-0.5">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block font-semibold">Trip Savings</span>
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-emerald-500">â‚¹{savings.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Interactive Actions Footer */}
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/15 flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/planner?tripId=${trip.id}`)}
                      className="p-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-650"
                      title="View Planner Details"
                    >
                      <Eye size={13} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/expenses/${trip.id}`)}
                      className="p-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-650"
                      title="Manage Expenses"
                    >
                      <Receipt size={13} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => openEditModal(trip)}
                      className="p-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-650"
                      title="Edit Basic Metadata"
                    >
                      <Edit3 size={13} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleDuplicate(trip.id)}
                      className="p-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-650"
                      title="Duplicate Trip"
                    >
                      <Copy size={13} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setActiveShareTrip(trip)}
                      className="p-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-650"
                      title="Share Trip Plan"
                    >
                      <Share2 size={13} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleDownloadPDF(trip.id, trip.destination.name)}
                      className="p-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-white transition-all shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-650"
                      title="Download PDF"
                    >
                      <Download size={13} />
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleDelete(trip.id)}
                    className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/20 dark:hover:bg-rose-900/30 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 transition-all border border-rose-100/50 dark:border-rose-900/10"
                    title="Delete Plan"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={13} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Edit Trip Modal Popup */}
      {editingTrip && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-6 z-50 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
            <button 
              onClick={() => setEditingTrip(null)}
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-white focus:outline-none"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>

            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-white mb-4">
              Edit Trip: {editingTrip.destination.name}
            </h3>

            <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Target Budget (INR)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={editBudget}
                  onChange={(e) => setEditBudget(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Duration (Days)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={editDays}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditDays(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Travelers</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={editTravelers}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditTravelers(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Travel Date</label>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    value={editStartDate}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditStartDate(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Trip Lifecycle</label>
                  <select
                    value={editStatus}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditStatus(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none font-bold"
                  >
                    {["Draft", "Planned", "In Progress", "Completed", "Cancelled"].map((st) => (
                      <option key={st} value={st}>{st}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Travel Style</label>
                  <select
                    value={editType}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditType(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none font-bold"
                  >
                    {['Nature', 'Adventure', 'Family', 'Religious', 'Beach', 'City Exploration', 'Romantic', 'Solo'].map((st) => (
                      <option key={st} value={st}>{st}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-500 font-bold mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">Comfort Score (0-100)</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    max="100"
                    value={editComfort}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditComfort(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 mt-6">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setEditingTrip(null)}
                className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleSaveEdit}
                disabled={updating}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition-colors"
              >
                {updating ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Changes'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activeShareTrip && (
        <ShareModal
          tripId={activeShareTrip.id}
          isPublic={activeShareTrip.is_public || false}
          shareToken={activeShareTrip.share_token || null}
          onClose={() => setActiveShareTrip(null)}
          onUpdate={(updatedTrip) => {
            setTrips(prev => prev.map(t => t.id === activeShareTrip.id ? {
              ...t,
              is_public: updatedTrip.is_public,
              share_token: updatedTrip.share_token
            } : t));
            setActiveShareTrip(null);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </motion.div>
  );
};



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/SharedTripView.tsx
================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import api from '../services/api';
import { TimelineItinerary } from '../components/TimelineItinerary';
import { TripChecklist } from '../components/TripChecklist';
import { 
  Calendar, 
  MapPin, 
  Wallet, 
  Compass, 
  Loader, 
  AlertCircle, 
  Leaf, 
  CloudSun,
  Building,
  Plane,
  Train,
  Utensils,
  Camera,
  Percent,
  LifeBuoy
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Destination {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  state: string;
  category: string;
  description: string;
  best_season: string;
  weather_intelligence?: {
    current_season: string;
    best_season: string;
    season_rating: string;
    crowd_level: string;
    temperature_range: string;
    rain_probability: string;
    recommendation: string;
  };
}

interface BudgetAllocation {
  transport_cost: number;
  hotel_cost: number;
  food_cost: number;
  activities_cost: number;
  local_transport_cost: number;
  taxes_fees_cost: number;
  emergency_reserve: number;
}

interface CarbonFootprint {
  transport_mode: string;
  co2_emissions: number;
  carbon_rating: string;
  eco_score: number;
  alternative_savings: number;
}

interface Trip {
  id: number;
  budget: number;
  days: number;
  travelers_count: number;
  travel_type: string;
  comfort_score: number;
  total_estimated_cost: number;
  hotel_tier: string;
  transport_mode: string;
  status: string;
  start_date?: string;
  destination: Destination;
  itineraries: any[];
  budget_allocation?: BudgetAllocation;
  carbon_footprint?: CarbonFootprint;
  packing_items: any[];
}

export const SharedTripView: React.FC = () => {
  const { shareToken } = useParams<{ shareToken: string }>();
  const [trip, setTrip] = useState<Trip | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [expandedDays, setExpandedDays] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchSharedTrip = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        const response = await api.get(`/api/trips/share/${shareToken}`);
        setTrip(response.data);
        
        // Expand all days by default for public viewing
        const allExpanded: Record<number, boolean> = {};
        response.data.itineraries.forEach((it: any) => {
          allExpanded[it.day_number] = true;
        });
        setExpandedDays(allExpanded);
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error(err);
        setError(err.response?.data?.detail || 'This travel plan is set to private or does not exist.');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    if (shareToken) {
      fetchSharedTrip();
    }
  }, [shareToken]);

  const toggleDay = (dayNumber: number) => {
    setExpandedDays(prev => ({ ...prev, [dayNumber]: !prev[dayNumber] }));
  };

  const getCategoryGradient = (category: string) => {
    const cat = category.toLowerCase();
    if (cat.includes('beach')) {
      return 'from-amber-400 via-orange-500 to-blue-600';
    }
    if (cat.includes('nature') || cat.includes('hills')) {
      return 'from-emerald-400 via-teal-500 to-indigo-650';
    }
    if (cat.includes('religious') || cat.includes('temple')) {
      return 'from-orange-400 via-red-500 to-amber-600';
    }
    return 'from-violet-500 via-purple-600 to-indigo-700';
  };

  const getWeatherRatingBadgeClass = (rating: string) => {
    switch (rating) {
      case 'Excellent':
        return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900/30';
      case 'Good':
        return 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-950/40 dark:text-blue-400 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-900/30';
      case 'Fair':
        return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/30';
      case 'Avoid':
        return 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/30';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700';
    }
  };

  const getCarbonBadgeClass = (rating: string) => {
    if (rating === 'Eco Friendly') {
      return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-250';
    }
    if (rating === 'Moderate') {
      return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-250';
    }
    return 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400 border border-rose-250';
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-500 gap-4">
        <Loader className="h-10 w-10 text-violet-500 animate-spin" />
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Retrieving shared travel plan...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !trip) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center">
        <div className="max-w-md bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-xl space-y-4">
          <AlertCircle className="h-14 w-14 text-rose-500 mx-auto" />
          <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Plan Access Denied</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            {error || 'This itinerary is currently private. Please ask the owner to enable public sharing.'}
          </p>
          <div className="pt-2">
            <Link 
              to="/dashboard" 
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-bold px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition-all shadow-md"
            >
              Back to Dashboard
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const { destination } = trip;
  const weather = destination.weather_intelligence;
  const carbon = trip.carbon_footprint;
  const alloc = trip.budget_allocation;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-850 dark:text-slate-100 flex flex-col items-center pb-12">
      
      {/* Category Gradient Header Banner */}
      <div className={`w-full h-64 bg-gradient-to-r ${getCategoryGradient(destination.category)} relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-end p-8 text-white shadow`}>
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-80 h-80 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="max-w-5xl w-full mx-auto space-y-2 relative z-10">
          <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-md px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase font-bold tracking-wider">
              {destination.category}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-violet-600/60 backdrop-blur-md px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase font-bold tracking-wider">
              Public Shared Plan
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-200 block">{destination.state}</span>
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-extrabold tracking-tight drop-shadow-md">{destination.name}</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-100/90 max-w-xl font-medium leading-relaxed drop-shadow-sm">
            {destination.description}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Core Container */}
      <div className="max-w-5xl w-full px-6 md:px-8 mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8">
        
        {/* Left column: Itinerary */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
          
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
              <Compass className="text-violet-500" size={18} />
              Travel Schedule Itinerary
            </h2>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Explore the daily chronological travel agenda details</p>
          </div>

          <TimelineItinerary
            itineraries={trip.itineraries}
            expandedDays={expandedDays}
            toggleDay={toggleDay}
            className="w-full animate-fade-in"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Right column: Widgets (Cost, Weather, Packing, Carbon) */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Metadata Card */}
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-center">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Budget</span>
              <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{trip.budget.toLocaleString()}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1 border-x border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Days</span>
              <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{trip.days} Days</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Travelers</span>
              <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{trip.travelers_count} People</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Weather Intelligence Widget */}
          {weather && (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                  <CloudSun size={16} className="text-violet-500" />
                  Weather & Season Intelligence
                </h3>
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${getWeatherRatingBadgeClass(weather.season_rating)}`}>
                  {weather.season_rating}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/40 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold block">Current Season</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{weather.current_season}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold block">Temp Range</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{weather.temperature_range}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold block">Crowd Level</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{weather.crowd_level}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold block">Rain Probability</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">{weather.rain_probability}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed italic border-l-2 border-violet-400 pl-3">
                {weather.recommendation}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Carbon Footprint Widget */}
          {carbon && (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Leaf size={16} className="text-emerald-500" />
                  Carbon Footprint Impact
                </h3>
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${getCarbonBadgeClass(carbon.carbon_rating)}`}>
                  {carbon.carbon_rating}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  {carbon.transport_mode === 'Flight' ? <Plane size={16} className="text-rose-500" /> : <Train size={16} className="text-emerald-500" />}
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-750 dark:text-slate-250">
                    COâ‚‚ Impact ({carbon.transport_mode})
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  {carbon.co2_emissions} kg
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-normal flex items-start gap-2 bg-emerald-50/20 dark:bg-emerald-950/10 border border-emerald-100/40 p-3 rounded-xl">
                <Leaf size={14} className="text-emerald-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" fill="currentColor" />
                <span>
                  {carbon.transport_mode === 'Flight' 
                    ? `Choosing train instead of flight for this segment reduces emissions by ${carbon.alternative_savings} kg.`
                    : `Eco-friendly choice! Traveling by train instead of flight saved an estimated ${carbon.alternative_savings} kg of COâ‚‚.`
                  }
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Budget Breakdown Summary */}
          {alloc && (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm space-y-4">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                <Wallet size={16} className="text-violet-500" />
                Allocated Budget Breakdown
              </h3>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-xs text-slate-650 dark:text-slate-350">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Plane size={14} /> Transportation</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{alloc.transport_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Building size={14} /> Accommodations</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{alloc.hotel_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Utensils size={14} /> Food & Dining</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{alloc.food_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Camera size={14} /> Sightseeing / Sights</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{alloc.activities_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Percent size={14} /> Taxes & Booking Fees</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">â‚¹{alloc.taxes_fees_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pt-2 font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><LifeBuoy size={14} /> Total Estimated Cost</span>
                  <span>â‚¹{trip.total_estimated_cost.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Checklist Widget */}
          <TripChecklist
            tripId={trip.id}
            readOnly={true}
            initialItems={trip.packing_items}
          />

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend/src/pages/UserProfilePage.tsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import api from '../services/api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { 
  User, 
  Settings, 
  MapPin, 
  Wallet, 
  Sparkles, 
  Sliders, 
  Check, 
  Loader, 
  Calendar, 
  Heart,
  TrendingUp,
  Award
} from 'lucide-react';

interface ProfileStats {
  trips_completed: number;
  total_budget_planned: number;
  total_savings: number;
  favorite_trip_type: string;
}

export const UserProfilePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, updateUser } = useAuth();
  const { darkMode, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<ProfileStats>({
    trips_completed: 0,
    total_budget_planned: 0,
    total_savings: 0,
    favorite_trip_type: 'â€”'
  });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Form Fields State
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [photoUrl, setPhotoUrl] = useState('');
  const [defaultBudget, setDefaultBudget] = useState(20000);
  const [preferredTravelStyle, setPreferredTravelStyle] = useState('Nature');
  const [preferredComfortLevel, setPreferredComfortLevel] = useState(50);
  const [defaultStartingCity, setDefaultStartingCity] = useState('Bangalore');
  const [preferredTheme, setPreferredTheme] = useState('light');

  const sampleAvatars = [
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535713875002-d1d0cf377fde?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&h=150&q=80', // Male Dev
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1494790108377-be9c29b29330?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&h=150&q=80', // Female Explorer
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1570295999919-56ceb5ecca61?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&h=150&q=80', // Traveler Guy
    'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1438761681033-6461ffad8d80?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&h=150&q=80'  // Backpacker Girl
  ];

  const fetchProfile = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const response = await api.get('/api/auth/profile');
      const u = response.data.user;
      setStats(response.data.stats);

      // Populate forms
      setName(u.name || '');
      setPhotoUrl(u.photo_url || '');
      setDefaultBudget(u.default_budget || 20000);
      setPreferredTravelStyle(u.preferred_travel_style || 'Nature');
      setPreferredComfortLevel(u.preferred_comfort_level || 50);
      setDefaultStartingCity(u.default_starting_city || 'Bangalore');
      setPreferredTheme(u.preferred_theme || 'light');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load profile details:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchProfile();
  }, []);

  const handleProfileSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      setSaving(true);
      setSuccess(false);
      
      const response = await api.put('/api/auth/profile', {
        name,
        photo_url: photoUrl,
        default_budget: Number(defaultBudget),
        preferred_travel_style: preferredTravelStyle,
        preferred_comfort_level: preferredComfortLevel,
        default_starting_city: defaultStartingCity,
        preferred_theme: preferredTheme
      });

      // Synchronize with AuthContext
      updateUser(response.data);
      
      // Update local theme state immediately if mismatch
      if (preferredTheme === 'dark' && !darkMode) {
        toggleTheme();
      } else if (preferredTheme === 'light' && darkMode) {
        toggleTheme();
      }

      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Failed to update preference settings.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-24 text-slate-400 gap-3">
        <Loader className="animate-spin text-violet-500 h-8 w-8" />
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Synchronizing profile configurations...</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <motion.div 
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: 'easeOut' }}
      className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-8"
    >
      
      {/* Profile Header Title Card */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4.5">
          <div className="shrink-0">
            {photoUrl ? (
              <img 
                src={photoUrl} 
                alt="Profile" 
                className="h-16 w-16 rounded-full object-cover border-2 border-violet-500 shadow-md" 
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="h-16 w-16 rounded-full bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-950 flex items-center justify-center text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400 font-extrabold text-2xl uppercase">
                {user?.name ? user.name[0] : 'T'}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
              <Settings size={20} className="text-violet-500" />
              Settings & Preference Manager
            </h2>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold block uppercase mt-0.5">
              Member Since: {user?.created_at ? new Date(user.created_at).toLocaleDateString() : 'â€”'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Aggregate Stats Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-center space-y-1">
          <Award size={18} className="text-violet-500 mx-auto" />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-450 uppercase block">Completed Trips</span>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white text-base">{stats.trips_completed} Completed</span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-center space-y-1">
          <Wallet size={18} className="text-sky-500 mx-auto" />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-450 uppercase block">Total Planned</span>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white text-base">â‚¹{stats.total_budget_planned.toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-center space-y-1">
          <TrendingUp size={18} className="text-emerald-500 mx-auto" />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-450 uppercase block">Total Savings</span>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-emerald-500 text-base">â‚¹{stats.total_savings.toLocaleString()}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-4.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-center space-y-1">
          <Heart size={18} className="text-rose-500 mx-auto" />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-450 uppercase block">Favorite Style</span>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-850 dark:text-white text-base">{stats.favorite_trip_type}</span>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Main Settings Form */}
      <form onSubmit={handleProfileSave} className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm space-y-6">
        
        {/* Row 1: Profile basic */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 text-xs">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="display-name-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Your Display Name</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              required
              aria-labelledby="display-name-label"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="email-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Email Address (Read-only)</label>
            <input
              type="email"
              value={user?.email || ''}
              disabled
              aria-labelledby="email-label"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-150 dark:border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl bg-slate-100/50 dark:bg-slate-950/40 text-slate-400 select-none cursor-not-allowed"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Row 2: Avatar select */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label id="avatar-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Choose Profile Avatar</label>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 items-center">
            {sampleAvatars.map((url, idx) => (
              <button
                key={idx}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setPhotoUrl(url)}
                aria-label={`Select Avatar Option ${idx + 1}`}
                className={`h-14 w-14 rounded-full overflow-hidden border-2 transition-all shrink-0 ${photoUrl === url ? 'border-violet-600 scale-105 shadow-md shadow-violet-500/10' : 'border-transparent opacity-80 hover:opacity-100'}`}
              >
                <img src={url} alt={`Option ${idx + 1}`} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              </button>
            ))}
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Or enter custom avatar photo URL..."
              value={photoUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setPhotoUrl(e.target.value)}
              aria-labelledby="avatar-label"
              className="flex-1 min-w-[200px] px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Row 3: Defaults preferences */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-5 text-xs">
          
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="starting-city-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Default starting City</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <MapPin size={14} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={defaultStartingCity}
                onChange={(e) => setDefaultStartingCity(e.target.value)}
                required
                aria-labelledby="starting-city-label"
                className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="default-budget-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Default Target Budget (INR)</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Wallet size={14} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" />
              <input
                type="number"
                value={defaultBudget}
                onChange={(e) => setDefaultBudget(Number(e.target.value))}
                required
                min={1000}
                step={1000}
                aria-labelledby="default-budget-label"
                className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="preferred-style-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Preferred Style</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Sparkles size={14} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" />
              <select
                value={preferredTravelStyle}
                onChange={(e) => setPreferredTravelStyle(e.target.value)}
                aria-labelledby="preferred-style-label"
                className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white"
              >
                {['Nature', 'Adventure', 'Family', 'Religious', 'Beach', 'City Exploration', 'Romantic', 'Solo'].map((style) => (
                  <option key={style} value={style}>{style}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Row 4: Theme and Comfort preference */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 text-xs">
          
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="comfort-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Comfort level Preference ({preferredComfortLevel} / 100)</label>
            <div className="flex gap-4 items-center pt-2">
              <Sliders size={16} className="text-violet-500 shrink-0" />
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0}
                max={100}
                value={preferredComfortLevel}
                onChange={(e) => setPreferredComfortLevel(Number(e.target.value))}
                aria-labelledby="comfort-label"
                className="flex-1 h-2 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-violet-600"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label id="preferred-theme-label" className="block text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[10px]">Preferred System Theme</label>
            <select
              value={preferredTheme}
              onChange={(e) => setPreferredTheme(e.target.value)}
              aria-labelledby="preferred-theme-label"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500 dark:text-white font-bold"
            >
              <option value="light">Light Theme Mode</option>
              <option value="dark">Dark Theme Mode</option>
            </select>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Action button */}
        <div className="flex justify-end pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700">
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={saving}
            className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white font-extrabold text-[11px] px-6 py-3 rounded-xl uppercase transition-all shadow-md flex items-center gap-1.5"
          >
            {saving ? <Loader size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : (success ? <Check size={13} className="text-emerald-350" /> : null)}
            {saving ? 'Saving preferences...' : (success ? 'Saved successfully!' : 'Save Preferences')}
          </button>
        </div>

      </form>

    </motion.div>
  );
};



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/services/api.ts
================================================
import axios from 'axios';

const API_BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:8000';

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// Interceptor to inject JWT token into requests
api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token && config.headers) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    return config;
  },
  (error) => {
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

// Interceptor to handle session expirations / unauthenticated requests
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    if (error.response && error.response.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('token');
      localStorage.removeItem('user');
      // Optional: Redirect to login or dispatch custom logout event
      if (!window.location.pathname.startsWith('/login') && 
          !window.location.pathname.startsWith('/register') && 
          window.location.pathname !== '/') {
        window.location.href = '/login';
      }
    }
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default api;



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/utils/formatters.ts
================================================
/**
 * Formats a numeric value into INR currency representation.
 */
export const formatCurrency = (value: number): string => {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'INR',
    maximumFractionDigits: 0
  }).format(value);
};

/**
 * Formats a file size in bytes to a human-readable string (KB/MB).
 */
export const formatBytes = (bytes: number): string => {
  if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
};

/**
 * Formats a standard YYYY-MM-DD date string into a friendly localized display.
 */
export const formatDate = (dateStr: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!dateStr) return 'â€”';
  try {
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateStr;
    return date.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric'
    });
  } catch (e) {
    return dateStr;
  }
};
